feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy seal oiy qey taio
oiil chan cheao oieo dair kaer qaad kaeieo sair
keer diiio kaey oaen qaad kaeieo sair qar oiel
chaed chiiieeain oair qaid saiar qar oiel chiael
cheear oair tair chaaao qein qaiao tear oir seo
oaio oiiy daeay qey taeaal qeil oed kear ter
chiair tael sead qaid oiiel char tiial oaaid tar
kiio cheien oieel kiiy daeay ted oer keid chaey
chail oeiy cheear kiio cheien oieel kiiy chaeo taio
oen chaied deay oeer diiil cheil saaaiiean oay
qial oaid kied cheeay seaad oaiid oer keiey kid
sir qar oaiy oaen seiaed tiay oir oeil seaan
keal siay oaen oear taid qiied oied saiy chaar
oieaao deeeo oiel oan siaiy kay qail saio chan
oeo oeeao qaad qair keail daid qiiio oiieo din
oiiy oiid kieo oieo cheal siiy chaaal chaeay teao
chiir taaao seial chiiio oeao oeo oieo oien oeeeo
chaal oier tiy tao chaal oail oiel say

feao dio oeean kaiil teio qiel chaey oail chey kay
qail saeil chel ken chiar kaeil deien chaar oeeeo
tiay oir oeeed oial siel oan oaen qeial chiain
sear tiaid qay qeaad oaiid oer qeaid oair cheey
chian chao chaair oaeo taio oen oiio cheao deay cheeo
sid teeeal dail sen chaaad cheear oair teaad chaen
qeaao chieey kaien oir seo oaio oiiy dier kaead
tey qeial cheeiy oaiio sid kaeay qar seial cheeiy
oair qeaao chieey ter oao seial cheeiy oead kiael
chien deao oieo dair kieio qair seay sear kiaiy
chead sean oiel chael cheear kao chieo cheey chay
qeaaey sear qey taaal oaaaay kir saaail chier
sein saiao tear siiir dieo chaid qiaiid oan cheaay
saiil qied qeo chieo oiay oied siil chied cheio tey
qial chair qein chiiil chiel oiael say kaaeey chaar
chiiin oey qaid oeel daio oeeo sir qiaiy kaid
diien oir saao chiiiio seaar sein oiaiy

feao dio oeean qeier qieo oaeiao sin oaer teao
oieo daid kiy qiir chaaao qeiy chaeay qaid oeiy
chael seeid tieen oeeen qar seier qial chel qaaiy
seir tiaid ker tao chaaio qair qar oiel chiael
cheear oaen oieio qair chiair kaeir kir seo oaio
oiiy daeay qey qaid oeel daio oeeo sir qiead
oail oeey deey oiaiy chaar oeeeo tiay seao oaiil
kiiey oear qiey oail saio chan chio seao saay chaen
sear tiair tead cheaed qin chieo oaio diieo chey
qiil cheieer siiid oair oaen oaid diien oaear
kied chieaal kiao tar ter chaieed oir qein
chaeaaaad qeil oer qaiiy oaen chail sieo chiiied
oeen cheeel oied qiel oan oeer chaio seao oeil sin
chieo sir tiean siir deeel oied qiel oan sein
chaio oiil chan seao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy seal oiy tiair
taio oiil chan seio oaeio kaen kaer teao oieo
daid kiy qiir chaaao qeil sin sar siiid dael chail
chan saiay tieeo oied tan cher chaieeo chiiio chen
saaao dieo oieo saeo taaid chaio oen chiy tead
sial saaain oar seeeo tiay siiir oeeeed daead
sir qiaiy teao cheil saaaiiean sar siead chiiied
oeen cheer oaid saiar tair seaaed daead oaaaao
chiaid tein taier qio seeid taal dieey ter chiiio
kaey seeel oieal chead qair oeiey tiy kir oeeeo
chiiaad tan saaal chiier tead siay oir oaaao qeiy
chaied oaaao oaaaiiean seaaey sar sied keid oaiy
oaen oieio qair chaeay tied qein ter seay oay
teao oieo dair qaiil qaay cheiey tead siay oir
chiel sin chiean

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy oaieo sir kail
chey kaer teao oieo cher taar dio seao saay deer
chaid diien oaaaay kiel oieo daio dian keid daid
teeay qar chio seao saay oiiir oeeeed daead sir
qiaiy teao cheil saaaiiean sar seeeo tiay chiy
kaiiay oir oaaiy oiiir oeeeed daead sir qiaiy
kay qar sieaad cher kaen chair seaaed daead oaaaao
chiaid tein kir oeao oael siil chead qair oir
oeeeo chiiaad qaen keeer oaio cheao oier teeel
oaair qar cheeo seil oer kieel kil qaaaiiean oiel
chaed chiiieeain cheer oiaiy tiao qaeiy chair qeier
keo oiel oiiio chiiieeain oir saeid dear seeid
qiir daaid tian keeed oiay oeao oed kaio dead
sen

feao dio oeean tieao oer tiar qaio cheao oier
taao chaed oaid say taar oiiid dael chaiy cheer
dieo sid tail sen cher chieeo oied tan cher saar
chair qeied qiien tieeiy chiiad taar oieo chaid
kier cher chiiio kaey seier qiad oeil oed tey
qeeaeo qiier sar siiid dael chail sen saar deaey
kir chiel sin chiean oear saaao chaaio sied kaaaay
kir sier oeao oael siil chead qair oir oaaal oial
cheeeaaien chiaid qin sial chel qaail say kaiiay
oeiey tair qeeid qiir chaaeey oaen sieo cheel
oiair

feao dio oeean tieao oer tiar qaio cheao oied tey
qaid chaed oaid say taar oiiid dael chaiy cheer
dieo sid tail sen cher chaieeo chiiio dail sen saar
diar kaead qeiar qar siead chiiied oeen oaiiad
oiaid chiiiio seied qaen taio cheeer tead cheaer
ter sail oeid oaaaiiean sar oiiieen cthaeiel oen
cheaeo kier chair sieo cheel cheeay qiiin ter cheed
teao oeao chaio oen seain oiiieen cphair keay deao
oieo dair qar cheian oiir diiad taar oaid chiiad
qiel oan

feao dio oeean kaiiy qaad tair tieo sid tio oain
chieo daio chear kaeiy qeal oiy taio sieaad oay
taar oeeeo tiay oaiiad oiaid chiiiio seied qaen
ter sail oeid oaaaiiean oaen qar seiail sin sear
kear oeeen kieio qair cher chiiio kaey chieo seaal
qaaiy chaeay qaid oeiy cher seal oen sier oeao
oael siil chead qair seain sar sied qaio oaiy
cheer oiaiiiir tieeiy cher oao chieo cheey chiir dier
qiiir oieo oeaiid chiy kiio cheeed kaey seeid qiir
daaid tian teer oieo sid qeao chied chin oir oeao
saal cheier

feao dio oeean kaiil teio qiel chaey oaiy saio
cheao oier keieeeer qiiil chieo oeeao oed kear
qiir diey saar chaaed keer der chaaad cheil oeay
oiir chaio chad keal chin oaiio said ken cheer oar
oeo oiel seaar oiir oeir qey kaeir kir oead taed
chiey saar cheieeeer qeaaey sar seeay chiir dier
taiy cheao chaieed dieen qaao oiiid qiiay sar
seeeo tiay seao oaiil kiiey chaaan kir oaeiel oen
cheaeo kier seeiio oen oeiey tiy kir chiel sin
chiean oieo cheo chaar oeeiiy sein chaio chad tieo
oaid oan seao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy sey taio oiil
oiiey qaad keid daid qeaao siil chied oiy qeal
oiy qiir chaeil daaey sio siieo kier kir saio
cheao oier keiey kid sir qar oaiy oaen seiaed
tiay oir cheal siil chiel cheay sein saial kian qar
sieaao deeeo oiey oao seial chiiin oaad keel oio
cheeead saio chan seaao chieey taeay ted oer kir
oeiey qeio kiaey oao chieo cheey chay qeaaey sear
qeio kaen qaad keid daid keo sir qar siiid oair
chied oeean sey taio cheio teel dail sen cher cheeao
deeeaaien chian oiel oiael say kir oeil sier kaen
seeel deal cheial oiy tiiaay siir cheaiio oaaao
cheay sein oieed oaair kied chaey seaao seao oaaao
chael saar kir saein oaen cheaan kaid diien chian
qeo oiain seao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy sey kaio chieaal
kiao tar teed kiil oaaaiiean oail chey keer diiio
kaey oeay oail saeo qaay diar kiier qeao oaieel
qaeo ker kir oaaeen chiao qein tier tiy kaaiao
keear oir oiieo chiiieeain chiy keao saal cheied
sean dead seao oaaao chan sear taieed dey teio
kiiel oiy taaad cheear oeiey qeio kiaey siey
diieio taair saiao tear chiy kaail chier sar siiid
oair chead siay siian keeeo chaal dar ter chaieed
oir qein cheey chian saeay sieo sid qead tain
seeel chael sead tain siaiy tiao qaeiy oaeiey
sear kieo daio chear taeay qey ter chiiio kaey
siaiy kied siean cheao saal dear seeel chael siil
chead qair

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy chiair taio oiil
chao sean oaio cheao oier kieel kiiy oeir kieo
daio chead qay tiy keao cheed qiil deeeaaien seal
oiy qiir chaaao qeiy oieo daio chear ter chaied
oaaaid siin oeay dair saad qaid tein tied oeean
sey ter chaaad cheil oeal chin oiiy oaaal oiead
chiiied oeen oair kaean tiieio taair chieo dio
saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy oaieo sir kaer
teao oieo dair keeo oeid dead chey keer diiio
kaey cheeo oeieeer oieo daio chear ter said oaio
dael sair oieer oier chil tey tio saio deeay oir
oiel oiael sin seiaed qeao oaaao dear saiiy cheal
sied qaio dael oaeay qar chieo saeil chan chaeay
kaeo teiay said saiar qar siiid dael chail sen
chian oair kaean taied oaaao deaad chey taaan kiid
kieo oieo chan chaeay kaeo teiay chair chieo chaid
qaaiy cher oear chaio oiil oiiey qieo seil chaal
oied tear

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy oaieo sir kay
qieo oiid qaal deeeaaien seal oiy kear oieo daio
chead qay taar chied chieo oial oier oay tio saio
deeay seain chair chaio oieo daeaaar cheao oieo
daid kiy ter chaaad dear siaiy tiao qaeiy chied
oeean sey taied oaaao deaad chan chieeiy oiio chial
sir qar seeeo tiay chiy kaiio seaay teio keid
diieo sair qar chieo oaaed tin saaao chaen teeo
seil oer kiiy oaiiay oir oaaal oial cheeeaaien
sein kao cheao chiiey sieo seio tael sieo cheeeo
oaar keiey tied oeean saiao tear chaar oieo sid
teao oiel oan sein said oeiy oiiid qiial cheao
chieed oiy qaid oaio dael sieo oieer siaiy tiao
qaeiy chied chiiieeain oiier keeel seeeey qar oaiy
chiy qaio cheao oier kieel kiiy der cheeao
deeeaaien chaeay qey ter cheed teao cheao sin chiean
chieo dio saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy sey teal cheial
oiy qieao oaaao dead chao oiao kiy qeal oiy qiir
dead seao cheil chear oaear qeo oieo oaaiy oaien
sier saao oeao dar kieo daio chead qay kir sier
seaad oaiid oer tiey chier qeain chair sieo seil
chaal oied tear seeen qiail tiao qaeiy chiy keaid
deeeo chieer seaaey sar chio siiil teeead chaan
oeeer daieo deeeo chied oeo saeo qay kair kaen
tio saio deeay oir oiel oiael sin sear keein
tieeiy chieo dio saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey taio seial
dael saiid say kiid qaal deeeaaien seal oiy qiir
chaaan taiy chied seial dael saiid say kir oeaiio
oaaao cheal chin oiiy oiel oieo chiiieeain chiar
tian tied oeeaey qar oiel chaed chiiieeain oiiy
oair kaean teed qiad chead qio char ter oao seial
chiiio dial chan oear sel ker kaaiao keear cheaeo
kier oay keo siial cheio tiy kir oaeil deeeo oeiy
sey qio siieo kier keiey tead siay oir oaeiey
saay oieio qair sey kaeir kir chiey sel kaen qaad
teel chiiieeain oeiey tied chiiieeain oair qeied
tar taaal oaaaay kiiy cheeel oied qiey chaio oiil
cheal chin oir oeao oed kaio dead sen

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seir kieo daio
chead qay tiel say tiy qeo oieo oaaiy oear chieo
dio saeo taaid say qaio cheao oied tey kear qiir
oiel chaey seiaid tar kaio chad kao seeiiy saad
qaio daio oiey cher oiaeo oaey oaen qar oaiy
oeiey tied chiiieeain oiel oiaid cheied char kieey
chaaal chaid kied oied qaaiy kiiy oiel oiaey
chaeaaaar kaiiay oir oaaao qeiy chaeay kaeo teiay
oay qieo cheel cheeay qiiin ter chaied oeil oian
teaay cheeo chiaid tein qeao oaiil kiiel oen oir
sir qial said tein kiiy oid sir kal tiaer ter
said oaio dael sair cheey cheal cheial oiy taeay
qaiao tear chaar dail oeiy chieo chiiieeain chair
kaiil tail sen chaid kiiiel chey qeao oeo oiiied
seiey

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey kiaed chiiey
qeir kieo daio chear qeal oiy qiir chaeiey cheao
oieo dair kaid diien saiid chier oeein taio sied
keid deaad chey keo oiel oeao chier cher said oaio
dead saeo taed oail oaain oiir daio cheeer qaial
kian qar chio seao saay cheeel seao chaaio char ter
saaao chaaio sied tair qiaiy kay qar seeel daied
oaaaid siio cheeay qeaaed daead oaaaao chiaid tein
kir chao chiiad keel oier kir oead kieo chiey cheey
chier til qid qier keeel seeeey qar siead chiiied
oeeo chan chead cheaer ter chiiey dian cheeel oaair
qear keeer oaio cheao oier kaiio oaeier kir cheal
siad deeeaaien sier kaead tey qar seeiio oen
chied oeean sey taid teao deay

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy seal oiy kiiid
kiid teer qeaaey oaid diien oiel oieo oail sieo
cher taar oeiaiy siey chieo daio chear tier til qir
qeaao siil chieed chear ter cheed teao cheao sin
oied chiao saio deer qar seeiio oen oair tail
oaen cheeed kan chail oaen sail saio deal chaio oir
qeied ken oied oieid tan oao cheao chiiel chear ter
seay oay qiead chiiio cheo cheeeaaien chieeiy chier
til tey qiiil chiiiiy ter said oaio dael sair sey
taio oiil daead oain daio cheio teel dail sen
oaio char taaao siil chear qier qaio dieeo say kir
oaaal oial cheeeaaien cheey chaied oaaao oaaaiiean
seaaey oaid diien oiiy oiel oieo oail sieo cher
kir saao oaieel kaiiad chier chian oead tiao oied
cheio keear chied oeean siey daio cheio teel dail
sen oeeo sean deal siil cheieer seeel dael saad
kean chian oaaiar taio chiao keel dain seeid qiir
oaein oaen cheaan tair chaaen qail saio deal chaio
chear

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy sey taio oiil chan
sieo oiid taal chiiieeain chiel say taar oeeeo
tiay oaear keao saal oen oao cheao diair qeied
tar tiieio taair cher chaaad cheil oeal oen oir
oaeiey siaiy tiao qaeiy seaiieo oaieean cheao
oieo dair qear keeer chaaiao keear saiid daan
cheer dieao oer qaao chaed oier kied oieir taaio
oiieo oeeaey qeeaer tao chiiad qiin chian oir chiey
chian seain chair oiiio diieo oead tiiiiy ter
oaeir keao oael siil chead qair oir oeil sier
qiiir oeeaeo qiier siiid dael chail sen siir oeo
oaio oiiy cheeer oaio cheao oier qiel chaid kay qar
saaio oaaaiiean oaid diien chil qir qieo saeo
taal daiy cheal oiy tieeo chaal din oaen siaiy
tiao qaeiy saiiy sier chiy qeiar kaid diien chiy
kaail chier sar chieo oaaed tin oiiy oaail chier
cheeo saio oaiy siio chiao saio deer qar seeiin
oay qaiil tieo oiair chaeay qead chaain saay saay
chieed deaad sir qiaiy kaid deaar oaaal oean chir
oeil sier kaen chad qiio seeid kan oeer daieeo
chiiio daiy oaen oied chaey cheio taid qeeaey keo
seio kiiio cheeay kir seo oaio oiiy oaid oeel
chiiied oeeo sir qiaiy kaid diien oaio chao cheeead
oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy kaieo
sir kay qieo oiid taal oaiir siir chaaao qeiy sey
taio oiil oiiey taaan kir chiy tao chaaio qaid
tein kir oaeil daaey chian cheaeo kier oay tiaer
keal oiil dear chiy kiel chaaao seao chaaio char kao
chieo cheel oair oear oaio chieel teeay oir sier
oeao saal oaaiy keao oael siil chead qair seain
sieo oeeao teial chin oir oeeed chier sead chaain
cheaad cheen siiio oaio oiied tair qaay sein sey
taio oiil chao sean cheil chiiar qeo oaio oiiy oiir
cheaid din oaen seeaer keao oaeo qiil chean

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy sey kael siil
chead qaiy chiar keao chiael cheil cheaad chey kail
chey keer diiio kaey saay cheiad din chiar qeao
chieo oaid oiiey ter chaaad cheil oeay oeay oieo
oaeil oial oieed oiy taio cheeer taid kay qar
seeeo tiay siiir dieo said ken saiir chaer qar
siead chiiied oeeo chan oear sein kiaed chan sar
seeeo tiay oeeed dieaain saio cheao oier keiey
tead siay oir chiel sin oieio qair sey kaeir kir
oaaiy chaen seeeey qeied kiy qio sail sieo
oaaaiiean sar sial oaid tail sen chian saao cheed
tiay oaeiad kaio daair oieey sey tiaeao chiael
cheiy sieal chead qaiy saeid dear sar cheian oiir
chieen

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy seir keeeo qail
sid kiy ter seir kiio seao chiiel chian teeeaan
ckhiir chaaao qeiy saar chir oeil oied qaen qaar
chaiio seaay qar sial chey kiir oaial kian tad
tael seiaid kiy kieed oiin saar dao chied qeil
oied qaen qiied qein taeay kaeo teiay seeeo tiay
cheer oar siead chiiied oeeo chan chaio cheeed tair
qaid saiar qar oay qieo cheel chier chaio chial oeel
daid kier chiiin taeay ted oer kir oeaiio oaaao
cheay cheaad chaen kal tar qio siieo kied qaen taio
cheeer keal sial saaid qean dao oaeo taio oeo
oier kir siiir daaao kiel oer qier chiain qaad
kaeo oeo sid qier tied oeean cher chaeay kaeo
teiay chaaan kir oiid kied qaen ter seay oay
qeeil chiied kaen chio saio deeal sair teaay oied
chaey oiel chaid teao cheil seiey ter said oaio
daey cheaar chiaed cheal chin oeiey teaeo kier sear
tiair taaal chair qar saaio oaaaiiean sein cheal
cheial oiy ter chael oear keal siay oaen oear taid
qiied oier siir deeen chied chaal oieo chaao oer
keiey tied oeean cher oiio seeeeo oaiir seio keao
din

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey taio oiil chan
oaiil teiad din chiar qaio cheao oied tey kail chey
keer diiio kaey chieo sir teeo sieo cher qaio cheao
oier tead siay oaead qir qeaao siil chieed chear
ter sey qiio siad chied cheio keid diieo sair tair
seeaeo qiier siaiy qad char teear kiaiy siio chien
oieo oead taed chiiieeain oaiiin chir chiey oieer
oier oaear qeo oieo oaaiy oieey oiio oaead kian
tail oeear chiail keel daio chin oair qaair tail
sieo chiiied oeen saiiy chiy taao chaal oaid oier
qeo oieo oaaiy cher sail oeel deal saiy oair
kieiy qar oaiy oair qeiaed tiay oir chiy tao
oiael oiao qear ter chiiil cheil saaaiiean oaen
tio saio deeay oeiey tiy kir seao oiaey chian
oair kay qeiael ken seaad oaiid oer kiiy oaen
chiao qaaao daeaad seo oer tair qieaad cher kir
oiieo chiiieeain oaen seiaey teaay cheio taid qien
saao oaeo kier cheey chian siel chaair chaio cheeer
keal chaio oaaaid siin chio siiil teeead chaan siir
chaaao qeiy chian saiid dian kir oeaiio chaeaey
sein chead cheaer kieey sein teed qead teaao saaiy
qaao chied keao oer kir oaaeen oaen cheaad chaen
qear qey taied oaaao oaaaiiean seaaey sar saaio
oaaaiiean sein cheal cheial oiy kiio chiao tear
saay sein chaaao oay qieo seio taal oien oeaid
diay oir chiey chian saiid dian tiy kir oeaid
deeead chaan oieed oein qel qin oeaio chiiio oaiid
say tiar tiain teeean daeay qier taier kieer
oaao oaeo qiaid oer keeeo chaal oier tiy tao
chieeid oaaaid siin cheaar chear seaay keal siay
oaen sieo sid teear tiiil diaiy taar deian sein
chiiad taar dio oeaiid deeead chaao say qaiiy oiel
oiael say qaio dael oiiieeain oiier keeel seeeel
kiy qeaay keal siay oaen chieao oer qeio taed
oair sey teed qeeir tiiil diaiy taar deian sein
sead deeeaaien seien qiio cheaao oaaiad kiao kiy
qeaal kian tail oeiy chian seaio char kaeid tan
chiiil diaiy taar deian sein sead deeeaaien chiaid
taain kaeiad qeiy cheaal qeaiil qear qeaal kian
tail oeiy chian seaio char kaeid tan oiio chad kiao
taio oid kiy qiir cheao cheeay oaen sieo oeeao
teial chin sier chil qir qeeaeo qiier sar oay qial
chel qaail sin saad qaid tein kir oeil oied tan
oieer oier oir chied oair oear seien keil sal
kiiel oiy kaio chieel teeay oir sier oeao saal
daaiy taio chial oeel daid kier

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy sey qiieo chiiel
oair oiel oiael say tiel say tiar qaio cheao oier
taar chaiy chiy qeo oieo oaaiy cher seay sead kaen
teaay oied chaey oaean taied chaeael chiao qaeao
say kiel oiael say kir chiey sead chier sail saead
dio sir qar sieaao oeieiy sey qio chiao qieo
oiiey taaar oaaaiieal qaid teio qir qeaaan deaad
chaen kay teeo oaid qieo sir teeo oaaaao seaiid
oeieil oan chaaar deal cheied daead sir kear qiio
seied qiien kiiid keier sar saaio oaaaiiean oiir
daio oead qair oir oaaan tiy kaiiay oair kaean
teed qiad chead qio char ter chaaad dear oaen qiead
chiiio chiiieeain saao oael char tiiar kiiy oeil
oied qaeo teeay chaaid qiel oan sar seeen kay
keeo oeid dear saeo taed oeeao oier kair keay
oiead daeaad sen siear kay tieo oeid oaid sair
qaay cheeeay deaay sieo oead taed chiiieeain
oaaiar teey oeal siay oiid taal oieo cheay sieo
sid teao cheead chey kear qiey deal cheied daead
sir qial chair tail oeiy siey deal cheied daead
sir keeao siad daaiy taied deay sar cheeeo chain
diaid qin cheeo seil oer kiiy seiar qar oiiil ched
kaain sein oaien siian qeao oaieel tear sear
qeio kaen qaad keid daid keo saaar qar saain
dian oiel oiael say kiiy siel chaair chiieir kaeal
qiil cheao oan seeiiy sein chaeaaaar qeio taed din
chaar cheeo sir qein oiio saad qeiy chier qiian
kiid taed oied tao oiiir kir oiel oiaey chian
oiel oeao chiiieeain oiiy oaen seeaer kaaial keel
dain seeel dael sead taio chin oir chiey oieed
oein taio oiil chan oeo oeel deal sail chey qiaiy
kied chaey sar siaiy kied chaey oay qial chel qaaiy
oeeen kal kiy teal chaio dead chieaal kiiey ter
sail oead kien oiaiy kay qial chel qaail chain
chaaao kiel oer kir oaaal oial cheeeaaien oeeer
dao chaal oail oiel say tied oeean cheey cheal
cheiay sial chair tail oeiy chael chead kiao qeio
oan sein sier kead qaaail oaiin qeao oeo oiiied
seiey

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey qiieo oao
sied qaio oair oiel oiael say tiel say taar chaiy
chair qeied kiy kao cheao chiiey chair seao oaaao
chao seao oeo oiao tein qaio cheao oier qaiiy chead
siay chaao chaal oaid oiao kaiin qar oaer qaad
qaid tiay oir oeao oael siil chead qair oair
kieiy qar oaiy oair qeiaed tiay oir oeao chael
cheeed taain qiel chaair chaio oead qaid sir tieo
oaaed tial sid tian qaiiy saao seier qiel chaey
chaio oir qiead oeiey qeaal keal chin oir cheal
sied qain cheeer oeao oied qeil chaain kair kaen
tio saio deeay oiiy cheer diead dear chaaid tian
qeo oieo oaail cheeay oir oiien chiir deal cheial
oiy kiio chiao tear saay oiir chiaed dael cheear
qiied qein qiir chaaao qeiy saar oaiid dian qeao
oed kear qead deeeaaien cheear taaal oaaaal tey
qar oied chieo oial oier oeay dieo said tial
oeean tieo sir kaid oaaaiiean

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy sey tiiil cheeeo
chaal oeeiid kan cheao oieo dair kail chey keer
diiio kaey cheeo sieo cheao oiaiiir saio cheao oier
kir chao cheeao chean saar dair kaen keo oiel oeao
chier sead chaain sar oay qial chel qaaiy chieeiy
chiiey dier tiy keeel seeeel kiy ter chiiil cheil
saaaiiean oeay oiaiy kay qar oied chieo oiil chier
oiir oier qier chiain teio keid diieo sair qar
saain oaar diy keil sal kiiey seiaey teeo oaio
oaaaiy sar chio seao saay deer deaad chaen tiean
oeein taaao seao cheil chair qiead chiiied oeen
cheer oaid saiar kaid diien chiain qail chaid
teeead chaan cher chaied oaaaid siin siaiy teeo
seio kao char taio oiil chan chial oeel daid kied
say kir oaiiad oiaid chiiiio seied qaen ter chaaao
oiil chian kiir oaaal chiier kair taaar dieeo
chiaear ter chiiaao sin sear tiair taaal oaaaal
tey qar saaio oaaaiiean oiir diaeiy kiio chiao
tear saay chaaar diieir kaeal qiil chean siir
chaaao qeiy saar oaiid dian qeao oer teeel oaair
kaid oaaaiiean sar seaao chear qaar chaiio seaay
qar oiel chaed chiiieeain oaaaay tiy tao cheal
saiio sio chear ter seay siaiy kied chaey sieo
oeaiid chil qay teeeo oiiio sen cher cheed kil tain
oeay oeeeed daead sir teaay cheeo chiair tail
oeear oeao oaeo qiil cheao oan oied chael cheeean
oiear tio oaeil oiay chaiy daaid tian keao chaieed
oaair qar cheaay chaaar chaeid tey teaay cheeo chiair
qaair qieo oeaiid chaiin keo oaid tail sen cheey
oeeer daaid tian kead taed oiair cher chiiaan
oieed oein keeer oaio cheao oier tead siay siian
keeeo chieo oial oear tieeid oaaan cheein qio sail
sieo oair oir oiieo chiiieeain cheer cheead kaen
qiead chiiio chiiieeain oir oeil chiao oeieeey
chiaid qin sieo oeaiid chiy teey oaeir kead taed
oiair oieer oier oead taair chian chair

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy seal oiy qey kaio
siil oeil chieen chiao seian tiar qaio cheao oied
tey keer oeay chio oaeil oiay oiel oiaid cheied
char qieao oieo oaaaiiean sieo oeaiid chiy taid
qiad cheier sar seeen teaad chaen qeied ken chieel
tear cheiad chier oieey oeal siay oiel chiao sen
chiiil cheaid chaeay qaad keel daio oiey sey tead
cheaer kaio seeid kan saay cheaar oiieo chaed cheeay
kaiiin chir cheael qaen tiiil oeil chaal oied tear
sar sieo oeeao teial chin sier siiio teal char ter
chiiio kaey sein oieed oein kaio char ter cheaal
qaaaio dail sen chieeen der oieey oeal chaio
oaaaid siin oiir diieir teaar chear oael sead
keal chin cheer deeo oiid kaey oay tiaed qay qaiiy
chil tey tial siiil teeay qaiil qaaaio chaain
cheeeo chaio daio cheio kiiel oen oiel cheeao oen
oael seio chaal oiaiid oer kir chiey chaeay kieo
oial cheeeaaien sey kao cheao chiiey siaiy kied
chaey oiir chiiio dian qeil chieo daao oeid diieiy
qiied qein ter chaeaay kaiiay

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy seal oiy qeir
kiio seao oaaao diieiy ter seir keeeo qail sir
keer diiio kaey oiir der oaiid chaiin cheer chaer
tad qaiy oir cheal siad deeeaaien chaeay kaeo
teiay sear qeien kiid taed oied tao oiiir kir
chiey chaeay kaeo teiay said chaal oeiaer qiaiy
kaid oaaaiiean seiaed qeao cheear saiiy saio cheao
oier kir chiey chaeay kaeo teiay oay tio saio
deeal saeo tier tair tair chaaan kir oeao seied
qeiy cheey oao cheao chiiel chear ter oao chieo cheey
cheay oaid chaal oeiaer qiaiy kay qar sail saeil
oeao sin siaiy tiao qaeiy oair tieaay qiear qear
tieeiy cher chaio chial oeel daid kier saar diar
qid kiel say kaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey taid
kal tail sair kaer qaad kaeieo sair keer diiin
taeay qaid oeiy sey taio oiil daead oain diieio
taair cher sey taio chad keeil chey keo oeeao tear
ter oao chieo cheey oaay seiaid qein chier tiy
kaiio sid tial saeo tier qaiiy saao seier kiiy
sir tail sieo chiiied oeen chaaid qiel oan saad
tel qieo chan oieed oein kear saaao chaaio sied
tair qar chair sieo seil chaal oied tear cheaay
cheeo chiair tail oeear oaaial keel daio chin oir
chiy keil sal kiiey chieo seaal qaaiy cheey chian
siel chiao seo oer kiel chiao chao oer taaid qiel
oan sar siiid oair chied oeean chead seaiil teio
qir qar saaio oaaaiiean seiaey teeo seio qair
oiiy oaen chio oeaiid deeead chaao say taaid qiel
oan seao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy seal oiy kiio
cheieo saio cheao oier taar oeeen qaay seeel chiaed
chan oaer tieo oaio dead saaiy kir cheal siad
deeeaaien cheey chier tiain qaad keid daid keo
saaar qar seeen qiaiy tiao qaeiy oeieeeed tial
sir qar cheeo seil oer kieel kaiin taaar deey
saar chaaaad oiaid oed qaeo qay kaaiar ter cheaal
keear oeiel qeio kiaey cheey oiiien dair qail
saio cheao chaen teeed qiiey kaid diien oir seo
oaio oiiy oein tiiiid cheied char taeay qead
deeeaaien cher seay oay keeao chio oeid deaad chey
tair sieo cheel cheeay keao oael siil chead qair
seain sar siiio oaaeed ken oair taaar chaiin
tieeo chiaear ter chiiaao sin sear qel qay kiid
taed oied tao oiiir kir saao oael char teaal tel
ken oeiey qeio kiaey chieeo oied tan cher sail
oeid oaaaiiean seeeey kay keo oiel oeao
chiiieeain oir chao seaar sar cheiad chier saar
chaaed keer dieo dio saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey teal
cheial oil qin cheaay oay qeaao siil chieed chear
qiir diey sey kao cheao chiiey cheaay oied chaey
oiel chaid teao cheil seiey kieed oein kieed oiin
oer qiel oeao deeead chaan chieeen der chaaad dear
oay qiiil chiiiiy kiio chial sir kaen tieo sid
tail sen cher chiiin qey tieo deeeo dao oaeo kiy
kiao qiad chaaio chein kir oaaal oial cheeeaaien
sein kao cheao chiiey oaid deaad chey qiaiy kaid
diien sier chiain tiaed qay qaeo taed oeeao oier
kir oeaid diay saiid dian tian kaio chiiio cheo
seio qay qaeo taed oeeao oier kair taaar daaal
oaaaay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey qio
seeid teaad chey kaer taaao qaeo tied kean siir
oeo oaio oiiy oaar chair kal kiy taied chaaio oiy
ter cheeao deeeaaien saar chaeiay kir oiiy cheer
deiey oiiied oeeo oaaed tin chaaid tian teal
seial daair saar cheiey qeio kiaey saiao tear chiy
kaail chied chear ter oao chieo cheey chiir chaieo sir
qear qein taied chaaio oiy ter chaaad dear seieir
oay qieo saeo qaal oiel oan sar saaio oaaaiiean
oieiy qeiaey qar sieaad cher teer deeo seio qair
oiiy cheer dail sieo chiiied oeen cheeel oied qiel
oan sar chio seao saay cheiey qaao oaeo tio sir
tieo seaal qiier sar sieo oead keeil chey teiad
oiair oaeo teao chaeiar kir oeao saal oen chaio
chial oeel daid kier cher oao chieo cheey chiir daeal
kaeo teiay sear qey kiio cheao chaaio chear qiir
oeil oied kaio chiiio chaeay tio oaeil oiay chaier
kiio chaio oir taio oaaar

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey
kiaid oiel oao sial saaad qead tain oaer qeial
diaeid qio sail sieo daid qied tey keer dieeo
oier qiiir oail sieo ched tey qeaao chieey ter
chaieeo chiiio daiy cheer chied chaey cheeo sieo ched
qeo oieo chaead kiao kiy ter chaieeo chiiio daiy
siiir oeaao chieey qead deeeaaien cher oao chieo
cheey deaad chaen taaar deal chaio oid kiy tier til
tey qiead diieo chey qar siiid oair oaen oaid
diien oaear keao oiao kiil cheal chin oir oaaal
oial cheeeaaien chaeay qier tiy qaao oeao dad kao
cheao diaid tein qeaad oaiid oer kir saao oed
tiey sein taied chaaio oiy teey cheear kao chaaal
qaiy seao oer tied chiiieeain seio keao din oaaal
oean cheil chiiiiy qaar oaiid dian kaaiar tiieil
taal oien oeaid chaeay keer oail oeid oaaaiiean
oiir dead cheaer ter oao chieo cheey chiir dier til
tey qaid chiao chao oier kir oeeed chier saar died
oeean sey keal sied qaaao diieo cheel chiiieeain
oir chiy keao saal oen chaio cheio teel din oeiey
qaao oaeo tio sir qieaad cher teer oiiio oeel
daid kiel oiiir kir saaao dien oeaaed chiiin
oeied tar qieao diair keer diiil cheil saaaiiean
oay qial oaid kied cheeay seaad oaiid oer keiey
tair qar oiel chaal oaiy cheer chieiy tiean oear
chaio oiil chao siien taio chial oeel daid kier
siir deeel oied qiey chian oeao saal oen

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy teeao
oeo chael cheeed teeay chiar qiiio teal chad qay
taar oeeen taaar cheeeo qail sid kiy ter cheaal
keear cheer diean oeein tead oeeao qail saaiy kir
seo oaio oiiy oaar chair kal kiy tead oaeo oeo
sair qar siiid oair chied oeean chiaid qay qeo
chaid taio cheeeo saal deeeaaien cher oao chieo cheey
chay qieo saeo qaal oiey chaeay kiio chiao qeo oer
kir siel chaey chiael tin seio taaid sair oeiey
tair qieo sieo diao seian kir oeil say kaen
seiaey kiiil daid tin chaar oaaio oaaaiiean sein
chead oaaed tin chaaid tian taar chiel chiael cheear
oaen oieio qair sey tiiel dear oir seo oaio oiil
dian cheeel oaair qear qein tead oaeo oeo sair
qar siiid oair oaen oiiil deear qead chaain oal
kiy kao sied qieo diieo cheel chiiieeain oir oiieo
chiiieeain oaen oieiy qeiaey kiel chaal oail oiey
chian saiid dian teaey qeain chair sieo cheel cheeay
keao oael siil chead qair oir oaaal oial oieer
siiid daey oiiil deear qead chaain oay qaiil tieo
dail sen cher chieeey seaaan oaid chiiad qen siied
qein taaad cheieeeeiel chaal daio chaaaad oier

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy seal oiy qey qio
cheaal oiy qaid oeiy oiio cheieo chey keer diiin
qio seeid taal chear ter oeeo sead kieo oaad kaio
chiiin oar sial oaaed teien seio taal oaiir cheey
chiael oiiay chair sieo cheel cheeay qiiin kaien oir
oaeo siiid daair saiao tear oaaed keer oaaal
chead keay sein chaio oiil daear

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy oao seial chiiio
oaaio chiiin oeio oaein qeir qel qay qaao oiain
oail chey qar saay saair qieo chaid kier cheal chead
tieo deeeo oeiy siir chaaan tiy qeao chieo oaid
oiiey ter sio saaio dain cheeo oiid kaey saay
chaid chio sir tio saio oeier tiy kaail chier saay
chiao qaio oaaaiiean seaaan oar cheeeo oein kieed
oein qead chaain oay qied keid oaiy chao seaar
sear ter cheeao deeeaaien chier qaeid oaaiy kiiy
saao siead tio saio oeied qaen kael seao chaaal
oial cheeeaaien oear chieeiy cheey oieer cheaid
deeeo deeeaaien chieeen der sio saiil qal kean
chian sier oiid kied qaen ter cheey chiiel dear
seil chieo oeeel dael oiiieeain sier oeaid deeeo
chieed siien keeen kaen qaay cheaao tail seo oer
qeo oieo chaear teey oiaed chan oeo oeear chao oied
qeil oer kaeaer siian kead taed chiey oiaen sey
taio oiil daead oain oio cheaal oiy taeay qaid
oeiy

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy seal oiy qey
kiiio oeiaer kaer qaad kaeieo sair keer diiin
teed qeiaio chear kieey oiio oaear ker keaid
chaeay qial said tein kir cheal siad deeeaaien
chier kir cheeel oied kaain sear taeay kaeo teiay
sear tiaid qay kied oieir keaid oeeao say kir
oeaiid dar tier qier oaead keean qaid saiad kiy
kaio chieaal qaid saeo tier kaiio cheel dail sen
chier keiey qeio kiaey sead chaain oay qeeal oair
cheey oer tel qail saaar teio kiiey sar siiid
daair chier keiey qeio kiaey cheey oer qiel chaieed
oaaaid siio oiy qiied keal chin oeil chiao sain
dier qier chead siil oaaaid siio char ter chiiin
taeay kaeo teiay seeel chiio chaio oid tio saio
oeied qen cheed qeiaio chear qiio chad kiier kiiy
siiir oied keid daear oaiiin oier cheeeo oeeao
chaid qeeeey qar chial oaeo chio oeid oaaaiiean
sein oieed oein teey chiao cheeeaaien chaaad chier
cheaay oay teeeo chaeiar kaaal oial cheeeaaien
chaeay kaeo teiay siaiy kear qey kao chaio oaaaid
siio sean chaaiao keear chiar kiid tiaeao oier kir
chied oeean sey tieeo chaal oaiid oier taar oeeiiy
sein chaio chad tieo oaid oan

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey taio saad
keel oaaaiiean oaer teao oieo dair qeeeed daead
sir kail chey keer diiio kaey siaiy kied chaey
sial oaaed teien siiin ter sey tael sead qaid
sieo diiiy dao chaaio qair qiaiy teeo seio taead
sir qar oiel chiael cheear oaen oieio qair sey
tiiel dear sier chiy qeiar qar chio seao saay
cheiey tiy tead siil oaaaid siio chad tair qiead
oaiy siel chaey chail oaen oied chaal deeead chaao
oer qaeay oeer diiin qey kaio chieel teeay chair
taaar oail saio chan sar chio seao saay chaen sear
qey tead cheaed qin chieo oaio diieo chey qar siiid
oair chied oeean sey qiio chieaal kiao tad tair
qar sieaad cher kaen siiio oeel daid kiel oiiir
kir oiel chiiad qiel oan sein oeal chaio oaaaid
siin oiiil daid taiy chaar dieo oaio chaey sein
oaio char kaien oir saao chiiiio seaar cheiey

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy sey kieo oeaao
oiy qeal oiy qiir chaaan qiiir deao chaiio chey qar
oiel chaal oail oiey saar oaeay sieaao oeieiy
saar died oeean siey deal oead kiiied oeen oir
chao cheal seeid kan oaen qiail qaar kair taaen
teeo saean cher sail oeid oaaaiiean sieo chaid
kier sio seeid taal chear teal cheied daead sir
qar oeo chieer chier qiian qaao oiain sar chial
oaeo saiil qal kean chier qier chao seaar cheao
cheil cheaad chey qar seeiin sear qead chaain seier
qied keid oaiy oir saeid oiel oan sear qaial
kian qeeid qiir chiid kieo oieo cheal siiy oaio
dael sair oeeer cheeeo chaed oied kied oiy qein
tiaead qay kir chian qeil chaid teao oeaiieo oaeio
kaen tio seao chaal chiy tieo dio saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy oiaed chiiey kaio
seao chied cheeay kail chey keer diiin taio sied
teel oaar qeao chieo oaid oiiey tieeo chaal oail
oiey chier kir oiieo chiiieeain chian keiel kiid
teer kaid oin seeid qiir chiiy sir qieo chaid kier
oael chead tieo deeeo oeiy siear qaaio oaaaiiean
cheao cheil saaaiiean oaen qieo oead kieo oiair
cher sail oeid oaaaiiean oiir dier kaead keean
qaad keid daid keo sir qar chio seao saay cheiey
tead siay oeeel seao chaaio char ter sio seeid
taal chear qaaao chaaio sied qieo diiiiy ter sio
seeid keen cheaar chear chaaao seeiid kian kaen
tieo oaio dead saaiy qeil chiao oieel kil tain
chair oeo chieer cher oiaed chan sear tieo dio
saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey
tiaear qiir chaaan kaead tey qiiio oead kiaio oiy
tiiar kir seo chiael oiiay chiean chaeaay kaaiar
taaao cheied daeaay kair qiiio oead kiaio oiy
kieo oeaao oiy teear kieer chied seeid kan seaaan
oar chaao oaio dael sair chaeaay qeain chaaar daied
cheil oeal chin saiid daan cheer diao qieo chan chaao
sid qeao oieo oaaaiiean sar siead chiiied oeen
chao chiiad keel oier kiel oiaid cheied char ter
siieo oao siad chieed oiy taeaaaad qeil oer keeel
seao chaaio char qied qeaiid deeeo deeeaaien sio
oaiir oir cheeel dad tiien oaen oaid diien chian
kaeier kir chead seao cheil chear chaiy cheaid oial
sen saaal chiier keiey qaao oael char ter sio
saiil qal kean chier qier chao seaar sar cheiad
dail sen chier keao oiiir teael qaen taaad
cheieeeeiel chaal daio chaaaad oier kaio chao cheeead
oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy oaieo sir qeeir
kiaed chiiey tael oaiin tail oeiy sel qay kaaer
qeeaer keaid oial siel oan siead oaiy seain
seeel oaid oeid diieiy qiir cheaiio oaaao cheay
siaiiy kaien oiiy oeaid oial oiey cher chaaad cheiy
chier keao oiiir teael qaen ter oiao qiad
deeeaaien chaeay kaeo teiay cheao cheil saaaiiean
chaaar daio sied qaio chan sar siead chiiied oeen
chian kaaer qar seeen keo oaaed qiil dair qar
cheao oieo dair qar seaao chieel qaaaiiean cheiad
din oeeel seao chaaio char taeaaaad qeil oer kir
chao seaar sar siiio oaaeed tein qaiid dair chaaar
daied cheil oan sar saay chaaar daied cheil oan
chiao keead chear chaao oaio daal dieaal kian
qeaaan oaay oiir chaeo taio oen said oaio deaal
kaio dear oeeeo chiiaad qaen teey chaied cheil cheay
oaid dear cheeel oaair teaan kear ter oiaed chan
sear qaial kian qeao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey qail
oeid chiiied oeeo saao oiain oeer diiin qey qail
oeid chiiied oeen saao oiain sar chio seao saay
cheieeeer tiean chiieir qiead dael chail sen cher
oaiid chieed chad tair qear qey kaeo teial chin
oeiey kaid kiy qail chey tio oeaiid deeead chaan
chier kaead tey qeio sio siier sar cheaad chaen
qear ter oael seao oeo chaid teao cheil cheal siiin
oair tair sail oeel daead sid kiy kiio chial sir
qeiair qar oaiy oaear qeo oieo oaaiy seir teed
tead kaey sar oaer teao oieo daid kiy kao chieo
cheey oiaiy kied chaey siiil chieo oeeao oer kir
cheeeo oeeao chaid kear ter sio seeid keen sar
chial oain diaeay chiao qein qail oeid oaaaiiean
oaen teeo saean oao seial chiiio chiel chaal oiaiid
oan cheaar chear cher cheeao deeeaaien oiaed dael
chian taaid tian kaaal oeeaey taaen qiiio oaio
oiier ter oael seao oeo chaid teao cheil chaaao
saiy oair kieil tein kir saein oair teaan

sher chieeo oied qaen qey qail oeid chiiied oeeo
saao oiain sar siiid daey sear taio cheeer ter
chieeo chaal dail sen chier tied oeean sey taied
oaaaid siin saiil tieo dail sen cheeao deeeaaien
oieo oial cheeeaaien oeeer cheao chaid teao oen cher
chaied oaaaid siin sear tead cheaer kao chieo cheey
oiaiy kied chaey seeaeo qiier cheao oieo dair keo
oeeao tear teaal keid chan siiil chieo oeeao oer
teeeo oeeao chaid kear ter sio oaiir oir oiieo
chiiieeain oaen char tael seiaid kaiin qar cheian
sein oieen qiear kaiy oeiey tied oeean chiair
tiaeid kael seao cheiy sio siil chiel cheal oiy
kear saaao chaaio sied kaaaay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey kiio cheien
oeao saal oen sio seeiid qaid chey qar sail oaaed
tail sen seal oiy ter saar chiel chiao seo chieo
oiaiid oan sial cheeean oiead chiiio chiiieeain
oaen seaaan oeeen qaay seaaed dear chiar tiao
qein keeeo teio kaid qieo oiel oieo oial oiir
kao sieaad chiiied oeeo chey qaay cheaad cheen sein
oao sieaad chiiied oeeo chey qeaaan oar oiel chaed
chiiieeain oaen oied chaey sail oaaeed teeo sir
qar seaao oiil cheal chin oiiy oair taaar oio
seeiid qair oeil saaain cheaid deeeo deeeaaien
chian oaaeey chaar dial oaeo chio oaio daear chao
oean oar seeiiy sieo cheel oiair

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey tiiil oaeid
oaaiy qaaad tial saaaio oaiid say tiel say taar
chaiy chiar qid kiey sey kiiied oeeo oaaed tin
oeao chaed oaiid say kir oeeed oial siey siey
chiiid qeil oiiieo chieed oiy taeay taeaay kir
chead seao cheel dain chaaar deal chead tain cheaar
oeid kaen qar siiid dael chaiy cheer oieo chaid
kier sier qiiir chiel oieo din cheal siad
deeeaaien chaeay kaeo teiay sear qeien kiid taed
oied tao oiiir kir seo oaio oiiy chaio chieel
teeay oeiey tiy kaaan keiey qeio kiaey chaio chad
keeil chel ken oieel qiad cheier chair seeeed daead
sir tair cheeo sieo daeaaar cheao oieo daid kiy
ter oao chieo cheey oeaaey siaiy kied chaey sar
seeen qiaiy tiao qaeiy oaear qeao chiiaad tead
oiy qio sail sieo oaaaiiean sar oaiy saiid dian
tiy qaeid oaaiy kir oaaeen oair teaan kied chaey
sar cheiad din oair qar chial oaio diiil cheil
saaaiiean sear taed oeeao oan chair siead chiiio
oaiid say kaiiay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy oiiid qeil oiiieo
chieed oiy qeal chian qaiiy oaeil oieo seio qieal
chead teed qaar taar chaiy chiy kied seeid kao sieo
oeid deaad chey teaay cheeo oeid dair qar siiid
dael chaiy siiir deeed kael cheeeao deaad chey kear
keeer deal cheao oen cher oao seial cheeil chan
saiao tear chiy tao oeeil saeo qey kieo oial
oaain oay qeaao siil chieed chear kieey chiael cheao
oaeo keail daid qaad kiir ter oao chieo cheey oaay
cheaad cheen siaiy kal kiy qaiao tead oeao oed
qiial oiy kaeio keao oeean cher oaio chieel teeay
oir oiid kieo oieo chan oaieo sir tair oiiio
diieo oead qier kir oeao oael siil chead qair
oaio chao cheeead oaeay cphaaad cheieeeeiel chaal daio
chaaaad oier

sher oael seio oao seao oaaaid siin sein oear
cheed qeiaio chear tead sid teear tiiin kieed oein
qein qio chead kael saail say tead siil cheao oer
kir oaaeen oair taid cheeay qear qey tieeo chaal
oaiid oier kir oaaal oial oieer saad kaeieo sair
qeeaeo qiier siaiy kear taaad cheieeeeiel chaal
daio chaaaad oier

feao dio oeean qaad qir kaer qaad kaeieo sair
keer diiin teed qeiaio chear ter chaied oaaaid
siin chaaar dead cheaer ter oiio chad tiael oaaao
chaey oaen keo said tial oiiel oiy kaio seeid
qeiey kear saaao chaaio sied kaaaay kir oiiy seil
chaal cheo oeel daeaad sir qear qiey dieeo chaal
oaiid oier kir oeiey qeio kiaey chiiin keeeo qail
sid kiy qaiay ker kied saaio dael sail sen cher
oed qeil oied qaen teey saar dao chied qeil oier
taaid tian kir oaaeen chaiy died oair oael seio
chaal oiaiid oer kair kay qeaao siil chieed chear
qaid oeiy seio kaen qaad kaeieo sair qiaiy tiao
qaeiy oaaan kaeied qir qaaio chiiin deeo saean
chaid qiad chaaiay qaiid dian tiar kiel oiiid
tiaiy qeio keao din oir cheal siad deeeaaien siey
dier tiain qaad keid daid keo saaar tair oaean
tael oil teeal chey keaio deaao seian kiid kier
kiiy saiid daan chaaid qiel oan cheaay oay kiel
chaid kaid oeiey kao cheao chiiey sar cheeo sid tiay
oaen siaiy tiao qaeiy oaeied kaen chio seao saay
chaeied kaen oaid diien chiao qein taaao saaio
oein ter cheal cheiay sein chier tiain qiad chaaio
oaiin

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey teed kiil
oaaaiiean oaer teeo oaio deeal chey qaid chaed
oaid say taar oeeen tiao keel oaaaiiean cheeo
oaio deeal chey teeo seio taead sir kay tiaed qay
kaid kaean teed qiad chead qio cheeer ter chiiil
oeil chaal oied tear saay oieil tein kir saao oed
qiiay sein chaeay kaeo teiay oieil tein kir
oaaeeo chan oaeo qiil chiir tier tiy keeel seeeey
qar chio seao saay oeaiil tein keiey tair qar chad
kael chead kiao qein cher seay oay qaid chaid teao
chiel chaaao oiy qieao oieo oaaaiiean sear qel qay
qir qiead oeiey kao cheao chiiey siaiy tiao qaeiy
oiel oiaey chian cheael qaen tier qeaiil tein kir
saein oaen said chiiad qen said oeiy chiao seian
kaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey kiiid kiid
qay kaiiay chiar kiid tiaeao oier tiel say taar
chiel chaed daan chian kiid tiaeao oier kied oieir
kair kaean teed qiad chead qio cheeer ter sail
oaeil oiier sear keal chaio oaaaid siin oieio
qair oear oaiid chieed char ter chiiin tier qaiid
dian kair kay qiiil chiiiiy ter chaaad cheiy chier
kiiy sir tail sieo chiiied oeen chaaid qiel oan
cheio tan sey teed kiil oaaaiiean sar seeen teaad
chaen qeied qein oiio cheao chieeo cheeay qiead dael
saiid say kir oeaiio oaaao cheay sear qaaal chead
taaao saaio cheal chin oir chiy keeeo chaid teien
cheeeaaiel qeeid keen sar sied keid oaiy oaen
sear tiaid taain keeel oil kiao kay teaay oay
keo siiio qay tao chieeid oaaaid siin sar seeen
taaar dead oaeil oial oiel cheal oiy kaio char
kieo oial cheeeaaien cher chiiaan oeeer chiid tiaeao
oier kair qaay oiiieeail tiiad qiin cher oiaed
diaiay siiir dair oaean kiao qiad chaaio chear
kieo oial cheeeaaien sey kaeid kear tiiin taeay
kaeo teiay oay kiiio diieo oead tiiiiy ter oiio
chad kaey oaid diieiy taeay qead chaain cheaay cheeo
chiaid tein qiel chaey sial said teio kaeo chin oir
seao chiiad qen chian oaiiad deeen dao oeao dad
taain kir oiiy siel chaey sein chael chead kiao
qeio oan oear taid kiiiel chey keer daied deay
oay qieo cheel chier oiiid kiid qay kaiiay chiar
kiid tiaeao oier kaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy chaio oiil daead
oaio daey oeeo oeid oail sair kail chey kaiiy
tail oeiy chian chiel say keao saal oen siir chaaan
qiiin ter chiao oeeed oiier sar chieo seaal qaaiy
cher chaio oiil chiiey kiaid dead chead kiiey ter
sail oeid cheen saay oieio qair sey tiiel dear
oir oeeed oial siey chian oir chaar oeeel dael
saiir chaeay kaeo teiay sar saiio sin oeer dian
oied seeid kan oeead tail sen

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey tead oiieo
oeiay oio seeiid qair chiel say taar oeeen tiao
kaeo oeo sair keail daid kied say tiy keao saal
cheied sean oeao sied qen oiio chieaal kiair chir
seaad oeaan oeay oieo saaar qeaao chieey ter
chiiil cheil saaaiiean oay teeo sieo ched qeo oieo
chaear kaiid chieed char taeay qier kir oiieo
chiiieeain chead sial oeir oail oeel daeaar oaeiay
kir oeiey qeio kiaey sey kiio sier taier qaid
oeil chaer oaid chiiar taaid tian kaaan keiey qeio
kiaey siey chiio cheien oir seil oed qaio cheao
chier saaao cheil oael char kear oeein teey chaeay
qaid oeiy chiiil oeao chaed oied qiel oan oiiil
ched keeeo sin siaiy tiao qaeiy oair qeier tieed
deaad sir qar sear keeen qar sear tiao oeeed
oiier saay seied ken saiiy saao seied qaen
tieeen chieey oeal sein oaeid tey tiead dear oiiy
siel chian seaad oaiid oer tiy tiail kiiel oen
oiid tied kiier kaiiin chaaan keiey qeio kiaey
oeeer dao oied tiaiy kieel kiiy der sail oaeil
oiier oeay chieio qair cher chiiin kieed oein teey
oaeo qeio sio chear kaiid chieed char qaid oeiy
cheear teaal teey chay qial chel qaaiy said oeiy
cheey chiaid qin cheaay cheeo chiaid tein taaid qiel
oan cheaay chail oaen chiaed qay kair qaay oeo said
tiay chiey chiair tead oiieo oier qeain siead chaey
sein chaeay kaeo teiay cheeo seil oer kiiy oeeeo
chieo oial oear ter chael oaiin qein chael cheeed
teed qaar tieel qaen qiied kiier taar chiiil daid
taiy oaen siad diail qein kao sied teel dial
oiiey oeer chao chieo cheey oaay chio oead qaal oeil
cheeay saiiy seao oer qeain cheeo seio qair oiiy
oiier keil chiaear kaio char kaien oaaal oiair
cheey chian oaaao kiid ker qeo chaid kiil chael
saaiy chieeen oiir deeel oied qiey chian saiid
dian keeeo oieo oiaiil qay keil sier kaen cheaad
chaen qiiio saio daeaal qeil oan oeer oaid oaio
daal oair sein taaao cheao cheieel tial sen oiaiy
seao oer qeain sar saaio oaaaiiean sein saial
kian qeiaey qaay sein oiaiy saao oael char tiiil
chain diiin taeay kaeo teiay seier qiiil chieo
oeeao oer kiier qaeay oeeao chio oeid deaad chey
qeaaan oar sieo chaid kied chien diao qaio diaeir
teear taaal oaio diieo sir tiaey kay qial chel
qaaiy said sid qaiil taed oaid say kaaiar ter
said oeieel tad taid qead keal cheeay taaar
daieeo chiiio dail sen said oeiy cheal cheieo oeean
qar saaio oaaaiiean siaiy qead kaeo kiy tiaeiy
ter sio oaiir chead siay oiid kieo oaiid say

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey taio
saad teel chiiieeain chiar qaao oiaio oiy qiir
chaaao qeiy chiair kiaed daear saao oiain sar
chaaao kiel oed qein ter sail oeid oaaaiiean
oieio qair sey kaeir kaaan keiey qeio kiaey chaio
oiil oiiey taaan keao oael siil chead qair oir
chead siil oaaaid siio char ter seal oen oaaed
keer oier taiy deal cheao cheier

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey kiaed oiiey
kaer teeo oaio deeal chey kail chey keer diiin qey
qaaaiid miiieeo ckheaiiey miao taael chaid tean
oaao chieo oaail say kir cheal siad deeeaaien siey
dead cheaer ter seal oen sier oeao oael siil chead
qair oir oeao sied tear chaaao kiel oer qeain sar
oiel chaed chiiieeain chair qeied kiy qio sail sieo
oaaaiiean saair tieo oaaeed kied say kaiiin chir
chao cheal chaio oaaaid siin sear kear cheed qeiaio
chear ter chiiaan chier tead siay oir oiieo
chiiieeain oair teiad deeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey tiiil cheeeo
chiiaad qaaiy qeao chieo oaid oiiey qeal oiy qiir
chaeiey sear tieeid oaaan deear taaao cheio keid
oaaaiiean sear qein taio sied qaio chan chiean cher
said oaio dael sair chaeaay qiiir oiead dael chin
seain sar chaao sir qeeel daied oaaaid siio chin
chaiy chaaal deiad din oir seo oaio oiiy daeay
kiio chiao qeo oer qiiio teal char ter chaio chial
oeel daid kier chieo dio saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy chead oeid cheal
chear qeir kiio cheieo chey kail chey keer daied
oaaaid siin oiir dead cheaer kear said oeil chiair
kieo daio chead qay kir oieel qeio sin sar sieaao
oeieiy chian oied oieir kir chiel sin sear teear
qio siil chiel cheal oiy kear oeeen qar seio chaaad
cheear

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy oaio chad taead
oain chieo daio chear qeir teed qeiaio chear qeal
oiy qeaay keal siay cheer chail chey teaay siier
chail oeear oaeiad qid tieaad chan chieo chair keay
dio saio daead sir kaer taaen keaio chiiio oaiid
say tied oeean chiair kiio chaio oid qeeil chaeo
qien chaier qead chaain oay tieo oaaed tiay oeeel
siil cheieed chin saiiy saao seier qiel chaey oaiil
tin oir saao oael char taaad cheil oeay oiir der
chiiin kieed oein kaio char qead deeeaaien cher
sail oeid cheen oiir dier tiain qaad keid daid
keo saaar qar chaao oaio dael sair saar deaeo
kier oay qeeal oair oieey oeal chaio oaaaid siin
chail oeiy cheed qiil cheao saiy oeiey kair kiir
oiieo oao siad chieed oiy tiieir qar oaiy cheer
deaad chaen taid chin oeao saal oaaiy keao oael
siil chead qair seain sar chair seied tar qio
siieo kier kiiy chaiy daaid tian qaiiy siel oeao
deeead chaan oieey oeeer oaao chaed oied kiiel oiy
tiao seian kir saiir dair siiid oair cheer chaid
diien chiy kied chieaay ter chaid qiier oiir chieed
oein kieen qier qaiiy chaiy oaao chaed oied kiiel
oiy tiao seiao qein tieo dio saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qeir
tiaid taain kaaer kied chael oaiin keer oaid oaio
dael sair oeein tiiar kaaiar taied cheil oeal chin
seain sar oaiy chiy keao saal cheied sean cheeeo
chiiaad qaen qeir kieo daio chead qay kir cheal
siil chiel cheay siiid daey oieil tein kir oeeeo
chiiaad qaen keeer oeao sead oen cher chead sir
keay oiead dael chin chair qaay seial dead sen cher
sail oeid cheen saay siead dael saiy oair taaar
daaao seeiir ter oaeo tean chaen saay sein oaeo
taio oen sead deeeaaien oiio seiair qar seeen
teaad chaen kaen qeeir ter chaaad cheil oeal chin
oair tair chieed deaad sir qar chair chaaan kir
cheal siil chiel cheay sein saiay kaien oir oaaan
qaiid dian qiiid oaiid say kaaer qear teear
taied cheil oan sar cheiad din chian kaiio seaay
kaid diien chiain qied qaio oaiy oir oaiiad dio
oaeil oiil chaain sear kear cheal siil cheied chad
qay taar oein chiiil oaeid daaiy tiiaay siir
chaaeen oair kaid diien chiy kaiio oeel deaad sair
qar seeiin oaen keeao siad oaaiy kaaer kaid dear
oeiey tead siay saein cheer oail saio diaial sen
oieen keein kaio chad taid kiy qiir chiid kieo
oien oiier qeao oed kead tair qeao oeo oiiied
seiey

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey taio
oail sen cheed kiil oaaaiiean oeer diiin keeer
oeio taal oaiir oiaey oao cheao chean oiio siad
chear qio siil oaaid taael sal qaaiy kir oaaan
tiy keeel sied teeeao oaar kir cheal siier sear
qiey chaeo qiil deeeaaien cher chier taiy oeio taal
oaiir chiaiy cpheen oaeay cthiao qiad chied char teeao
deeeaaien saar cheieeeer tieed deaad sir teial
chin seo oieo oien oir oaiio seaiiy kiir chieed
oein taeaay qiiin ter chaaad cheil oeay oiir chaeo
qiil chear qel qay keil chied tead saail say kir
oiieo chiiieeain cheer diao keel dair kay tio saio
dain chio oeaiid deeead chaao oaeo kiy ter chaaad
cheil oeay oiir dier qeao oaiil kiiey sar chio
seao saay deer deaad chaen tieo sir kiiil deear
qead deeeaaien cher oael seio oao seao oaaaid
siin oiir chear chaeaaaad qeil oer kir sier chead
siaiil ked kead tied qaaiy kir oaaan qaiid dian
tiy teeeo oial seil chiiiin oaio chieel teeay oir
oiieo chiiieeain oeiey kair qeiaey qar oaiy oaen
chiean oear oaio chieel teeay sier oeao saal oeo
oer keieeeed tial sir kiel chaal oail oiey cher
chiiaan oieed oein qiey chaeo qiil deeeaaien chaeay
qey ter oao chieo cheey oein oieed oein tier tiy
keeeo oieo oaiid oier kaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy siieo chiiaad tear
saad kaeieo sair kail chey kaiiy tail oeiy saar
diel say qiiir dail oaeil deeeo chiieo deeen
daaio seo chiiad qen chaied diaiy taar oeeen tieo
chied diaen qail oiieo oiaid oed tey taaar chiio
chad taaad cheil oeay sear kaiid chieed char tieeo
oaiiy kir oaaan qaiid dian qiel chaaid chien oaad
kaeieo sair qeeaeo qiier sar seeen teaad chaen
qeaaed daead oaaaao chiaid tein kir oeaid deeead
chaan sein taio cheeer tead cheaer ter chiiin kieed
oein teey oao sied qieo daead saio oeied qaen
teear teed qeiaio chear tiieio taair chaeay kiio
chiao keied char ter chaail oaaaid siio seeeen
oieil tein qeain saay oiiil daid tail sen oieed
oein qey taio sieaad oal kaio daey cheeo saaad
taaey cher chiiin keal saiio chieo oiay chead sial
saaain dio saio oeied qaen ter oeeer cheeed oial
siey chied til qir qiiil chiiiiy kaien oir chead
siil oaaaid siio seeeen oaid dear cheael qaen
kaien oir oiieo chiiieeain oaen cheaad chaen qeiaey
qear teear taaal chair qar seeel dael saiir sey
kao cheao diaid tein tied oeean sey teal siead
daeaad sair qeeid qiir daieo deeeo deeeaaien cher
oieed oein teey oeal sied qieo daead saio oeier
keao oiaid cheear oaeiad qeiy cher cheeao deeeaaien
saar chaeiay keiey kair teaay cheeo oeid dair
keeao said chaid taael say teal seao cheier chaio
oeo chaain tieel qaen keal siay seio keeey qiiir
cheo oaaeed kier chier tiy kaiio oeel deaad sair
kail chey keer oeay oay qaid chiael cheil sair oiiy
saeay chail sieo chiiied oeen oaiiin chir oiiy oeao
oiiir teael qaen qier qaeo teeay seiar qeaao
siil chieed chear kieer oier oiiy chao chiiaad tear
oied chaey saair tiaer kaeo taio oen chaio sieaad
oay kiid taal oien oir oaaal oial cheeeaaien
oieed oein qiey oieao oaaao oaaaiiean seeel
diieiy teear qio sial saaad kied say kir siiir
oeiaed tiay oaaal oeeaey qaair qail oiieo chaeay
teeeo chaio oair oair kay qiiio said teio qen
oeao dio oeean taaar diaeid kaey oeo oaaeed kier
seal oen oiid qied keead saeo taaid say qaiiy
oiid qier qaiid dian teer oieo oeeao kead taaid
oiao qiier oail chey qeeiiy seao oeo oiiied seiey
ckhieo chao siien tian chao chied qiey chiaead ker
kiieo deeen oaao oiain sieo cheel oiair chier tian
qaao oiiid qiial oiy qaid oeiy sio seeiid qair
saio dael oiiieeain oaaial keid deed kiy qiey
chiio cheeed taaid say qaiir oeiaeo sen oeal siay
siiir deeo saaad taaey saiao tear oaeiey chair
siead dael saiid say kiel oiaid cheied char keao
dio oeean kied chaey saair keo oeil oiiid tead
oiy qio seeiid qair oaaan qaao chiiiio seied qeil
chieo dio oain chaeo teao chaeiar taier teed qeiaio
chear tead sid kiy ter said oeiy sieao oaaao
deaad chey qeeen teeo sid qieo seaiil tin oiid
tiaeao oier kied chad qay kir cheal seao oaaaid
siin oiir daeay qey keao dio oeean kied chaey
saair qail oiieo chaeay qeeen teeo sid qieo
seaiil teio qeo chien diao qaio diaeir keao dio
oeean kied chaey saair qeiaed tiay saao chiiiio
seied qeil chieo dio oain chaeo teao chaeiar qieo
chao siien tier taiy oied sen seay oay qieo
oaaeed kaeo kiy qail sid kiy qiir chiel chiao seo
chieo chiiieeain siel chaey chair saair keo oaaeed
kier

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy sey taio oiil
daear oiel oieo chiel oied qaeo qay tiar kead
tied qiel oan siiio saead deeeo chaiin chiel say
taar oieaao deeeo oiel oan saad qaio daio seeeen
sied kaid tail seo chaaao cheied chiain said saiar
qar saay chio oead keao oiaid oieo chaear kiio
chiao kieed cheio qieo daear oir chaiy dao chael
cheeean chao cheao diaid tein keeeo oiaid oaean cher
chiiin taeay kiio chiao keied char kiio siad chaeiad
tey qar chio saio dael oaeay keair qeao oeeel qid
tian kir oeieeeeo oeaan sein saiao tear chiy
qeiar qar oiel chaal oail oiey chian cheael qaen
kaien oir chiey siey daied chaaio oiy qead
deeeaaien chaieed oaaaid siio oiy teed tead kian
kieiy qear qeien keaid oaiio oaein kaiid chieed
char kaien oir seo oaio oiiy chieed oein keeer
cheeeo chiiaad qaen tier tian keaid oeeao say kir
chiey cheal cheied daead sir qiaiy teeo seio taead
sir qar saiil taair oieed oein qey kiao qiad
chier siaiy kay qar seaao seao cheil saaaiiean
sein oieed oaair qear qein taed oeeao say kir
oiieo chiiieeain oaen oieil tein kiel oiael say
kaaed keer deed teao cheao sail oeey oaar died
oair seir taio oiil oiiey teeo saeao say keaid
chiel chaay dail oaen cheeo saio deaao cheal oiy
kaeo taio cheier oeer diiin teal siil oeo oed tey
kear teear qail saio daear oir oeaiio oaaao cheal
chin chaiy oaao oiain cheeo chiaid tein keao chaid
qiad chieed char kieed oein teey chaid qead keal
siiio saead deien oir saao chiiiio seaar chiao
qieo daear

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oay qieo
oiid taal chiiieeain chiar keaid oaiid say tiel
say taar oeeen kay qiead deaad chey qar seiaiy
chier tead siay oeao chaid qiad chieed oiy qail
oeel chiiied oeeo chey qar siiid daey sear qio
oaean qio chead kael oen chao oaiid oieo oaaaiiean
sar seeel dael oiiieeain chil qein keeen qiaiy
kear ter sey taaao cheied chiiio oeaao saiil qaay
chir oiieo chiiieeain oeiey kair qeiaey kieil tein
kir oeil chiiiiy tian chaaio oeid chiiey tiiaay
siir dead seao cheil chear oaen oaid diien chiy
taid qied teeeay cher chead oiieo oial seiy oeeer
chiel chiair tiiad qaear dieo sir kaid oaaaiiean
sar saay cheiad oiair chaied oaaaid siin said
saiar qar chio seao saay chaen cheaad chaen kiiil
deear tier qiiir oaiil qaay chir oeao saal cheier
sieo seil chaal oied tear siaiy kay kiiil daid
tin oaen oied chaey seeil chiier taar oeial cheeiy
chiy tiail kiiey saiil taed oiair chaeay qey tiiad
taar oaad qaio daio oiel chaay oein oieo oial
oier oail chey kaer taaao kiel oed qein

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy chiael chiel chaaal
siiil tad kaen qaid chaed oaid say taar oeeen
qeial deal siiil oeeel qid tian kir oeeed oial
siey saar deael qaen ter chaeay kaeo teiay chio
seao saay deer chaid oaaaiiean saair qaiil qaay
cheiey qeio kiaey oao chieo cheey oeied ken oir
oaeil daael oaey chiaeo taio chaain chair chieo
seaal qaaiy cher chaaad cheear chian kead tiair ter
oao chieo cheey chay keeao chio oeid deaad chey
qiaiiiid kael char taeay qey ter chiiaan oeeer
chaeil oail oeeel chaaal ked qein ter said oeiy
chaieeo chiiio dail sen oeeer dair qaair qaiil
taal chiey siear kied chaey sieo saeo keao oaair
kiir

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy chaio sied keid
deaad chey qaiil kiaey oiio siad chaeiar taar
oeeen tial oead kiaey oiio siad chaeiar kir chiey
sein tieeo oaiiy taar oeeen qaid chaid kead keao
oail sid kiy ter chiiil cheil saaaiiean siaiy kaid
diien chiy qiiin qiear qeio chaaad cheear oaaed
keer deey cheear kaiid cheead chey qar oiel chaed
daan cheer deaad chaen qear qein kiio siad chaeiad
qaen kear chaio chial oeel daid kier cher oiaed
daear cheer deaad chaen kear keein qio sail sieo
oaaaiiean seao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy chael cheial chio
sair kail chey keer diiin tael cheial chio sair qar
cheeo sieo ched qeo oieo chaead qin sar seeiin oiir
chieed oein tier kied siad chiel chiien oir seo
oaio oiiy dieeiy chaeay qier kir oiieo cheo
cheeeaaien saial kian teiad cheeay keiey kaid tian
kir oaaal oial cheeeaaien sey tael oil teeay
siiin qiear qeio chaaad cheear oeiey tair qar chio
seao saay deeeo oeeao chaid kear taeay qier

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oail sein
qey taio siieo kiael oiiey teaal kaeo chin chao
oeeeo keo saaaid siio siad chiel chaaan oaiiy tail
oeiy chier tiel say tiar qaao oiaio oiy qiir
chaaan kied cheeed teal seeao say taier qio cheio
tiy taier teal cheeed qeiey qiir deal siil chiel
cheay sear tieeo oaiiy kir oaeiey sear qead chaain
sar oaiy oair kied chaey oiel chaal deeead chaao
say kir seo oaio oiiy dier kair qeier qiiil
chiiiiy ter chiiil cheil saaaiiean siaiy kear kaio
seeid kael oen seaad oaiid oer kir seao chiiad
qeil sin chaaan kir oiiy chaiy chieel kiiy chaeo
qiil chear qel qin cheeo oaid qieo sir qeeel dien
oiaiy teeo seil oer qiiir deeo saean cher oaio
seao oeiy saar oaao oael char qey taio oiil chan
siiio oaio dael sier kir oiieo chiiieeain oaen
oieiy qeiaey qar oeo chieer oiaid oaeao oan sar
oiel chaal oail oiey saar deael qaen qaar oeio
taal oier keaid chaeay qial chel qaaiy sio sead
keal chin chaaio oeid oiiey keer daaad cheear chaiy
chieel kiiy dier tiy kaiio oeel deaad sair qar
seeen taaar chiio cheien oir seo oaio oiiy oiey
daio sied qaio chan siead chiiied oeen chiy keil
chied tead saaiy saiao tear chiy qeiar qar chio
seao chaieey chaeay qaaal chiier kair kay qiiil
chiiiiy ter chiiil cheil saaaiiean oeay oaiil qaay
cheiey tair qar saaio oaaaiiean sein saaal chiier
tiy keil chied tead saaiy chiaeiy ter siey oio
siil chiel cheal oiy taaar daiy cheeed oial siel
oan chiao qiiiy kiel chaed daan oaen cheaad chaen
qar oiir oaiao tear oir oaaal oial cheeeaaien
oieey saaao chiid kael chin cheaeo kier oay tiaer
tier kaear kied oieid kain daaal oaaaay kir
oiieo chiiieeain oaen oiel say kiel oeao
chiiieeain oiiy oaen chieo sid tail sen saaal chead
keal chin oir oeaid diay oaen cheeo seil oer tiy
keeeo chieo oial oear ter said oeil oiiied oeen
oaen sail oaaed taiy seao oer teeel oaair qar
seiaiy chian chied oeean sey qied kead qaaaiy sar
sieaao deeeo oiel oan sein chier tiy kied seeid
keal sean chieel kiiy oio sieaal siiir deey chiiil
chaaeey oaid qaair teeo saio oail chey qiiio oeel
dair qaiil tiao sen sio chaaao taao chaed oail
oeeen

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy oaio siil del tad
qay kied siad chied oiy qiir chaaan kaaed tio oaio
daey chieo oeel oal qir qar cheiad oiair saar oeao
chiiad qeil chieo oiay oir oeaid deeead chaan saar
oaiid dian tead siay cheal siil oaaaid siio sean
chir saiir dieo saiiy chao chaal oail oiel say kir
seao oieel oaiid chieed char taeay tier keeed oial
siel oan oiir deey saar chiid kiiid qeiy siied
qein tiiin keeo oiiil daal oeiair qar siead
chiiied oeen oir oeeed oiay cheer dieed daair chier
keil sal kiiey chaaan teeeaaiel qeeid keen sar
sear tael oil teeay seaad oaiid oer kaaan keeel
seao chaaio char teaal keid daear oied siil chiir
kaio chad keal chin saio cheao oier tao seaar seio
chaaad cheear oir chiy keil sal kiiey siiil chieo
oeeao oer kir oiid qal kean cheey said oeiy saaao
chiid qay kir chiey saar chir oaaal oial cheeeaaien
siey cheeo oiiil daal dieaal kian kied chaey chaaen
qieo oeeao qeiaid kiy tiiil cheil saaaiiean saay
oaid diien chaiy oeao chaal chil ked tey qaaio
oaaaiiean sein chiaeiy tieo chaeay tail sieo
chiiied oeen oair tail oieeo oeo oeean teeead
oeid daeaad oeieel kil kan saad qaio dain saad
tair qeiaid tar teeao oaaan chiaed oiiey kail chey
tieao oer tian keaid oaiid say qaeid daaiy kaeiy
teed kear tieeel oiy taaal oaio diieo sir

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oay qaad
keid daid keo saaar tieo oaear tiel say keeeo
saal oaeo kian kay tieo oeel oeaan kear oieo
daio chead qay kir sier chao chieeid oaaaid siin
oeo oaaed qiil dair qar chair oeo oaaed taan
saaao chiied tey teeead oeil oiaio oiy taied cheil
oan sar siiio saead deeeo chaiin oir cheal siad
oiin chian chair qeied kiy qio sail sieo oaaaiiean
sar oaiy oaead tey teao oieo dair tio saio deeay
oeeeo chiao sen oear seien keil seaad oiy kiaed
diaial sen chier qiel oiio cheear ter chieeid oeil
chied qiel oan saay chieo oaear qaiir dair cheiad
din oaen chiao qieo chan sieo saaar teian sein
oieen tiiad taar oieo chaid kier oieed oiin chaied
chaaeey chiy kaiiad oiead cheao oiaiil tieeo oaio
siil dey qeao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey taio oiil
oair oiel oiael say tiar qeo chiael oaaao deeao
oaaan deal cheied chao char qeal oiy tiaid
qaaaiiean sieo oeaiid chiy teey oaeir taieo deeeo
deeeaaien cheey oao sied qieo daear oaeiad qeiy
siir chaaan qaio cheao oier kir seo oaio oiiy deey
sieao oaaao oaaaiiean cheaar oeir ter oao chieo
cheey oein oael seio chaal oiaiid oer kir oeaid
deeead chaan chieeid cheied seeeeo oaead kian qaid
saiar qar seial cheeiy oair qaid saiar qar seial
cheeiy saio cheao oier keiey qaao oaeo qiaid oer
kir saiid dian tiy keeel seeeey qar oaiy cheer
dieo oaaed tiay sier chiain qeaaey sar oaiy saiid
dian tiy keao chaieed del kiiieeain oiel cheeao
cheier chieo sir kaid oaaaiiean sar oaiy saiid
dian tiy kaeiad qeiy cheal saaio chan oaer kaeo
tian kaaaad oiaid oer kir seo oaio oiiy oead
chier chieo sir teao cheil saaaiiean sar said oiao
qiad chiel chiien saiid dian kiiy chaieo deeeo
deeeaaien cheal saaio daear seao oer tied
chiiieeain oir oaaeen siiir oail saead oiaid oer
tied oeean sey kaeir kir oiiy oeao chaieed dieen
qied saaiy taar oieaao deeeo oiel oan oiir chieed
oein tead cheaer tieeo oieo oaiid said oeid daio
chial sieo chan sar oiel chaed daan oaen oear qey
qio sail oieeo oeo sair qar oaiy saiid dian
kaear qeo oieo oaaiy oear oieo daio chead qay kir
chiar qeaad oaiid oer tiey chier kaeiad qeiy cher
oeeo seao cheel dear oaen oieio qair sey kao cheao
chiiey sar sail saeiy oaen sear qeien kaiiay oir
chead seao cheil chear saiid dian qiiir cheo oiel
cheaao chao oier qeao oer tied chiiieeain oir
oeaiio oaaao cheal chin oaen cheaad chaen tiao qaeiy
oiier qaeo qal kean seir qey kaeir kir oaaeen
oaen cheaad chaen kaid diien chaiy chaiio oeel deaad
sair qar oied chaal deeead chaao chiiiin chiieil
taal oien oeiey tied oeean cher sio oaiir seio
keao oiair

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey tiael chier
cheiad cheeay tao cheal chaio oaaaid siin sear qaial
kian qial oaeo saiil qal kean chier teael qaen
kear sio oaiir cheeel oied kaain siaiy kaid diien
saao oael char taaal oaio diieo sir tial oaio
deal sead keal chin oair tair cheao oieo dair tio
saio deeay saiiy chiy kied sied keid deal siiio
saeo tier kiieo chiiieeain oair kaean taed oeeao
say kiel oiael say qeil chieo daio diao qaeo
taair chaio seial cheao oeean chaed diay oaen oeeao
siad daaiy tieeen diiil oaeid daaiy tiiad taar
oaad qaio daio oiel chaay chaid dear chian keaid
oaiid say qeo oaio siil dey

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy sel qay qeao oeao
oied kieed oiy qeal oiy qiir chaaan qiiir deio
qiiil qeiaer qar sail saeo qaal oen seir keein
tiael chier sar siiid dael chail sen chian oir
oeaiio oaaao cheal chin oaen cheaad chaen qeeid qiir
deeel oied kaaio saaio cheal chin oeeed oiier sein
chieeo oaiiy teaeo kier oay qeeal oair cheey oiaiy
oeaid oied kieer siaiy qead kaeo kiy kael chead
tieo daaey cher oao chieo cheey oein oieed oein
qaaal chiier kair kaen qiead diieo chey tio seao
saay cheiey tead siay oaiiay oir oiieo chiiieeain
oaen cheeo saio oaiy oaeiay kiiy oaen chail sieo
chiiied oeen chaaid qiel oan oeer cheal siay oaen
cheaad chaen teaan teiao oiey siir deal sied qain
oieal chead qaid chey qiaiy kaid diien siel chian
seao oeao oied kaiin saead dial sir qar cheiad
chier sead chier seeid qiir chaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey kiaed oiiey
tieo oeel oay tiel say taar oeeen kay qeeel
chiaed oiiey tieo oeel oay tead siay oeao oiao
qeieiy cheey chead cheaer tiiin qieao oaaao
oaaaiiean saiil taed oiair sel qay kaael deead
qay qeo oieo oaaiy seir kieo daio chead qay qier
oeeel seao chaaio char qied qeaiid oaiin cher saaao
chaaio sied kaaaay kir cheal siad oiin chian oair
qeied qiien teed qiad chead qio cheeer kieey said
oeiy cheed qeiair qar sail saeiy oaen sead qeien
kied oieir kaiiay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy oaieo sir tiaed
teeao oaaan chaio chaaal ked kiir qeal oiy qaid
oeiy chaio cheio qieo deear tiiil oaeid oeo oaieed
cheiy oaio chaaal ked kiir ter chiaeay saio cheao
oier qier oeieeeed tial sid qier kir chiy kied
oieir taid tieaad deaan oaaal oial cheeeaaien
cheey oear oaio chaaal ked qaair qead qel qay qaao
oieo oaiio oiiey taied chieeo chiaen qaad keid
daid keo saaar qaaio oaaaiiean sein cheal cheial
oiy tiaeid tieo oiier seeel dael oiiieeain oaen
chair oaean tead cheaer tieeiy cheey chaied chieeo
chiaeo tin oied chaaal teeay seao oeeel qid teear

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey taio saad
keel oaaaiiean oaer tieo siiil tad kiier tiel
say qiel chaey chiael tin seio taaio tey kay tieo
siiil tad kaen qar siiid dael chaiy oiiy cheael
qaen tael oaiin qaay oeeao siad daaiy tiiaay
siir chaaan kiiy chaieo deeeo deeeaaien sead dao
saaaiiean saair qaiil taal chiey cher cheear keeo
cheeeao oaaaiiean seeiin sear tiair taaal oaaaay
kir oiied kear teey saial kian qiad oeiy oeein
taaao seao cheil chair qar sial chieen kaen oiein
qeaaan cheer oaid sir qeial cheeiy oaen sear qeien
kaeil daaey sio chaaey siear kied chaey oiel chaed
chiiieeain oair kaean teed qiad chead qio cheeer
tead cheaer kieey sey teed tead kaey oeer diiio
kaey saad kaeieo sair qeeaeo qiier oaer qaair
keeao siad oaaiy qeao seaao qay kieel kiiy oeal
oiy qeaay keal siay oaen oeo cheel chiiieeain
oeaid chaeay keer diiaan said oeel chiiied oeeo
sir qiaiy kied chaey sar saaio oaaaiiean sein sey
tael oil teeay oaeiay

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oay qiiio
oeel daid tieo oaio oaaaiiean oail chey keer
diiin kiao taaal tiaeir ter oiaid oeil oer kir
chaiy cheil saal oeaiieo oan oaer kaean taied
chieed oiy ter said oeiy chaieeo chiiio dail sen
cheey sieal chead tiieo oiieo din oir saao oiaey
sein qio sial saaaio chiiieeain oir oeeed oial
siey chian saiir dair chio seao saay diy keil sal
kiiey saiil qaay diar qiiin qiear qeio chaaad
cheear oir seao oaiil kiiey sar sied keid chiiied
oeeo chel ken saeid cheier siaiy kay qar siead
chiiied oeen oeiey tair keer daied deay oay qieo
cheel din oeeeo oeao dad tiao seian kaio chao
cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seir teed qiad
chead qio cheeer kaio siil del tar qeal oiy qiir
chaaan kaaal deiad dio oaio daey chieo oeel oal
qir kied chaey sieo saeo tiaial sen siey dead
cheaer ter chiiin qieao oaaao oaaaiiean saiil taed
oiair oieo daio chear ter cheed tead kaey sar oaiy
oaear qeo oieo oaaiy cheear taio cheeer tiial
oaair qeaaan dair siiil chieo oeeao oer taid qeao
chieen sar oiiio diieo oead tiiiiy ter chiiaan
chaeay qey teey cheaal keid daear saao oaeo qiaid
oer taiy oeao oeao dad qay kir oaaal oial
cheeeaaien sey tael oil teeay oaiiay oair kal kiy
teed qiad chead qio cheeer ter sail oeid oaaaiiean
saay cheaay siaiy kay qar saaio oaaaiiean sein
cheal cheial oiy kear sey kaeir kiiy oeaid deeeo
dao chaaad oen sey teed qiad chead qio cheeer kaio
siil del tad tair

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qel qin
oaiil kaeo ken chao oeeen qaid chaed oail oiel
chaaan saay chieo said teio keear oeeeo cheeao chiid
teed kiy taeay qead deeeaaien oiio cheeed qay
taar oiiid dael chaiy oair kaer tad qail chey kear
keeer deal cheao cheier sar sial chey tar qiieo
chaed deeay qeeiiy oeer diiin tier qeao oer qaio
dael oiiieeain oir oead taed chiey chier kaiio
seaay qaay sein oiaed dael chian taaid qiel oan
sar sieaao deeeo oiel oan sear qieal chead tiieo
oiieo din oiiy sir teaad chaen tiao keid dear
oeao sied tear seaaan oar oiel chaid tean chair
kaid deaad chey tair siaiiiid kael char kiio chial
sir qeiair qar seeen kay keo saiil chey qear qey
tieeo chaal oaiid oier qaao oaieel qaen tiiil
chieel kead qair siiin ter chaeaaaad qeil oer
keiey kair qar seaao siil oiel oan sear kaio
seeid qeiey tier tiao qein kaio cheaay sar chio
oead kieo oeean cheey oaeir tied oaiid seaan
taeay kiio saad qeiy cher chiiin qey qaiao tair
teiad oiair sio seier kay tiao keel deaar oeao
chaid qiad chieer sar oaiy chiy kied oieid tan oiao
taaal tiaeir kead oiiey taeay qey ter oao chieo
cheey chay qieo saeo tied tian qeil cheaao saeo
tied kean chaeay qey kieey chaio sied keid
oaaaiiean oiiio deer kay qail oaaaay taar dail
oeiy chian oeiey tieel qen chiiil diaiy taar chiel
chaed chiiieeain seio taal chiiieeain oair kaen
tieo oaaed qaiir oieen qey ter sio sail sid qier
keieeeer qaay saad tied dein kaeaer sier chiain
qied keid oiiey teao chiel cheal seeao oier kir
oiieo chiiieeain oaen cheeo oaeo chio saeo taair
oieen kao sied qaio diieiy tael oear teed kear
tian chair qieaad ched qen cher chiao seian tieel
qaen qiir del kied chaal oiaiid oan sein sio
siier oaer taaen taaan kir chiar qiian taao cheaao
saeo tied kean cheey oaio chead kiao teien oeeeo
cheeao chiid teed kiy taeay qead deeeaaien chaaao
cheeed qay keao chaieed dieen kir oiel cheeao oeiy
cher saaaiieal kaio chiiied oeeo oaio chan oiiio
deer kay teeo oaio chiiio oeiaer qar seeiiy chieo
oeel chael oeo saeo taair chieo dio saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey keein teed
qiad chead qio cheeer tead oeid dead chey keer
diiin qio siieo kier kir oeaiio oaaao cheal chin
oaen oiiil ched kaaio saaio chan sar said oiao
qiad chiel chiien saio cheao oier kied oieir kir
seo oaio oiiy chieed oein qiey oio siieo kier
keiey qaao oaeo qiaid oer kiid taal cheo saaaid
oieo din oir seiad kiy qail oeel deal saiy oir
chiey chian oaeiad qeiy cher chiaeay saiid dian
keiey teaeo kier cheiad dio seaiid oiay seao
oeeel qid tian keid chaey chail oeiy cheear kaio
chaaal ked kiir kiaiy oeaid dial cheay oeer diiin
kao oaid oeiey qiear tail oeiy cheed teao cheao
saiy seio taaio tey teied chael char teed teao
chaeiar taier taaao sieaal oeo sair qar chio seao
saay oaiid dian tiy kaiiay oair qaad keid daid
keo saaar qar siiid oair chied oeean sey taio
cheio teel oiair cher chiiil cheil saaaiiean cheaad
chaen keay oiiio oaaeer qiaiy kay qar saaio
oaaaiiean sein cheal cheial oiy ter oiaen chian
saiin kaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey tead sid kiy
teed qiil deeeaaien seal oiy qiir chaaan kiid
tiaeao oier kir siiio teal char tieeio oeiy cher
chaio oiil chiiey keeen qar chio seao saay dead
siil oaaaid siio char tiieil taal oien oeiey tiy
taar chiiil daid tail sen cheey cheed kiil
oaaaiiean sieo cheel oiair cher oiio seeeeo oaiir
seio keao din

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey tiiil oaeid
oaaiy qeaiid oiay oaaiao keear chiel say taar
oeeen teao oieo dair qar seeaeo qiier seiaed
qeao cheear oir oied cheio keid deal seean siey
chiio cheao deay cheeo seio taead sir kay tiaer ter
oao chieo cheey chiiio diieo oead tiiiiy taeay qey
ter oao chieo cheey oein oieed oein tier til tey
qiead diieo chey qar siiid oair oaen sear qey
tiaeil qin chieed deaad sir qear keein taied
chaaio oiy tier til tey qaid chiao chao oier kaaed
keer deey cheey oaiid chieed char kaeo taio oen
chaeay qey tiiil chain dier tian keaid oeeao say
kir oiieo chiiieeain oeiey kaid tiao tey qeiaey
teeo seio qair oiiy oaen seeel cheal seao oaaaid
siin siaiy tiao qaeiy oaiio cheel din oaen oaid
diien chiy taieo deeeo deeeaaien chiiel dear oir
seil chiao oeil oiiir kaen cheaad cheen saay chieed
deaad sir teaar oeir tiiil chain oiir deeel oied
qiel oan sein oaio chad kan cheaay oay qaid oieo
chiiieeain oeiel qeio kiaey said oeiy chaieeo
chiiio dail sen chian oaaed keer chieey sey qead
cheied char ter sail oeid cheen oear qio sail oieeo
oeo sair qar cheiad chier sead chaain oaean taid
qiad deay sar chio seao saay cheao oael siil chead
qair oeiey tied oeean cher oael seao chaaal oial
cheeeaaien oear chieeiy cher oiaen chian saiin kaio
chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seir kieo daio
chead qay kied siad chied oiy qiir oeo oaio oiiy
diaid qin sar siiid dael chail sen oieo daio chear
kaio chad keal chin oir oaiio said tein taiy del
kied chael cheeean cheal siay chied chiiieeain siiir
oeeeed daead sir teaad chaen keer deed kil taio
oen sier oead taed dieo chey teao oieo daid kiy
ter oear chiiil oeil chaal oied tear siiio oaaeed
tein teer chiiil deear tier taiy dao oied qeil
cheeal chey tieo oaaed kaeay teao oieo dair qaid
oer taiy chaaaad oiaid oer kair qaair qiiil
chiiiiy teal cheiay oiir daid qiad cheier chiao
qaeiy chiy qiel oiio cheear teal saaio oair oaaeen
chaiy oaio cheao oier keiey tied oeean cheeao
deeeaaien cheal cheao chaeaey sail oiieo chier cher
oieo daio chear qaid oeiy saaao chiid qay kiel
oiael say kiieo diay oaen cheaad chaen kaer kay
tiaer ter oiaen chian saiin kaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qieao
oaaao oio siiir dead oead qead oen siir chaaan
kir oeaiio chaeael chin chaiy cheaid oial seil oan
oied chaey chio saio oeied qaeo qay kir oieel kil
qin chiean cher chiiin taeay kaeo teiay chaaao kiel
oer kir saio cheao oier teaeo kier oay tiaer ter
sey tael oil teeay chiy keaid deeeo deeeaaien
chieeiy cher oao chieo cheey cheay deao oieo dair
qiaiy kay qar siiio oaaeed ken oir saao oael char
teaal tel ken oaaan keiey qeio kiaey chaio oiil
oiiey tiaeo taio chaain sar chieo seaal qaaiy cher
chaio chial oeel daid kier saiao tear chiy qeiar
qar seeiin oeay cheo oiel oied qaaiy keiey tiy
kir saein oaaed keer dead chieer oieen

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sieao oaaao
oaaaiiean sail saeil chel ken oied sied tied chier
sio oail chey keer diiin qey kiio chaio oid qeeil
chaeo qien siiio teal char ter oiael oan chieen qar
siiio oaaeed tein taiy dao cheal chead kiiey ter
cheaal tel ken oeao seay sar siaiy tiao qaeiy chiy
keil sal kiiel oiy kao cheao chiiey chair chaio oieo
daeaaar cheao oieo daid kiy ter oaiid chieed char
ter chaaad cheil sair oeaid daio deeeaaien chieeiy
oiio chial sir qar chio seao saay oiiir cheo oiel
oied qaaiy kair kaen tio saio deeay oir oaaeen
cheer oear teey chaied chieeo chaan oaiiay oir oiieo
chiiieeain oaaed keer deal cheial oiy

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seir tiiil chiel
oiael say qiel chied kean chead oeid dead chey keer
diiin kiiiel cheal chiao keel oaaaiiean chieed
deaad sir qar seial cheeiy oiid kied qeiy chaeay
qey ter said oeiy chiael oiiay saay oieiy tiead
dear oiiy oaen seaaed chiiin oaair tail oeear
seil oied kaio chiiio diay chaar oeeen qar seiaiy
oeao daio oieo daeaaar cheao oieo dair qiaiy kay
qar siaiiiio dieen cheey saial kian keer oail
oaeil oiier oaeo tian qeo oieo oaaiy oear seien
qaeo taed oeeao oier tied oeean sein kaeid tey

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey cheao oieo
dair qaid chaid teao chiel chiieo chey kail chey keer
diiio kaey chieo sid teeay qaid oieo chiiieeain
oir oaeil daaey seio kaen qar seiaiy oieo daio

oil teeay chao chieeid oaaaid siin siaiy kear ter
sey tael oil teeal chan cheed tead kaey saay

seaan tian oaaay oail saeil sen siir dao chiiad
qeil sin chair oied chaal deeead chaao oed kear

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey teal cheial
oil qin oaer qied keid diieiy qeal oiy qiir
chaaan qaao oiain sied keid diieiy kaieo sir
teiey seir teal chad qeiey ter sail oeid cheen
sear qeien kiid taed oied tao oiiir kir oiel
oiaey chaeay kieo oial cheeeaaien sey teal saaio
cheao dio oaen sail oiieo din oiiy oeaiio oaaao

qey teed tead kian qear qeien qeaiiy qear teear

oead deeeaaien oeein teed tead kaey oay qiiil

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey teal
cheial oil qin cheaay sied keid oaaaiiean seao
oaaao deaad chey keer diael oaaao chaey sieo cheel
cheeay kied cheao deeeaaien cher chiiin kaio seeid
kael sein oiio oaead kian qaad qaid tein kir
oaeiey cheao oieo dair qiaiy kay qaay sein sio
siier saaio oaaaiiean sial said teio tey qar
sail oaaeed qiil oir qaay oieiy qar sial said
qaaiy cheey sead chaain oal kiy teal oead kiiied
oeen oiiy oaen oeead tail sen oieey sey qieal
chead qaiy oiiy chiey oeal cheeo saair qar cheeead
chaao chaeaal kiel sin cheaay sied keid oaaaiiean
seao oaaao deaad chey qar sail saeiy oaen siaiiy

oieo cheal siiy diel say qaeo qied kean sail
oeaiid chied oied qaen taio cheeer keal sial saair
qar sail saeiy oaen sear kiio oaead kian qeaaey
siaiy tiao qaeiy oeaiio oaaao cheay sein chier tiy
kaiio oeel deaad said tein

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sio seeiid qair
saao oiain oail chey keer diiin taid qeied ken
seaad oaiid oer kir oaeo siiid daair chiaeid tieo
oiier sar siiid dael chail sen oiio cheien oeiey
kaid tiao tey qar sieo seil chaal oied tear sar
oeead qiaiiiio chael oen oir oaaal oial cheeeaaien

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey kiio seao
chiiel oiy qio seeiid keen oail chey keer oail
oaio chiiied oeen saaao dien deeo oeieeer oiio
seao chiiel oiy qier kaear tao cheal saiio oier
taar deeo chiair tail oeiy seaal kan sar siiid
dael oier chier tiain qaid chiao chao oied qaeo qey

saead chied oied qaaiy keao oed qiial oiy ter oao
chieo cheey chear keeer dao chaal oail oiel say tiar

tear sar seeel chiio chad kael oen oir sid tial

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey taio oiil
oiiey tiaeo taio chaain oail chey keer diiin kaeio
keao oeean saaao chiir ter seio kaen kiiio dael
chial sir qeaao chieey ter sail oeid cheen oaeo
tian til tey qaad keid daid keo saaar qieo saaar
qaid saiar qar seial cheeiy oaen cheaad chaen kieio
qair sey tiiel dear oir cheal siad dan oeeo
siaiiiid tieo oeieiy cheal cheio teey oar siaiiiio

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy chead oead kieo

oied chad qay kir saao chieo oaail say teaeo kier
oay tiaer tier tiain qaad keid daid keo saaar
qar sieo seil chaal oied tear saay oieil tein kir
oaaeen oaen sear tiair taaal oaaaay kir oaaal
oial cheeeaaien oaieel qaen taeay qier kir sid
tial said tein kaen sar saad qaio daio oen oair
qaid chaid teao chiel chiieo chey keaio chiiio

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey tieeo chael
cheeed qair oiid taal chiiieeain chiel say taar
oeeen taaao kiel oer keiey tead siay oir oeid
deal siiio sair qar sail sieo ched tey taaar deed
qiil deay siead oeiey qeaay keal siay seio taal
oier qiiir deeo saaad taaey chaied diaiy kiiy
oied siil chiid teal chin chiar qaeid oaaiy qaead
chied oied qaaiy qaiir deeo seio taead sir qaad
keel oaiy chead siil oaaaid siio char ter oaio
chieel teeay oeaid chaeay qar saad keel oaiy oeao
saal daaiy keeen qar sieo seil chaal oied tear

cheiad oial sein qiear keo oeil oiiid tear

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey qieao oaaao
deaar oeao chaaio keel oaaaiiean oail chey keer
diiin teed qeiaio chein keiey tead siay oir oiiy

kiiy oaeiao oan said saiar qiead chiiied oeeo chey

seial qen oeal siay seio keao din saao oed taaio
