qal qeo chael oiiiey oir chiy teeeaad cfhaaaiio
cphieaaiy cfhaeo keeil oead kien cheo siiio tian
oeeeo oiaid oaaao dieen chieeiy cheed qiil deay

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy oaieo sir kay
qieo oiid taal chiiieeain chiel say taar oeeen kay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey kaio seao
chied cheeay qaid chaed oaid say taar oeaao oiil
chaey chieo oaio dead saaiy kir oeaid deeead chaan
saar dead siay cheal siil oaaaid siio oiy taied
oaaaid siin seaaed daead oaaaao chiair keo oaaed
qiil dair qiiil chieo oeeao oer keieeeed tial sir
qaid chaal oaar teeeo oeeao chaid kear qio oaiir
siiin qiear qeio chaaad cheear chiey sey tead oeid
deal sail oeeen oiio chieaal kian kaer tio saio
oeier kir seo oaio oiiy daeay qey qein kiio
cheien sier oiier qaaaid chaed oiaiid chied tel tin
chiey chian oeid oeiey taio oead qair sier seo
oiiieo chean cher cheal cheiay sein chier tiain qiad
chaaio oiy kieey oiao kead tain cheaay oay qied
keid chiiied oeeo chey qaiil taed oiair cher oiaen

chail chey keay deeo sid qiied oiy qein taeaaal
sieo cheal oiy qein ter oaeir qaao oiain

feao dio oeean kaiil tin oeil saaain chiid qier
qiiir deeo saean chiaeiy kaeil kian qeiaid tar
teeao oaaan chiio cheien oeeed oial siey chier
qeaad oaiid oer qaeay cheeo seaiil teio tey qieo
saaar keo chieer saar dead siay seaad oaiid say
kaaal oial cheeeaaien oaien oiiy oieel qiad oiey
cher chiiil cheil saaaiiean chaaan teeel oaair qiaiy
kaid diien chaar oeeen kied saaal chaaio chieaay
ter chaied oaaaid siin chaaar dead cheaer ter cheeao
deeeaaien saar chaeiay qeaad oiier sear kaieel
tiy kir oaear kied oieir qeaad oaiid oer kir

chad keao oier kiiy seio kiaey oeein qiieo seal
oiy taaad cheil oeay oiir dieo chaeay tail sieo
chiiied oeen oir saein cheer deaan

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey qaid
oeel chiiied oeeo sir kaer qaad kaeieo sair
qeeaeo qiier saair tiaey tiao keeao kiy teed
qeiaio chear kiio oaead kian qial cheeean deeo
chiair tar taied diaiy tieel qaen ter oaeiy qaar
deal siad chieed oiy ter saiao tear chiy kaail
chied cheio tiy keeed oiied oiy ter saar chair kay
qiead diieo chey keead qaad tey qar sieaad ched

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey teal
cheao deal chear qeir kiio cheieo chey keer diiin
kaio oaid chaein ter oaiid chieed char kao chieo
cheey oiaiy kay qear teal cheao deal chear qiir
diey sey teaal teey chaer taaao kiel oed kead qay
kir chiar keao oael siil chead qair oir chiar keid
deal seeao oier kir oiier kaen siaiiiio dair
tiaed qin oail chey kaiil tein kiier qeio taaio
qeiy sey tiiil cheaid deeead chaan seir kaeid qir

oiel oieo chiel oier taar oein sead deeeaaien

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy oiio chad kiao
qiad chead qal kain chieo daio chear qeid kaen qaad
kaeieo sair kaid diien chiy kaiio oeel deaad sair
kail chey keer diiin qey tiaiy cthiiieed deeeaao
cthaieeay cfheiey ckhaeo keeil oead kien oar oiel chaed
chier chier teaey kir seo oieo chaear tier kair
tail oaeil deeen oeo oieo chaead kian kaid

qaay chad qaiy seo oieo chaear ter seay sear kear

sier cheal siil chiel cheay sar chair chieo seaal
qaaiy cher chaio chad keeil chey keo oeeao tead kian
qar oaiy chiao qein qead deeeaaien oeein kaeid
tey tio saio oeied qaen taead oeid daead sir
teaay chail oeiy oeal siier cheeo sid kiel chieo
din saiid daan seaid oair said oeiy chiaid ker
kaiio oeel deaad sair tiaey qeeaer kir oaaan
kiiy seo chaid taio cheied cheao diair qaay seeiiy
oeaio chiiio chiiieeain oir chiey chaeay qead chieed
oen chead seao oeo oiao kay qar cheeo sid qaiil
teeay chaiy oeo chaed oiaid oer keiey tiy teeeo
oaad qeil sin cheeo sid teio keid daid kaiin qar
seeen kaid dear chiy qeo oieo chaead kian tieo sir
qiaiy kaid oaaaiiean oaiy chiy kaaiao keear sier
chead siil oaaaid siio char ter seay sein oear
seien teal siad oaaaid siio cheio tiy kiid kied
qeil sin sar oay kiiio diieo oead qier keiey
tair

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy tead
oead taed chen saio cheao oier taar oiiid dael
chail sen siey chieo daio chear kieed oiin saar
chaaaad oiaid oer qeaad oiier oeead kaen qar oaiy
saiid dian tiy qaeo taed oaid oiao kay qear qein
qio sail sieo oair oir seo oaio oiiy oiey diiiid
cheied char kaio char qead deeeaaien cher cheey sieao
oaaao oaaaiiean saaio oaaaiiean siaiy qead kaeo
kiy teal chead tieo deeeo diaiy kir oeaid deeead
chaan chaeay kaeo teiay seaaed daead oaaaao chiair
qaid saiar qar seial cheeiy chaiy chaaaad oiaid oer
keiey qaao oaeo qiaid oer qeo oaio oiiy daeal
kaeo teiay oay qeaaey sear qey taaal oaaaay kir
oeaiid dar tian oaeied kaen oieio qair sey kaeir
tiy keao saal oeo chien cheaio oeier kir chiey chian
saeay oied chaey chieo sid tin oeid chaey chaaen
qiiio oaio dael sier kir oeeeo chiiaar kaen cheaad
chaen kiiil deear tier taiy chaiio oaaer qar saaio
oaaaiiean sear qeien qiiin tiaeiy ter chael oiy
qaar oaao oed kaeil oaid say taar daao oaio dael
sair saar died oeean sead deeeaaien cher oao chieo
cheey daaar chiio siad chaeiar qaiir chaer qar seial
cheeil chan oear chiao oeeed oiier oieio qair cher
oear oeeen qar siiid dael chaiy oaen siaiy qead
kaeo kiy qeal oen oeiey qeio kiaey oear chaaal
oaio diieo sir qar chaid chin oeao saal daaiy taio
chial oeel daid kier

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qeir
teed qeiaio chear qey taio saad keel oaaaiiean
oeer diiin qey tiael oaean cher oaiid chaiin chian
teaey tied chiiieeain saao oeao dar kaiid chieed
char qiir chaaan kair qar chio oeaiid deeead chaao
say kair qeied tar taaal chair keair tied oeean
seien kaail chier oear teear qio siil oaaid kiy
tael oiy tian seo chaieed oaaaid siio oiy tiiaay
chael siad chead qein qiir deal siad dan chian oair
kaean teed qiad chead qio cheeer tead cheaer ter
chaid tean chaen oieio qair sey tiiel dear chaier
qio chaaey siir chaaan kir chiey sey keal chaio
oaaaid siin saad kaeieo saen tiieio taair cher
chaaad dear oeay chied chaey chaaen qiiio oaio dael
sier kir seo oaio oiiy cheal sein siieo oao siad
chieed oiy tiael oaean saiay tiiil chaaiay kaead
tey tieo oaaed kaeay teio keid diieo sair qiaiy
kay qar oied chaal deeead chaao oed kear tieo dio
saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey teed qiad
chead qio cheeer tead oeel dead saeo tier tiel say
taar oeeen keo oeeao tear ter chaieeo chiiio dail
sen saar deeel oied kaaio saaio chan sar said
oiao qiad chiel chaaan cheao oieo dair qaid saiar
qar chio seao saay oaiid dian taiy dao chaaio qair
qiaiy qad char teed qiil deal cheao oen chieo din
seiar kiel chaal oail oiey cher seay sein chiaeid
kaey sar seiaiy oieed oein taeay qaiao tear
saeid cheied chieaad daey chieo siiil tad kaen qiad
oeiy oeal siay oiier qeao oeao chil ked kear kaeo
taio oen chaied diaiy kiid taal oieo cheay oeer
diiin kao oaid oeiey qiear tail oeiy cheed teao
cheao saiy seio taaio tey teied chael chad tair
qaad qaio daio cheio tiy taier taaao siil chieed
chear ter oao chieo cheey deaad chaen kay qeaaey
sear qel qay kiid taed oied tao oiiir kir oeeed
chier sead chaain oay qieo saeo qaal oiel oan sar
seeel dael oiiieeain saiid dian taiy cheeeo
chiiaar keiey tiy kir oiieo chiiieeain oaen sail
saeil chey qar sein oieed oein kieen

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey taio
oiil oair oiel oiael say taar oaid oiao qiad
chiel cheal siiin saio cheao oier kied oieir kir
oeaiio oaaao cheay siaiy qad char qio siieo kier
teeel oied kaaio saaio chan sar oaiy oaen seiaed
tiay oir oeaiio oaaao cheal chin oeiey qaao oaeo
qiaid oer kiid taal cheo saaaid oieo din oied
oieir kir seo oaio oiiy oey tieeiy chier tiy
keeel oead kiao qear ter seay seier qail oiieo
oiair seir kaio seeid kael siien kao cheao chiiey
sar chio seao saay oaiid dian kiiy chao oied qeil
cheeay seao oer tied cheo cheeeaaien cher seay cheaad
chaen kaer tail oieeo oeo oeean tio saio deeay
chied oair sey teal saaio daear oir oiieo
chiiieeain oaen siaiy qear ter chiiin kieed oein
taio chad taed chiey chaed oail oeeeo oier kir
oaaan qaiid dian qaeid cheied chieaad daey seial
deal siiil chaal chil ked kear ter chaed diay siel
chaey saair qeial daaal oeel oal qir tiao qieo
chan siead oaiy chaar oeeen taio cheel diiiy oeao
chaal chil ker taier kao oaid oeiey ter chiiaan
chaio chad taed chiey chaed oail oeeeo oier tied
oeean oieed oiin cheal saaio chan sar chieo oaaed
tial oain chaio siil del tar qiey diiaan sead
chier siaiy kaid diien oir seao chieo diay oaen
cheaad chaen teeo seil oer tian qeao oed qen cher
chiiaan cheey chiair teal saaio chan siaiy teeo seil
oer kir oied cheio keid deal siiin saiid dian
tead sial saaain deao oieo dair qar seeel dael
oiiieeain oaen cheaad chaen teeo oiid kaey sar
cheeo oiid kaey oear qiey daed oied keied char
qiir daaid tian taiy chiel oiaid cheied char taio
oead qair oir chiey oieed oein qey taio oiil
daear seao oaiil kiiey saay siiio oeel chieo oaad
tan saaao cheil oael char ter oao chieo cheey oiaiy
kay qieo cheel oiaid chien oieo seil chaal oied
tear sar saiil taair sey kiao qiad chier oieio
qair cheey cheal saaio daear oir oaaal oial
cheeeaaien cheey oaio chieel teeay oeiey tiy kir
oiieo chiiieeain oaen cheaad chaen qiaiy qear kieen
ter oaio char kiao kaid diieo chey qaay seeiiy
seier qeao oiel oied kaio daey oeo oaiiad deeeo
deeeaaien cher oiio seeeeo oaiir seio keao din
chaar oeeiiy sein chaio oiil daear

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey qieao oaaao
oaaaiiean sieo oaeiao sin oail chey keer diiin
teed qeiaio chear ter sio chaaao kaen siiio saead
deeeo chaiin oiid kieo oieo sir qar cheeo saio
diiaay cher cheeao dan chian oaeiay tair keay chiel
oieo din oir oeaid deeead chaan chian saiid dian
kied oieir taar chiiil daid tail sen cheey chiaeio
oen saao chiair keeeo ken seil chieo dail oead
kieo oeeao oan chail oeiy oiaiy chead siil oaaaid
siio char teed qiil deay chail oeiy chaied diaiy
qaiir chiiil daid tail sen chian chao chaieel
saaaiiean oaed tian kaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey teal cheial
oiy qeal oiy qeir taid qead keal siiio saead
deien chaar oeeen keeao siad daaiy taid qead keal
chin oeeeo oiaid oaean cheey oieer cheil oiiid kiid
teer qeaaan oar seeiio oen chied oeean sey qied
kead qaaaiy sar siead chiiied oeen oaen said
saiar tio seao saay chaen chieao oer keiey qeio
kiaey chier tiy kied cheeer qiear qear qieao cheiy
oeal siay oaen siead oeiey qeaal kan oail chey
qiaiy tiao qaeiy oaaan qaio cheao oier kaaaad
oiaid oer kir oeao oed qiial oiy qio siieo kier
kir oaiiad oiaid oer tao seied qaen ter chaied
oaaaid siin sein chead cheaer ter chaio cheeed kaey
sein saiao tear chiy qeiar qiaiy tiao qaeiy oaaan
kaear keid chael sied qaaan diieio taair chaied
deal siiy chiier qaeay oeer diiin qey kiio cheao
chaaeel oain deaal teey oar oaiy oaen cheaar chear
oiao kiy taio chial oeel daid kier chian oir chiey
chian oaeiad qeiy cher oao chieo cheey oein sead
chaain oay keo chied dial oaaaan oieel qeao oeo
oier kir chead seao cheil chear oaen chair oaean
kiao qiad chaaio chear qey

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey taio saad
keel oaaaiiean oaer kaiil tio saeid oen seal oiy
qiir chaaan qeao chiiad qeil sin saad qaio daio
seeeen sar chieo oaaed tial chan oiio cheao chaaeey
oir oeaid deeead chaan chian oied oieir teaeo kier
oay tiaer ter seay oay qiiil chieo oeeao oer
qaiiy chead siay chieel qeo oiaed chan sar seier
qaid saiar tail oaid qien oaael deead ker kir
oeil sier kaen seeey daaad cheiy siir oeo oaio
oiiy chaio chieel teeay oir saead chied oied qaaiy
keiey tiy kir oeao oael siil chead qair oaen
chiean oear chaio oiil oiiey qieo seil chaal oied
tear oeer diiaay chian oeao saal cheier sar sieo
saaar qieo sid qeao chied chin oaio chao cheeead
oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy sey teal cheial
oiy qeir kiao taaal tiaeir qeal oiy qeir tead
said tial oead tier taar oiead chiiied oeen siiir
oaid saiar qar seeen kay qial chel qaaiy siieo
chaed deeal ken chao chieeid oaaaid siio oen oeiey
qaao oaeo qiaid oer kir chead sial chey teao oieo
dair qeeeed daead sir qar sieo cheel oiair sail
oeel deal saiy oeiey qaao oaeo tio sir qar siead
chiiied oeen oaen sieo saaar qaid saiar kieio
qair sey kaeir tiey chaeay kaeo teiay said saiad
qean chied siil chaeiar keiel tied chiiieeain oir
chiey sey taio oiil oiiey kiel chaid teao cheil
seiey kao cheao chiiey sar oied chieo oial oier
chaaar dead oeel diaeir taeay tied qein qaiao
tear oir oiel oiaey chian oaid tian kaead keean
kiiieeail tiiad qiin siir deeel oied qiel oan
sein chaio oiil daear oir saao chiiiio seaar chiao
qieo chan

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy chead oaaed tial
chan chaaao oiil oaaaiiean oail chey keer oeay oiir
dieeo oaiiy kir oeeed oial siey oiiiey oier taiy
deal cheao cheier sar oaiy oeiey qeio kiaey oieo
daio chear kao cheao chiiey chiean said oeiy oeein
teal cheial oiy ter oao chieo cheey daio oieo cheao
oiaiiir saio cheao oier keiey tiy kir oaaeen chaiy
cheeeo saal deeeaaien chier tian qeo oieo oaaiy
cher cheal cheial oen oair kaean tieeiy cher oao
chieo cheey chied chaal deeead chaao oer keid oeiey
taeay qead chaain seao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seir tead oaaed
tial chan chaaao cheied chiiio oeaan said oaeo qiad
oiel oier tiel say taar oiead chiiied oeen oiiy
oeeeo oiaid oaaao dieen chaio cheeer tead cheaer
ter chiiin keao daio oieo daeaaar cheao oieo dair
qar sieo sid taael say kieel qiad cheier sar sieo
seil chaal oied tear oieio qair sey kaeir kir
chead seao cheil chead chan chier tiy qeaad oaiid oer
kiiy oeaid daio deeeaaien chieeen der seay oay
qeiaed tial chan cheaal teel deio tiy keiey kir
oaaeeo chan chaeay qey qiir chaaeey oaen sieo cheel
oiair cher chaio chad tieo oaid oan

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy chaid teao chiiel
char taio oiil daear chiel say taar chaiy oaear
kaaal deiad cheeay kead kieo oaail oer kir chiy
keao saal dear saiil qaal oaeo qey kear saaao
chaaio sied tair qar cheiao oen oaaed keer oaaao
chaaio chio oiiey cheaan kaid diien chian qeo oiain

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy chead cheaed qay
kaaal oiiio saeo taal oeiaed kiy qeal oiy qiir
chaaan kaeil oiiil oeeel qid tian kir oeeed oial
siey oeal siay cheer chaid oaaaiiean saair qaiil
qaay cheil sier teer cheeao siad daaiy qio sead
keal chin oaaan keiey qeio kiaey oaeio keao oeean
cher oaio chieel teeay oir oeeed oiier oaen qied
kaear kir oeiey qeio kiaey oao chieo cheey chay
keeao chio oeid deaad chey qiaiiiid kael char taeay
qey qaid oeiy chaio cheio qieo deear taeay kaeo
teiay chaaar oier kiier kieel qiil deay sar seeel
dael oiiieeain oeiey tair keer diiaay chian oeao
saal cheier

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy oieo oiied siad
chead qay keao oed qaio cheao cheeay taar oeeen qar
siiid dael chaiy chaiy oaio daaid chaed oied tey
qar siiio saaaio cheo cheeeaaien chaaao cheied chiiio
oeaan siaiy kear tiiil cheaid deeead chaao oao oen
oir cheal siil chiel cheay oiir daeay kaeo teiay
saiil kiiieo oen oeeer oaio daaid chaed oied tey
qar oied chaey sieo saeo keao oaair kiir der
chiiin qaid cheeel oeeao chiiar kir oead taed chiey
chier tiain qeaaey sar seeiin chaaar chieo oiied
siad chead qay keiey tiy kir oiieo chiiieeain chiy
keil sal kiiey siaiy qear tiaeiy qiir daaid tian
teer oieo cheel oiair

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy chiael chiel chaaal
oeel oal qir kail chey keer diiin tiael chiel
chaaal oeel oal qir qar siiid daey oiir oead chier
sar siead chiiied oeen chao chieeid oaaaid siin
siaiy teeo seio taead sir qar oay qial chel qaaiy
oeeen keair kaeo siiid daair cher oao chieo cheey
chaid dear oeiey tair qiiil chieo oeeao oer kaio
chao cheeead oaeay cpher saaao chaaio sied tair

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy chead cheaer kiio
siad chaeiar qeil oed qaio cheao cheeay taar oeeen
qaid saiar teeo oeid diaeir ter oao chieo cheey
chiir dier tiy keaid oeeao say kir oaaal oial
cheeeaaien chieeiy chaeay qier kir oiieo chiiieeain
oeao oiiir keiey kaid tiao tey teiad cheeay kaaan
keiey qeio kiaey chael seeid kiain chaaan kaiiad
oiaid chiiiio seier kir oaeil daael sin chair seio
chaaad cheear chaier kear oael oay qar seeel dael
oiiieeain oeiey qier oeao oael siil chead qair
oeiey tair qeao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seir qel ker tao
cheal oiy qaaao cheiy chier til qir teied chael char
tead oeid dead chey keer diiin keeo sial saaain
dio saio oeier qiiin ter chiair kao cheao diair
tieed deaad sir qar siiid dael chaiy chaiy dao
cheal oil qaaao cheiy cher sey tead oaaed tial chan
oiaid oeil oer qaiir oeaaed chiiin oar sial chair
qein oaeo taaay qiieo chaed cheeay kaaeey chaar
oiiid daair oeal chaio oaaaid siin saiil qaay
oeao oer qeain sar oiel chaed chiiieeain chaiy
oaead dial sir qear qeien kiid taed oied tao
oiiir kir oaaan tiy kaaal oail saio daead oain
oio siieo kier kir sieo cheao daio cheieeer oieo
daio chear tiiiid cheied char ter oao chieo cheey
cheay chaid dear chair keay deied chael char ter sail
oaeil oiier oiir chear cheear teaal teey chieio
qair cheey chied qeaiir cheeao siad daaiy tiiaay
cher chiiin tian oair kay qiiil chiiiil qay kir sid
tial said tein kaen sar oaiy oaen chiean oear
saaao chaaio sied tair qar chair sieo cheel deeay
qieo seil chaal oied tear sar seeel dael
oiiieeain oaen cheaad chaen teeo seil oer qiiir
cheo oiel sal teeeo oiay oair taaan qiel chaey
oiir diaeid tieo oeeel daear oeaid chiey siir
chaaeey oaen sieo sid qeao chier sar sieo cheel
oiair

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey kaio siil
dieen keao sied tead oiy tier tiy kied sied qaio
chiiied saaio oin cheeead oiieo chen chiel say taar
oaiil qaay chiiy seao chaal chaiin qeaao chieey ter
cheeao dan oaeo qiiy daiy oeao sead oen oeein
kaio cheaay seaaed daio seial cheao oeean chian
oaiio seaay kied chael cheieo chey tieo oaio chiiio
dail sen cher chaaao cheied chiiio oeaan oied chieo
oial oier siaiiy qiey chaien oaaed keer daio oead
qair oiel oeao chiiieeain chair keay oiiio cheel
chiiieeain oir oeaiio oaaao cheay chaaar daaao oiil
oaaaiiean seier qial chel qaaiy oaien oiiy cheer
diaer kaio oaid chan chial said qeao oaaao oeiey
ter seay oay kiel chaal oaad kiel chaied dear chieo
seaiil kied oiao qear ter cheal saaio oaiy siiir
oiead dael chin oir oeil chaer chiir chieed oein
keeo chad teaal keid dead chey qeeiiy cheaar chaeo
tean deer chied chaey sar seeen teaad chaen kaer
kiel chaed daan chil tey qiiio cheel chiiieeain oair
qaay sieo oiieo chen oir oeiey qeio kiaey sein
kaeid tey qar seeen teaad chaen qaay oiel chaal
oaad kiel chaaao siead chan chieo seaiil kied oiao
qear ter sail oeid oaaaiiean chaaar daied cheil
oan cheeeo chain oey tael sead qaid oiiey cheaan
kay qial chieen qiel chaey cheaad chaen taaar daied
cheil oan sear qey qail oeid deaad chey qail
oaaeey sain oein kaio cheaay oeer chael seao oeao
chiiad qen sein kaio cheaay cheaan qiad diaiy kir
saao oael char qail oeel daid kier siey daied
cheil chaear saao oael char teey chaio saaio oin sar
sail oaid qiair chian saiid dian qier chiain teeo
saio oein qaid oeiy cher chiiin kieed oein teey
oaio siil dey qar oiiil daid qeean sead chaain
oay qiiio saead deien oir oiieo chiiieeain oaen
seiaey qar sial chey qein oieed oein qiieo chaed
cheeay kaaeey chaar oiead chiiied oeen saio cheao
oier tao chiiad keel oier kaiiad oiaid chiiiio
seied qaen ter chaio oiil oair cheal siil chiel
cheay sar sail oiieo chen oair qaay cheeead oiieo
chen chaar dio seao saal oen sio siier saay chieo
oeel oay tied oeean cher chaaao seeiid kian teaad
chaen taaar daaao seeiid kian qiaiy kay qar sial
chey taaar chaio oaid daear saao oaieey kaaeey
chaar deeo sid kaiy cheer oeao diaiy teael qaen
teey oeeer oeao sead cheier chiao qieo chan cheaan
kay teeo oaeier kir oiiy chied oair chead oaear
qeaad oaiid oer qaiin tiy kiid taal oien oied
siad chiid qay kir seo chieo deeead chaan chian
saiid dian tead siay oir oiieo chiiieeain oaen
cheaad chaen qeiaey keer oaaal chead keal chin oaen
oeo oeear cheal chad kiiied oeeo chey qear keein
kiiied saaio oin saay saad tair teeeal oaid oan
seeaer keeeo oiao qiil chaeiar kir oaaiad qay
qeao oieey cher chiieir qiiio cheel chiiieeain oir
oaaiar taio oead qaid chey qail oeid daead oeear

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey kaio chaio
chead teaad chan oiao taaal tiaeir qeal oiy qiir
chaaan keeeo chaid teien seao oeao oied kieed oiy
kiao taaal tiaeid qay taar oaad qaio dain oiir
diiil cheaid chaeay kaiy oir oeeed oial siel oan
oiir dier qieo daid ken chao oied qeil chiiiin
cheaal teey oeeiiy chieao oer kiiy chao oean
chiiieeail kieo deeead chaao say taar diaey qaad
tair qear qeien qeiar qaiil qaay diel say kaiiad
oiaid chiiiio seied qaen ter sio chaaal qaiy oaaan
keao saal oen chaio chial oeel daid kier cher seal
oen chied oair oear oiaid dead chead kiiey kear
oeeen qar chair seio chaaad cheear chiel say kir
seiay keiey tair qieo sieo diao seian

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey tead oaaed
tiay chiel say taar oeeen kay qaid chiiad qeiy
said oaio dael sair chaeaay qiiir dieo chied din
seain seeen keay dieo chied din oir oeaid oier
taiy daier qaar chair kaean tael cheeed teed kiy
kaiid chieed char ter seay oiir daio cheeer tieeo
oaiiy keiey qeio kiaey chiiaan saar chair kal kiy
taied chaaio oiy ter cheeao deeeaaien saar cheao
oiiir qeaad oiier oeead kaen qeiaey qiaiy tiao
qaeiy chaar oeeeo tial chan oaio char qead
deeeaaien cher chiiaan saar died oeean sey qied
kead qaaaiy sar sieaao deeeo oiey saar dead siay
oaeiad kaio daaid oer taieo oaiio sin cheaay
siead deaad chey tiao kaeo chio said kiy qio sead
keay sead teear kead oan cheiad daio oen cher sier
tian qaaad keeay tao cheal saiio said oiiey sar
seeiin oiir dier tiy kaiio oeel deaad sair qar
oeo oiel oeao chied cheeal chey qiad chead taead sid
kiy keeer oaao oael char taio chieaal kan sar
seeen teaad chaen taaao kiel oer kir oiid tiaeao
oier kir oeao oed qiial oiy kiaid dead chead
kiiey ter sey taio oiil oiiey taaan teeeo oeeao
chaid kear taaal oaio diieo sir qiaiiiid kael char
kaeo teao cheao oian qaad keel oio cheeead saio
chan sar chio seao saay chiiy oair kaen taaan kir
oiieo chiiieeain oaen seiael tian kaiiad deeen
oiian keeeo chieo oial oear taeay kaeo teiay
saaio oaaaiiean sein chead oaaed tin chaaid tian
kir oaaal oial cheeeaaien chian chied oeean oeeer
cheaid oaiid say qeio tiao seo oier kir oiieo
chiiieeain chaiy deal seao chaaio cheeay tiao qieo
chan chiao qaio oaaaiiean oeead kaen

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seid kaen taaen
tieo chied oaiy siel chian oied seeid kaey said
chaed oaid say taar oeeen qaay siead dael chial
sen cher chaied chear tian oeao oiiir kaiio seaal
tian kir oaeiey oay taid cheeay kaer kay tiaer
ter chiiin teey oaio chaio oid kiy kaio char qead
deeeaaien cher chaaad dear saay said chiiad qen
oaio oaid chael chin chead siay chiain qieo cheel
cheeay qiiin ter cheeao deeeaaien chian oaeiay kiel
oeao chiiieeain oaen sieo saaar tieed daair saaal
chead keal chin oir oaaan qiiir oail oaear qaiir
oiil oeaay qar oiel chaed chen oiiy oaaal deiad
oiair chaio cheeer tead cheaer tier tiain qaad keid
daid keo saaar qar seeen keay oaiil qaay oaiir
oaay said chiiad qeil sin siead dael chial sen cher
chaaad dear sein saaal chiier tead siay siian
keeeo chieo oial oear tiiin kaio chieel teeay oir
chiey chian chao saal deeeaaien chiiin taaal oaio
diieo sir qiaiiiid kael char ter chaio oiil chan
sieo seil chaal oied tear sar saaio oaaaiiean
sein chiaeiy teal chaio deeeaaien cheey chian siel
oeao deeead chaao cheeao oen saaal chead keal chin
chaar oeeen teaad chaen kay qeaao chieel qin seaaed
chiiied oeen saiir diao qieo chan oied chaey sieo
oead taed chiiieeaio oaiiin chir oiiy saiid daan
oaiiad deeead chaan sey tael oil teeal chan chieeeo
dead sen chieeen der chiiil cheil saaaiiean cheaad
chaen qaay oeeao siad daaiy tead oaaed tial chan
oaio oaid chael chin oair qaay seaaed chiiied oeen
oeeed chier chian oair kay qaid chiiad qaaiy kied
seeid ker taar chiiil daid tin oaen chieo sid tin
chaar oeeen qein oieed oein ter chaieeo chiiio daiy
oaen oeaio chiiied oeeo oiieo din oair taaen
qeaaed chiiied oeen oir oaaeeo chan oieed oein
qead chaain oay qeaao siil chieed chear taeay qead
chaain cheaay oaid diien chaiy cheaid diaen tiao
kaeo chio sair qaay saay seaao chaio oaaaid siio
chin oied seeid kan seaaan

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy oiaid oeil oer
tiar tiain qaid chiiad qeiy seal oiy qiir chaaan
qiiir deied chael char ter chaaad cheil oeay oiir
oaial kian qar seeiin oiir chieed oein qead chaain
oay kead taid kiiiey oir oeaid deeead chaan saar
chied oieir kir oaaan keiey qaao oaeo qiaid oer
qaao chaeaaar oeair qaao oeao daio cheieeer oieo
daio chear ter sel qay tao chieeid oaaaid siin sar
sieo seil chaal oied tear oiiio diieo oead qier
kied siil chiir keeeo qail sid qied tey qar oaiy
oaen seiaid qein chier kiiy seaiid chier cher oiio
chial sir keo oiel oied qaen kieer oier oaen chiao
qieo chan chieo sir kaid oaaaiiean cheeo oaeier kir
oaaeen oaen oaid diien chil tey qied keid oaiy
oir oied oiil chian teiad cheeay

feao dio oeean kay keo oaaeed kier seal oiy qeir
keao daio oieo daeaaar oiel chaal deeead chaao
oiao kiy tier tiy kaiio oeel deaad sair qaay
seeiiy oeaio cheao chiiieeain oaeiad qeiy cheal
saaio chaey oaiiy tail oeiy sio sial saaad kier
sio seeiid qair chiel say taar oeeen kay qiead
daeaar saao oaieel qaear daaao kiel oer teal
siad oiin saar chied oieir kair kaean teed qiad
chead qio cheeer ter chiaeay cheer chieio qair oear
chaio oiil oiiey taaan kir oaaeen siiir deeo
saean chaeay qey qiey cheal siay chao chael cheeed
qair chieel qen chaied diaiy qeaio char kaio seao
chied cheeay keair kied chad teal seeao say keil
oiiid kead qay keao oiiir keao saal oen cher seal
oen oair qeiaed qeao cheead sid keaio chiiio
chiiieeain saiiy seiar keo oaaeed kier oiio cheien
saiid dian kaaed qeier kaeo tian teeeo oeeao
chaid kear tiiin kaeo taio oen chaeay qaid oeiy
oeao dio oeean kied chaey saair keaio chiiio oaiid
say kaaan tiy qeao chiiad qeil chien dieo chaid tin
saao oaieel qaeo ker keao chaid qiad chieed char
teed qeiaio chear ter chieeo oied qaen keeer chaiio
seaay qar oiel chaed daan chian kied oieir kair
kaean teed qiad chead qio cheeer ter chiaeay oair
kieio qair sey tiiel dear sier oeao saal daaiy
qio sieaal chiaen taaan kir oaaan qaiid dian taiy
oaao oiain chaaar cheal siay oair qaair keaio
chiiio oaiid say tao chiiaad tear oaiil tin oaaal
chaeay qar seeiin oiir daeay qey ter cheeao
deeeaaien chiaeiy qeay oiir oio oiil oaaaiiean
chair oied chaal deeead chaao oed kear ter seal oen
sier chaieo deeeo dao saaaiiean siaiiiid kael char
kear cheal cheeed kiaio siad chead qay qier saeo
kaiil kaaiay keeel seao chaaio char keeo siay
oiier kiel cheeao diaiy tao chiiaad tear seeen
qaid chaed oied tey qail saeo qaay chir siel chieo
dad tair kiir dieeo oaiiy kir oaeil daaey saar
oeain cheaay oay kiiio diieo oead qier kir oaeiey
oiir oaiao tear oir oaaao qeiy chaio oiil daear
oeao oael siil chead qair oeiey tead siay oir
oiieo chiiieeain cheer oeiaey qiaiy tiao qaeiy
oaaeen chaiy oaao oiain siaiy kay qar chio seao
chaay chaid dear oeao oael siil chead qair oeiey
tair teaay cheeo oeid daid tar

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy seal oiy qey taio
oiil daear oaeo chiaen teeo oaaaao oaeied tead
oiy taied chead tied kean siir chaaan taiy cheaid
oied kaid qien oir cheeel oied keeeo saaaiiean
sear tiair tead oaaed tio oaio daey said chiiad
qeo chaio siil deeeaaien cher saar cheiey kaid tiao
tey qeiaey qar sial saaad qiair chieo chaeay qaay
oiir oio siier chiao qieo chan cheeo sid qeiaey
kied chieo oial oier oiir daeay kaeo teiay chieo
sir kaid oaaaiiean sar chieed dieen chaid qeier
kaid dael saiir cher chiiaao sin oiir chieed oein
tier kaear kied oaiir seaad oaiid oer kir oead
taaid sair oeiey qeio kiaey sier kir chead oiiil
daal oeiair qiaiy kear ter chier kaeo siiid daair

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy oaieo sir kail
chey kay qieo cheel din oaeo chiaen kaer qiead daid
qead tain oeer chaiid chieed char qaar diy kir
oaiio oead keao oail oeeeo sin siaiy tiao qaeiy
oir oiel oieo dieaaio oier oiir dier keaid deeeo
chieed oiy tieeiy siear tieaal kian qar siead
chiiied oeen chead oiiil daaao seiair qar siad
chiel chaaao chieo said tein kied oieir teaeo keear
chiy qeiar qar seeen taaao kiel oer keiey tead
siay oir chiey chian seain chair seeel chael siil
chead qair

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy siio seial dael
saiid say kiel oiael say taar oiiid dael chail
chear oaer tad qaiy chair taaar oail saio daear
oir oaaan tied oeean sey taio oiil daear oaeo
chiaen tieaaio sieo daeaaar chieen kaiy chiy keeeo
chiiaad qaen qeir kaiin taio chial oeel daid kier
cher chaaao seeiid kian teaad chaen qeeid kael seao
chean sey tieeo chiaear kear cheear taaao seeiid
kian qar chair oaen tiaed qay kiieo oied tey
qeaao seiaid kiy qeal oen oeao sied tear seeiio
oen oeiey qeio kiaey chier qaeid dear cheaad chaen
qeaaed diiil cheil cheaar sier oaiiay

feao dio oeean qeeel deal cheial oiy tead oaeil
oial oieed oiy taaad cheel oair chair taaar oail
saio daear oir oaiio said ken oeiey qeio kiaey
seay oay tieen qiiio oaaeed tein qier oeao sieal
dear sar chair seaaey sar siiio oaaeed ken chiy
kir siiir deeead chaao chaid teao chaeiar tiey
chieeo oaiiy kir cheal siad oiin sier tian kied
seial dael saiid say qaiiy saao chaed oied qir
qar sail saeil chan chied qein taied chieeo oeo
oeean kiiieeail tiiad qiin cher chaid kiiiey oaen
seeid qiir daid teear

feao dio oeean qiiel daer cheeo oead kieo oail
oeeeo say kair qaair teeo saio oail chey kiel
chaed chen seio taal cheo chaao oed tey qar siiio
oaio chiiio oeeaey qad char tiair kaeir kair kaean
teed qiad chead qio char ter chaio oeail daid kier
cheey saial kian qar siiio sid taaey chian oir
oaaeen oaen sear qey taaal oaaaal tey qar seeel
dael oiiieeain seaiil tein keiey qeain sar char
qaial kian qein cher seal saiir chier taao chaid
teao chiel cheal oiy tiiil daieo daio deeeaaien
chier qid qiead chael sail saaiy qeo oieo oaaiy
cher cheal cheao oaaaid siio oen cheael qaen tier
qeaiil tein kir saeio chan siio seeid keal chin
siio chaey seio oaein

feao dio oeean qeeen tiaeo taio chaain oiiio deed
kiy ter saaao cheil oael char qein ter sail oeid
oin oaen qear tiaid taain kiid taed oied tao oer
keao oiiir taao saiar qar chio oaeil daaey chian
saiid dian teaeo keear chiy kaaaad oiad chaio dear
oir cheal siad dan oeeo siay oiel chiiaad kaaio
char ter chaeaaaao chaiin oid sir kaen tiaeo tiao
kiel chiien oir oeaiid dar tian sied sen oaio
chieel teeay cheeeo oaar qaao oieo chiey ched oer
kaear qeiar qeial dead sen oieey cheear tael
cheial saiir cher chael oaiin tail oeiy saaal oaead
din chaar oeeen qaay oeo said tiay chiy keil sal
kiiey sar seial cheeiy oaen chaid chin siiin qiir
cheaid chiel chaay diaeo taio chaain

feao dio oeean kaer qeeel deaal keid oiiey qeial
chael chieo siiil tad kaen qear teear kiio cheao
dao sail chey qiead chiiio oaiir oeal chaio chear
taio sied qeaio oiy keeo chial saaaio dieo seaiil
kied oer kair kaen teeo saaad qaad kiir tael
oaiin qaad tair kiir cheeo siay cheeeo oaad tey
tail oeaiid deeead chaao oan saay oiir oel qay
qaao chaed oied qaair kail chel tin saiiy chiy
kaeil oiiil oaiiy qaain qaao oiain sar oaen qieo
oead kieo oiair oao chieo cheey oiaiy kaer qar
oeay cheo oeel deal sail chey qar siead chiiied
oeen chian qaeid daaiy ted oer tiy qeiar qieo
saaar keeo cheaer ter chaio oeail daid kier chian
cheael qaen ter chaaad cheil oeal chin oaen oieil
tein qied sen sein kaio chieaal qaid sid kiy teey
chaied cheier oay teeo saaad qaad kiir kieey sey
tiael cheeel chieey teey oiaed daear oir chao cheal
chaio oaaaid siin sein oead oan chieo oaio dead
saaiy qaeid daaiy

feao dio oeean taao oeiy seien kaeil oaid oiiey
kiaed oiiey qear teear kiio cheao deal oiy tiiin
taio sied keid dieen oiio cheien oeil oied qeao
oed kiieo deeen chiiy oaaiar ter oao chieo cheey
chaen qieo oead kieo oiair chaeay qeir ter sio
siil chiel cheay chio seao saay died oair chaeal
qeir ter oeeo seao cheaid deeead chaan sein kiaed
oiiey qieo saaar keeo cheaer ter chiael oiiay oay
qaiil taed oiair chaeay ted oer kir oeao sieal
chead qair oiiy cheael qaen ter seay saay chaao
oeiy saaao chiid qay qied sen oaeo teao chieed oiy
teed qeio sio chad kiier tied oeean cheear qier
kead qaaaiy sear teear kiio siad chead teed qaar
qaiiy chiy kaeil oaid oan cheiad cheeay kir oaaeen
oiiy oair kay qiiil chiiiiy ter oed qeil oaieel
qaen tian oeiey qeio kiaey chiiin qel qay tao
cheal saiin cher sail oeid oin siaiy kieio qiier
sar oeo oiel oeao deeead chaan chier qied sen chaio
sieaad oal tey qeao chied cheeay teiad cheeay

feao dio oeean taao oeiy seien kiid tiaeao oier
qiio oiiio oieo sir qeeen keeao said oied qeao
chieo cheeay kiid tiaeao oier kir cheal siad dan
sein tier tiain qaad keid daid keo sir qar oaiy
siel oeao deeead chaan chiieio taair oieer diar
kir oeeed oiier saay oieio qiier oay qeeal oair
chier tiy keeel seeeel kiy ter chiael oiiay oay
qial chel qaaiy sieao chieen saiil taed cheeay
keiey tiy kaio dien oein oer keil chiaear ter sey
tael oil teeay oeao saal dear chaaao tan chaeay
qey ter chael oaiin qein oed teael qiel oan oeer
cheeo seao cheel dear oaen oaid oaaaiiean saair
qiiil chiiiil qay kaeaer chiy tao oeeiy chaier
tiaiar sey tiiel dear chaar oiiid daair cheey
oaiid chieed char kieer diar kir cheeel oied qen
chaio oiil daead sir taao oiiel char keeer chead
qaaaiy cheio taid qien oaeiad kaio daio chin saiiy
oiel sied kieel kay qar chial oaeo chio oeid
chiiied oeen cheer daid chin oiid tiaeao oier kied
chad kiier qaain tian tao oied tiaiy qiio cheeal
sir qar seeiin sear keeer oaao oeao oied qeo
oieo chaead kian qeied tar tieeo chaal oaiid oied
qaen ter chiiil cheil sin saad kaeieo sair qeeaeo
qiier siaiy kear

feao dio oeean qiiel daer oeeel chael seao deeeal
say kair qaair teeo saio oail chey qeeen qaad
kaeieo sair qar siead chiiied oeen chian keao
oiiir taao saiar qar siiio sid taaey sein kaio
seeid kael sear kead oan oiiio diieo oead qier
kiiy oaaeen oiel chiao oeel daeaad sir qear qey
tieeo chaal oaiid oier kaaan qiiio teal char qaial
kian tad kiao qeio say kir sir tial seaiil tein
teer oaay oiir oio sear qaial kian qar seeiin
oiir chael seio chaal oiaiid oer qiio chaey chail
oieeo siien tier taiy chaiio oeel deaad said tein
kaaan kaeied qir teeo saean chaid qiad chaaiay
qeaay teeed teed kiy taid qiad chaaiay kir cheal
siad dan sein tier tiain qaad keid daid keo
saaar taid chin chiain qial chel qaail say taieo
deeeo deeeaaien cheed tead kael oiiieeain saiid
chiiieeain oaio oaaaiiean oeead taiy saiiy chiy
teal seial daal dial oiir kao cheao chiiey sar
cheeo sid tiay oir oaaal oial cheeeaaien chiaiar
chaaao saaio oein ter cheal cheial oiiieeain oair
kaean taed oeeao say

feao dio oeean qiiel daer oiiio oaio deeal chey
keo oaaeed kier sail oeid oaaaiiean cheeeo chain
chiio siaiiir oeeeo chieo oaail say tair kay keo
oiel cheaao sair qar saair keo oiel cheaao saeo
qey kieey oiio oaead kian tail oeiy seaal keal
chin oir oeeed oial chead oiiieeain oair kaean
qiio siad chieed chead kian keair kair kaean taied
chaaio oiy ter chiiil cheil saaaiiean sieo saead
oiier oeaio chiiio diao tan cher oaiid chieed char
taeay qier keiey qeio kiaey chiiin teaal keid
daear oir saead chied oied qiel oan sieo sid
taael say kir seo oaio oiiy dian chair taaar oio
siil chiel cheal oiy ter oeal sean cheeeo chieo
oaail say kir seao oaiil kiiey sar seio sio
siier sar oiel chiael cheead chan saiao taair sey
qio chaaey chiiil daiel oied tear sar siiil chieo
oeeao chiiiin saiay ter oed qeil oaieel qaen teey
ched oer tiy qeiar qeeiin chaaar daaao seao chiiel
oiy tier kaear kead kiiieo oeio qin chieed deaad
sir qar seeel dael oen oiiy oeiey tair qar sial
chieen qiel chaey saay oear taid kiiiel oaeo chin
chaar dial oaeo chio oaeiy oein oead oan chad qaial
kiao keeay teed oaad keil oied qaeo qey keeeo
qail sid kiy ter oiiio cheo cheeeaaien chian chaao
oaio seeid keal chin

feao dio oeean qiiel daer oiead chiiio chaeo teieo
chey keeo cheel oaid say tiey sey kao cheao chiiey
chaid chin oaio chiiio oiaid oiao qiied oiy kaiio
qay taar oeiaid tar kaio siad chead kao saail
saiy chiel saiy oir saao oed qiiay saay sar sial
said qaaiy cheey oeal siay seaiid oeao cheiy cheal
cheied daead sir qiaiy kaid oaaaiiean oeead kaen
qeier qail oeaid chied oied qiel oiiey ter cheal
cheiay cheaad oaaaiiean sear tieeiy oiien qel qay
keao saal daaiy keeen tieo oaaed tiay saiiy saao
seied qaen kaio chieel teeay oir sir kieil tein
qaiid chiiieeain oid sir kay tiaer ter oeeo seao
cheel dear saiid chiiieeain oaaal oail saeil chey
qiaiy kieio qiier oeer cheal siay oaio oaaaiiean
oieil teio kaeo chin chaid qied teeeal say

feao dio oeean kay qeeel deal siad chied seao
deeeaaien seir taid qiad chaaiay kaaan qaao oiain
sied keid diieiy kaieo sir tiao qieo daear chiar
kiel oiiir kiid qay kir cheal siad deeeaaien cheey
chier tiain qaad keid daid keo saaar qar sail
saeiy saiid chiiieeain oaeaer oaear qaeid oieo
oaid oiel cheeao oiel oan saay sieaao deeeo oiel
oan oeail daid qiiio oiieo din oieel kaiin kieil
tein qier seaiil tein kair qaair qiiil chiiiil
qay qaiid chiiieeain oaio oaaaiiean oeo seil
oiiid tear oeead tail sen cher seay chaid chin saao
oiain saiil qaay died chen siian kiid kied qaen
qel qay kiel chaied chaaio char kead oan chaaan kir
oaeo oeo oeear oir oaaal cheail daid kier cher
cheeao deeeaaien oieed deeeaaien oeeo seio oaeo
taio daaiy taeay tied qein ker keil chiaead qay
kir oeaiio chaeael chin oaead kain oio siieo kied
qaen qieal chead taaao saaio cheal chin oir oead
kieo chiey cheey chier qeaiil tein kir cheal sied
qain deey chiaiar cheey chiao seian

feao dio oeean qiiel daer chaean tiao seian keaid
oeeiiy oay qail oeel daed oeao oeil cheal seeiid
qail sir qeeen kay keeo oaaed tial chan oiao
taaal tiaeir ter chaaad cheil oeal chin siiir oieo
saaar kieil tein kir oeaid deeead chaan saar daao
saiar qar sieaao deeeo oiel oan oeo oaiiad
deeead chaan chaeay ted oer tied oair chieeo cheeao
cheao chear seaan keiey tiy kir oeeel seao chaaio
char ter saaao chaaio sied tair qar oay teao oieo
dair qail oiieo dail sen siir oiel chaey sein
oeeo siay cheeeo oaar kaaeeo cheay chaid chin saio
cheao oier qeaid dio sid kiy qier kiier keeeo
chiael chaaal deeay

feao dio oeean kaer taaen teao cheeeo oao char
taeal tied qein tiiin qein kieo oeeao oao char
taeal tied qein qeir keein qiio seeid kaey sar
oaiy chian keao oiiir keeed oial oear ter chaied
oaaaid siin chaaar chiao taaal tiaeir ter sio
seaaed oiin sad char qey kaeir kir oeao sieal
chead qair oir oeeeo oed qiial oen oir oeeeo oieo
cheo cheeeaaien cheey chier tiao qein taeal teer qar
siiid dael chail sen oeeer daiel oied teay sar
cheiad chier chaeay kaeo teiay cheeeo dao saaaiiean
seeid qiir oeo chaieed daao say

feao dio oeean teao oieo dair qiaiiy tieeiy chier
kiier qaao oieo chiel say kaaan taaio said tieo
oaio oiiey qaad kaeieo sair qar oiel chaed
chiiieeain chian kair qeao oaaao chaeo qiien teed
qiad chead qio char ter chiiaan chael oil teeay
chaieo daio deeeaaien cheaal keid daear oeiey qiio
siean chir chiey chaeay qead deeeaaien chieeo oied
qaeo taain kir saao oed qiial saiir oead oiiey
ter chiiaao sin sear qel qay keeel seeeel kiy ter
chiiil cheil cheaar seaiiy qiaiy kear qiear kaiy
cheed diao keil oied qaen kieo daio chear taeay
qead deeeaaien cher chaieeo chiiio dail sen siey
oeir qey kaeir qeio kiaey cheear kiao kil tain
sar cheeo sid taael sin oear teear qail saio chan
chiear deear kao siad cheied char kiaid chiiey seaao
chieaal kian qar cheian oiir chead oan seaaed daead
oaaaao chiaid tein taier kead oan seio sio siier
cheaad oaaaiiean oiid daeaar oaaal oial oaaed
tial oeid daid tio oain chieo daio chear

piear kaiy cheed diao keil oied qaen kieo daio
chear taeay qead deeeaaien cher chaieeo chiiio dail
sen siey oeir qey kaeir qeio kiaey cheear kiao
kil tain sar cheeo sid taael sin oear teear qail
saio chan chiear deear kao siad cheied char kiaid
chiiey seaao chieaal kian qar cheian oiir chead oan
seaaed daead oaaaao chiaid tein taier kead oan
seio sio siier cheaad oaaaiiean oiid daeaar oaaal
oial oaaed tial oeid daid tio oain chieo daio
chear

feao dio oeean kaer qaad tied oied keid dead chey
qeeen qial said teieo siiio teal char ter cheeao
deeeaaien saar deaeo keear saao oiiid tey qar
oiel chaed chen oair kaean teed qiad chead qio
cheeer qiir oaain taaio oied chead keal cheeay qaad
kaeieo sair keeo sid teear ter sail oeid
oaaaiiean oiir chieer oied sen cher chaeaaaao chaiin
oeiey qiio siean chaid tiao keao cheal char ter
oeeo sial chaao oer taar deaad oaaaiiean oeeao
said oeao cheeo sen siir cheil oied kean chieo
chiiieeain oair qaair tieo chaid tin oiiy oaen
oeeao said oieo oeeao say taaid qiel oan cheaay
chaaar deal cheeeo chead qay taar oiead chaey sieo
chaid kied say kiid tiaeao oier taao oed qaair
kaer qaad kaeieo sair qar oiel chaed chen cheer oad
chio sir qiaiy kay kiiio deer taaar oail oaid
qeiaer

feao dio oeean kaer qiiio saead oial oaiin oiid
qaal deeeaaien chiiin qio chial chil tey qiiio
saead deien chair qaair qial oeaiil sen oaien
siian kiel sead chier sar saain deey saial kian
kiid dieo chaen keer deey oer kaiio sieo cher
teael qaen kiao qeio say tiar kiier qeao sead
oen siir oeo oaio oiiy cheeer cheaid oial sen
oieer diar kir oeaid deeead chaao sin cheeead
cheaer kieey sey kiao qiil daao cheed kiil
oaaaiiean oeer diiaan chieo chiiieeain cheeel oaair
qear qiey oio chead kael sail oeear oir chead
oiiil dair qein oead oan oal tar tael oil keao
oeeey oio siieo kier kir oiel chaaao seao chaaio
seeeen sar oail oaen siiio saaaio chiey chiiad
taar chaiy saeo taal cheeo oiid qaal deeeaaien cher
chiiad qaear oeiaio seiay daaao cheied chiaio
seiaen

feao dio oeean kay qail oiieo oaad taid tio
oaiio sieo cher qiel chaey seaao chieaal keal chin
seio taaio tey kay tieo seaay qar siiid dael
chaiy saiid chiiieeain cheael qaen tael oaiin teaad
oaaaiiean oeeao siad daaiy tieeen oiir chaaan
qaiid chiiieeain chaieo oaiin oieed deeeaaien
cheaal keaiil sen cher said oain cheeo cheeeao
oaaaiiean sein cher chiiaan chieo chiiieeain oair
kay qail oeeeo chiiiil qay kiier qaiid chiiieeain
oeid chaey chaaen qiiio oaio daal oier keil say
kaen oiein qeaaan cheer cheeo char tiael oiiay seao
deeeaaien chier tiain qeial cheeiy chao oeeiy chaier
keeo chad qail oeid oaaaiiean sear qeien kiid
taed oied tao oiiir qaaaid saiar teaay oay qaad
qaid tein taar deean qaad kaeieo sair qeeaeo
qiier sar saair keeao siad daaiy kaio oaiio kiy
teaal teey daao oain oeal oiy qeaal keay chail
oeiy chian chao saal deeeaaien seal oiy qiir
chaaeen oeeal cheeo sir qiail taao chaien oar saaio
oaaaiiean sein sey tael oil teeay cheed oeao dian

feao dio oeean kaer qaair qeeel chiiio cheo saeo
keao chier chiiin tiiil oaaao oio cheio qieo oair
oir oiieo chiiieeain cheer chay qial chel qaaiy
sieao diaid tein teal oiy kieed deeeaaien saar
chaaal oiil chaeier taar oiiid daair saar dead
oiiy der chiiil daiel oier teer oear keein taied
chaaio oiy tael oaiin qaad tair kiir chieed oein
tiao seian tieel qaen qiir oeo oaio oiiy oey
tael oil teeay seaiil tein kair kay qied til
qiel oier taier tier tiy kaiio sieo daid tiay
oir seo oaio oiiy cheeer chaaal deeeo dao oiao
taio dear siaiy kay qar saaio oaaaiiean oiir ched
qeil chiaead qay kir oaiio oeel daear cheal siil
chiel cheay siaiy kear

feao dio oeean tieao oer tiar kiier kaaal deeeo
dao oiao taio dear chaio oeo chaain tieey qiel
chaey chieao oer kaaal deeeo dao oiao taio dear
oail chey keer daaad cheil oeay oiir der chiiil
cheil saaaiiean oiir oead chaain oay qeaao siil
dao oier kir oiieo chiiieeain cheer dad tead oaaed
teeay keer oaid oaio dael sair oeao oier qiiir
dieo chied din seain sar oaiy chiy kiid tiaeao
oier kaaiao keear sier chead siil oaaaid siio char
ter chiiil cheil sin oiir daeay qead chaain

feao dio oiir qeir tead oead taed oaiid say tead
cheao chaeiad qaeo qay qiel chaey chiael tin seio
taaio kiid qay taao chiael cheil cheaad chey qar
siiid dael chaiy siiir oar seaao chead kian qear
keeer dead cheao chaeiar keil seaan qaar daaio
oeid oiiey qeeaer kir oaaeen cheer oear qey taid
kal tail sair qar seeel dael oiiieeain oeiey tiy
keao oael siil chead qair oir oaeo siiid daair

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sieal chead qaiy
chied oaiir chiaid taain kied siad chieed chear
qieal chead qaiy chiel say kaiiil say taar oeeen
teied chael seao cheieed saail saaaiiean said chaed
oaid sio oaead kael char ter saaal chead tiien
oiiy oair kaen qaid chaed oaiid oier taar dar
tiaead ker kiel chiao sen chaaad cheil oeal chin
chaar deio tied oeeao say teer dieo sir kaid
oaaaiiean

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey taio oead
qair saaain oead qel qay taid qieo cheal char
teaal kaid qeeaey keer diiaan cheey oiiien chair
kal kiy tiao chao chaair oao seial chiiin dio saio
dain sar chail oaeil deeen oeio kiael oan sar
sial chey qein oaiid cheear cheeel oied qen siir
chaaal oial cheeeaaien chian saiid dian kair kaen
kead taio oiel oer kir oiieo chiiieeain saiid
dian tian kaeo oeo oeear seao oer qeain oeer
oaaal chead kan sein chaio oead qair

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy chieo oiaid oaiid
dan seir kiio cheieo chey qeeel oeiey qiir diel
sin sial chair kiel chaed deeead chaan oiio cheien
oiid taal chiey chieeid oaaan deear kaeo teial chin
chiy qeaad oiied oiy tier tiy qeaad oaiid oer
kiiy chaaio chieed say kaiiin oiel chian oiid taed
oied kaaial tan chael oiy keal siay oair kaiil
tin chead siay oeeed chied say taar dieed dieen
chier tied chen siian qeaad oaiid oer qier oead
taed oieo oiieo oier qeao sied keier siiid oair
oair qear qey kaeid tan oaiid chieed char qaaal
chiier tead siay oir oiien oear taio cheeer qey
tael oil teeay sid tial oaeied tey keer diiin
kiiio dian qeao oer tied chiiieeain oir saeo qaal
chean cheey oeal siay oair kail cheay chaaar chaio
oaid daear seain oied chaaid say taar oeeen qaay
chiao keel dair tieo sir kaid oaaaiiean oaid
diien chiy kaiio seaal qean daid qied teeeay cher
chaied oaaaid siin saay cheiad dio oiiey said
saiar keer dian chead siay chead seao oieo oiiey
keer diiin qaid oeil oiiien oaio cheao oier keiey
kid sir qar oay kieiy tad kiao qeio oaein keeeo
qail sir qar oay qiiio oead kiaio sean cheieeeed
tial sid qier kiid taal cheo saaaid oieo din oir
oiid taal cheo saaaid oieo din oiel oiao kiiy chao
siieo teal chad keao oier kaen oieil tein qaao
oaeo qiaid oer tiy qeiar qial chieen qiel chaey
saay seiaid qein chaied deay seeel chao chieo chair
keer diiin keeer cheeeo chiiaad qaen qaaal chiier
kir seo oaio oiiy daaad cheiy chier qiiir dieo
chied oiair oeeer oiel chaey chiean chaied deay
seeel oaid oeid diieiy tael cheeer tian saiid
dian tead seao oieo chao saaiy siir chiid kieo
oieo cheal siiy chaen oieil tein kair taaen teao
oieo daid kiy kiio saad qeiy said cheeeao oaid
say taar oaad keel oail oeey dian oiel oiael
seaan tier kaead qir qiiil chiiiiy teey chian
saeid cheil oaen keead tail sen siir chiid kieo
oieo cheal siiy chair qeeel chael oaeay kaen teeo
saean chael oiiien cheal siay chieel qaeo kan oaiil
tail oeiy cheear taaao sied qaio dael oiy tiiil
diaiy kair kal tar qio seeiid keen oeer deed
qiil deal siiy oiiir cheo oaaeed kier oaien oaaal
dial oeel daid kied say qier oeieeeed tial sir
qaad keel oio cheeead saio chan sar saad keel oio
cheeead saio chan sail saeo qaay dian tao oied
qeil cheeal chey teeo saean seal chio oeiy oeao
oier teael qaen taeay tied qeio qay tao chiiaad
tead oiy tiair kao cheao diaid tein tieel qaeo
keal siay sieo dair teaad chaen teiad cheeay kaaal
chaeay keer chiaed daear oair qead tiaid taain
kiieo deeeo oeil oier kieel qiad cheied oen oiiy
chao oied qeil cheeay saeid cheier saad keel oail
oeey daieeo chiiio chaen taid cheeay kaer kay tiaer
taeay qead deeeaaien cheed qiil deal siiy oeio
taaio kiid qay taiy deeeaaiel tieaal kiao kiy
ter chiiaan sein kiio cheien oair keay oied kaid
tail saaiy tian qiel chaey cheiad cheeay tieel qen
cher chaieeo chiiio dail sen chieo oiaid oiao qiied
sean oaeo taed oaid oer kair keay oied kaid tail
saaiy qaao oed teal seial daair oeal siay chaiy
chead tied qiel oan chair oaean kao cheao chiiey
oeer dieeo saal deeeaaien chier kair qaay siad
chaaio chear kieen qey ter cheeao deeeaaien chian
oiel oiael say kiiy siel chaey cheaad chaen qeeel
daied oieo oair oeaid chiey cher chael oear tian
saiid dian kaaiar tiieil taal oien oeaid oiair
siir chaaan kaen chieo sir kaid oaaaiiean oaen keo
said kaeay teeo saean seal chin siel chaey oieil
tein kaaal chaeay qial chair keay oiiio oead kieo
oiay saao oeao dar tael siad chied oiy tiiad qen
chiiaay siir oeo oaio oiiy oey tael oil teeay
oeao chiiad kead kiiieo oiel oan siaiy kaid diien
oir saein sieo daid ken chead siay oaeo chiaen

peaal keal siay oiier keeeo chaid teao cheil say
kaaal chaeay qear qel ker tao chiiaad qiin siir
chiid taal chiel chaay daaar oio seeiid qair seain
seeel chael siil chead qaid chey tair siaiiiid kael
char teed qiil dao oiiied cheao oen cher cheed qiil
dao oiiied cheao oen cheal cheio teey chaen keo said
tial oiiel oiy kiio cheien chiel oao siay sieo
dair kieil tein keiey kaid tiao tey keo oaaeed
kied say

shiair kao cheao diaid tein tieel qaeo keal siay
sieo dair teaad chaen teiad cheeay kaaal chaeay
keer chiaed daear oair qead tiaid taain kiieo
deeeo oeil oier kieel qiad cheied oen oiiy chao
oied qeil cheeay saeid cheier saad keel oail oeey
daieeo chiiio chaen taid cheeay kaer kay tiaer
taeay qead deeeaaien cheed qiil deal siiy oeio
taaio kiid qay taiy deeeaaiel tieaal kiao kiy
ter chiiaan sein kiio cheien oair keay oied kaid
tail saaiy tian qiel chaey cheiad cheeay tieel qen
cher chaieeo chiiio dail sen chieo oiaid oiao qiied
sean oaeo taed oaid oer kair keay oied kaid tail
saaiy qaao oed teal seial daair oeal siay chaiy
chead tied qiel oan chair oaean kao cheao chiiey
oeer dieeo saal deeeaaien chier kair qaay siad
chaaio chear kieen qey ter cheeao deeeaaien chian
oiel oiael say kiiy siel chaey cheaad chaen qeeel
daied oieo oair oeaid chiey cher chael oear tian
saiid dian kaaiar tiieil taal oien oeaid oiair
siir chaaan kaen chieo sir kaid oaaaiiean oaen keo
said kaeay teeo saean seal chin siel chaey oieil
tein kaaal chaeay qial chair keay oiiio oead kieo
oiay saao oeao dar tael siad chied oiy tiiad qen
chiiaay siir oeo oaio oiiy oey tael oil teeay
oeao chiiad kead kiiieo oiel oan siaiy kaid diien
oir saein sieo daid ken chead siay oaeo chiaen

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy tiair
teaal taid kier seir qiio seial deal siiil cheaad
chey keer diiin qiey daio sied keid deaad chey
qaid chiael cheil cheaad chey keay dail oeiy chiiil
cheaid chaeay qaad qaio daio oiey sier kaear keeeo
oeeel kaaio oied cheeal chaan siir chaaal oial
cheeeaaien sey kaio seeid kaey saiil qied tear
saad qaiil sair taaan keil sier teer deaad chaen
keeao siad daaiy tiieir keer daaad cheear chian
qiiin kaien oir seaad oiier seier qial chel qaaiy
sieao oaaao oaaaiiean saiil taed oiair chaeay qey
keal seao oaaaid siin sein said oeiy oeeer chied
seial dael saiid say kaiio saaar taaar oaaaiid
miiieeo ckheaiiey deeao deeeaaien oeal siay seaad
daio diao qaeo taair oeeer oeao chaed oail oeeeo
oier taiy chiieo chiiieeain chied oair oeeer oeiad
kiy tieeo chiaear keeer chiid taal chiel chaay chiel
chaed oaiid say keil sier kaen cheaad chaen keeao
chio oeid deaad chey qeaaan cheer diiin teey cheaal
keid dael siair cher sein keeen qar oiel chiael
oaaao dead chan oear sey kaeir teaeo kier seaaan
oein said oeiy sieao chieen oeer deed qeeeen chair
oaean teaal taid keeay qiiin qiir oeo oaio oiiy
dian saiid dian kair kay tieaad dael oiiieeain
oaiio saio diieo sair tio oeeao qail sid taio
chear tian seain chair saad keel oio cheeead sain
der oear cheed qiil dao oiiied chean chiel oiao
kiiy chear chaeaaaad qeil oer kiiy oaen chiao qieo
chan oied chaey sied qaio deaar chaaid qiel oan
oaiy oeieeeer kay keeao chio oaaed tin sier cheeeo
oeeao chaid kear qiir chaaan tiy taaio oied siil
oeil sair oieel qeao cheied chear teey oiio chial
sir qaay chieo oaio diieo chey qeial dead sen
chieeen chieer cheaiio oaaao cheal chin oaear qaeo
taed oaid oier teaeo kier saay saiil tieo oiaid
oier taar dio seao saay chaear qaeo taed oeeao
oier teaeo kier saay siead diieo oaio daeaad
sair qaay saiil tieo dio saen taed chaeay qiaiy
teeo seil oer kir oeaiid dar tian oeeeo chaal
oied teal siaiy keer diiio teer taaar dead oaeil
oial oieed oiy tieeo oiiir kir chiey oeal chaio
chear tiair teal cheao cheeeo chiao seian qeair chiel
chieo deeead chaan oeeer chied seial dael saiid say
kaiio saaar qiaiy kay taaar cheal siay oeao chaaio
qaeil sen chaied deay seeiin sein sead chaain oay
qiiio saeo taed cheo oeeed oiay oaiio oeel deaad
sair taaar chaio siad chieed chear qead chaain cheeo
oiid kaey oeay oail oeid deaad said kiy qiir
chaaal oial chan cheey siieo chaed cheeay keiey tiy
kiiy siel chaey oeeao siad daaiy taied deay sear
keein taied chaaio oiy kiao kiil dain saay saad
tair tail sieo dair tair cheiel chien oiaiy kay
qieo oaeil deeeo chaain oeer dead oeel deed tiay
oaen seeid qiir cheao sied qiin sead chaain oaen
tio saean seal oiel chaay oein saial kian kied
chaey seio oaein tiiil diaiy

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey kaio
siil dey qaay oaid diien siian qeo oiain oeeo
oead kien cheer diiil chain chieen ter oao siad
cheier saay chieo oeel oay kiiy oeieeeer qaair
qaid chaid teao chaaiay keao sied tear chial said
qeao oiiay oiiil deear keal siay oaen saay chail
oeiy chian saeay chaao sid qeial diiaay chiiin qein
tead oead taed oail oaain cheaao tail saaiy tied
oaiid oer kiiy sir teeo oeid dair qieo chaid kier
oael chead tieo daaey oiio saad qeiy sein tead
oead taed oail oiel oier tiey cheey chaeaay kiier
qeao oieey sail oaio dien deaay seao cheieey tieo
oeel oay kiier qaiir cheeo oead kieo din saeid
cheier chail oeiy chian oied oiil chiao tein qeain
sar chial oaio diiil cheil saaaiiean siad chaaio
chin sier oaiiay oeil sier kaen cheiad cheeay
kaiiin daar dio seao saay cheieeeer qaid chaed
oaid sio oaead kael char tier tian qaead daaey
cher oaio cheaay saay saad tair qein oieed oaair
keo seio kied qaen taaao siil oir qiiid oair
chaar oaid chaid teao oen chian saiid dian tied
oeean oeein kao cheien oir oaaeey chao oied tead
oiiio deer qeao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oay keo
oaaeed kier seay cheaar chiaid diail qeo cheeay
saao oiain cheeo seio taead sir qeeel daeaad sen
siir chaaan qiel chaey saad kaio daear saiir oiiid
dael chin siel chaey chaaar chiio cheien oaid tiao
tey qial said qaid oer kiieo deeen oiel chaey oeo
oaaeed kied say tieel qen cher oeeer chied oieir
qaao oiaio chear keeer oiel chaey cheeo seio qiiey
kaid diien siiir oeaao siil chieed chear tieeel
oiy qeaal kan chaaar daaad cheil oeal chin oair
teio tied oiaid oed keean taaan qeain sar seeen
kay qaid chaed oaid sio oaead kael char ter oao
chieo cheey oear teeal saeo taaid sair saao oiaio
chear kieey chiair kiiid qeil oiiieo chieed oiy ter
chael oear keal siay siiir deeo saeao oier qiiir
dail oeiy sio chaaal qaiy chieel qen sio seeiid
qaid chey taaar cheal siay oaaeeo cheay oaid diien
siiir oaid saiar taaar chao siieo teal chad keao
oier qier oeieeeed tial sir qar chair saad keel
oio cheeead saio chan sail saeo qaay oiiir cheo
oaaeed kier cher chiiil cheil saaaiiean chaaar oio
seeiid qair oied seeid kan sial chair qein siieo
oao seao cheier oeer deed teao chean cheed kead qin
oieil tein qeao oed kead qay keeeo chaal oier
keeed chied say kaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey teal
cheial oil qin cheaar chiao qiieo kael chio oiiey
cheeo oeid diaeir tier tao chied qiel oan oeer
diiil chain chieen ter chaaad dear oay qaad keid
daid keo saaar kaid diien oaear kied oiil cheal
chin oir oaaan qaiiy oiid qied kiiey cheeean chieey
oeal siay oaen oail chey kaiil tin chaar oeeen
qeaaed daead oaaaao chiaid tein kir chao chiiad
keel oier kir oeaid deeead chaan sein tead cheaer
qiir chaaan qiiio teal char taeay ted cheear ter
oao siieo teal chad keao oier kaen sad char qiey
chieo daio chear tiiin keal siay chiy qeao oaieel
tear oay qaid oaeo qiad dao cheal chear kieey sey
taaao oaid oaiy oeil chiiiiy teey chieeiy chaied
oieo cheeir oaaeey oair taaen qaad keid daid keo
saaar keer daaad dear saay said oaeo qiad dao
cheal chear taeay qey ter chiiil cheil saaaiiean
chaaar chaiin taeay qead chaain chail oaio chiiied
oeen oaen chair oal tar taied chaaio seao cheieed
seao oaaaid siin sar cheiad oiair chian oied oiil
chian tiean cher chael oear tian oiel oiael say
kaaeey chaar oeeen tail oeiy sel qay kead qead
chied oiy kiio siad chaeiar tead seao cheel dear
oeieel qaay sail oaaed qeiaer tiean cher cheey sio
sial chaid kied oier kir oiiy oeeeo oiao qiil
chaeiar kir oiiy seao sead chiel cheay sieo chaid
kier oael chead tieo daaey siir oaeid cheier chail
oeiy sein kiio siad chaeiar kir seil chieo oeeel
dael oiiieeain oaid tian qier oaiiay oir oaaan
kied siad chied oeo chiaid tial sir teaar chao chieo
cheey oear qein kiio siad chaeiar keil sier kaen
seeel chiaed oiiey qeaaan oear teear taio sied
keid oaaaiiean cheaay oay qail oaaaay kiier tiel
oan chail oeiy chaio siieo kiael say keil saaaio
cheel oeied qaen keal siay chieel qen cher said
oaio daal oair chaeaay kiiy oiel chiiad kaeiar
teeel oaair teeo seil oer tian qaao chaed oeiaer
keer diiaay oer teeel oaair qied tied oair chiiin
keal siay oiiy oiel oiael say kir oeaiio oaaao
cheay sein seir qey kaeir kiiy oied chiao keid oio
sail sair qeeen tieo sir kiiil deear qead chieer
saay chail oeiy oieen qey taied deay seiaed qeao
cheao oan sar siiid oair chian qaao chaed oeiaer
kear qiey oail saio daear oir seo chaaio kiel oed
qieo dair qein saiao tear chiy qeiad qier qier
chiain tieen qeeeed daead sir qaay sein cheal
cheial oiy qiied keal chin saein oaen siead cheao
chiiieeain chead siay oaeo chiaen

feao dio oeean kay qiiio oiieo chaen kaer qaid
oieey chaaao cheio qiiil qeiaer qeeen taaar chiao
taaal tiaeir ter saial kian qeiaey qar chio seao
saay oiiir dail oead qaal oiiar cheaar oeir ter
chaied oaaaid siin oiir oead deal char teed teao
chieed oiy tead cheaer taied deal siiy oaeay chaaao
kiel oer taar oeeen qiad chiel cheal seeao oier
kir oeaid deeead chaan cheey chaeay ted oer kir
oaeiey chieen qiaiy kear ter chiiil oeil chaal oied
tear chair siaiiiid kael char taaal oaio diieo sir
kiiio diieo oead qier kir chiey chaid qiad oiel
chied qaio daio cheio kair kaer keaio chiiio oaiid
chaain kiid kied qeiy chiiin tiieio taair chaeay
qaid oeiy said oeiy cheear teal siad oiel oian
qar sial oaaaay taiy oeio keeey kaaay chail sen
chiieir keer daied oieo oaaaiiean saay siad chaaio
seao cheieed chear taio cheeer qaial kian kied chiao
seo chiael cheead chey taaar daaao seeiid kian kaid
diien siiir oail oeid cheal chear teed qeeiir keal
chaio oaaaid siio char kieed oein qel qin chieo
oaaed kaeay qiiio oaid qieo sir qar seaao seao
cheil saaaiiean sear teey chaed oeeao chieo oiaiid
oier kir oiieo chiiieeain cheael qaen teal cheial
oiy ter oiaen saar chaaed keer daied oaaao
oaaaiiean oied chien oeio oaein kear said oeel
chiiied oeeo sir

feao dio oeean qiiio oiieo dail sair kaer teeo
saean chaid qiad chaaiay kaaan kaeied qir qaaio
chiiin deeo saean chaid qiad chaaiay kaaed kieir
kiel oiiid tiaiy kead taed oeeaey teiey cher sail
oeid cheen oeay chieil tein kair kaean teed qiad
chead qio cheeer teey oer teeel oied qen oieey sey
qail oead kieo oial chiaiy qeo oieo oaaiy cher
sail oeid cheen siaiy qad chad kaen kay qial chel
qaaiy sieao oaaao oaaaiiean saiil taed oiair cher
sail oaeil oiier sein saiao tear chiy kaail chier
sieo seil chaal oied tear chiean oear chaeaaaad
qeil oer teeeo oeeao chaid kear qaid oeieeeed tad
taid qied kaid keeay tiey cheal siad oiel chien
oaad kaaer kaer keaio deaao seian kiid kied qeiy
cher oiao kaid daead sir tieed deaad sir qar oeo
chieer oeeer cheeeo chiiaar keil sier qiel chaey
chaaar deal siad oiiao kaaer qiead oaiy oeao
chaaio qaeil sen cher cheear taed oeeao say teael
qaen tiiil cheaid daio dead chin oaaeey chaar chiel
chiael cheear siiir oiiio oaaeed tein tied oeean
cheal siad oiel chien oaad kaeier kir oaiio cheel
chiiieeain cheer oear qein taed oeeao say kir
oiieo chiiieeain oiel oiael say keil sier teer
diao qiiiy qail saeil chey keo oaid taiy chaar
deian siead cheao chiiieeain chead siay oaeo chiaen
tair siiel chiiied oeeo sir

feao dio oeean kaer qeial chaey chieo oeel oay
kair qaair teeo saio oail chey qeeen qieo oead
taed chiiieeain saao oiain sial said teio keead
sir qaaio chiiin daaar oio sead keay oaen tiao
keel dair tieo oaio dead saaiy qeio keao din oir
seo oaio oiiy cheeer deal siil chiel cheal oiy ter
oeeer cheao chaid teao cheier cheaar oeir ter chaied
oaaaid siin chaaar chiaed oiiey qieo saaar qaid
saiar qar sieo seil chaal oied tear chaaar chear
chaeaaaad qeil oer teeeo oeeao chaid kear ter
chaaal oaio diieo sir qar chial oaeo said oieo
daio diao qaeo taair sio oaiir chiy keil sal
kiiey oeaio cheao chiiieeain oieel qiad cheil oan
chieo saeil chaan chaeay ted oer kied oieir kiien
dail sieo dair qieo oead kaieo chey kied oael
cheeean chaio chieaal qaid sair qial chair kiir oaid
oeiy saaao chiid qay qeo sead chied chin oiiy cheer
chay qiiio oeid daid qaad kiir qeal oiy kieey
chiael oeil oaio siil dey qar chio seao saay dian
qaeid daaiy taeay qey ter cheal sead keay chaaar
chaio chieaal qaid sir kieiy tair oal tar taio
oiil oiiey keo seio taal dar tieo dio saaaid
chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaer taaen keo said tial oiiel
oiy kiaed oiiey qear teear kiio cheao deal oiy
kaeiy teed kear kaeo qiieo teed kiy teed qiad
chead tead chey qiead diiaay cheed kead qin saad
tied oied qaeil chey qar chiaey keay oiiio oaaeed
teio tey tair chaaao kiel oer kir sier oiid
tiaeao oier kied oieir kaaal oiead chiiied oeeo
sen saiao tear chiy qeiar qar oal kiy qio sail
sieo oaaaiiean oeeao chio oeid deaad chey qar
cheiad cheeay qeil chieo dio oead kieo oial oiel
cheal siiin cheer dair chieaal kian qiad oiey oear
chieeiy chieo dio saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kay tead taaio tel tin chiar qaao
oiaio oiy tier kiier qaao oieo chiel say kaaan
keao chaid qiad deeeaaien oiio cheien oeil oied
keead sir qaay oeo oaid tail sen cher cheeao
deeeaaien saar chaeiay tied oeean oaio seeid
qeiey qieao chean oao chieo oeiy oeeer deal siil
chiel cheay sar sieo oead kieo oiair chiaear kieer
cheel chaiiy kir oiien dail sieo daid kiiieeain
cheael qaen qail oeid deaad chey qear tiaid taain
kiid taed oied tao oiiir kaaan tiy qeil chiao
oied cheao oieo oein taied oieo chan cheied oaid
said teeay qar seial cheeiy oiiy oeiey kid sir
qeeen qieo cheel cheeay qiiin kaiid cheear siiir
dieo chied diaen tiao qaio deaad chey kaer tad
qail chey kieil tein kair taaen taaan kir oiieo
chiiieeain chaiy oaiid dian qeil chiao oeel daeaad
sir qeiaey qeeen kay qial chel qaaiy oaio chieel
teeay oir chiy keil sal kiiey seio chaaad cheear
oir siiin kieey oiio oaead kian tail oeear seil
chieo oeeil chaeo teeead chaao oan saay chail oeiy
chiieir qeeael qiil chaaiay keaid dial cheay sar
chio oeeao qail sid taio chear teey saiao tear chiy
qeiar qar oiel chiael oieo oaaaiiean oieio qair
sead chaain saay seiaid tar keal chaio oaaaid siio
seeiiy seeen teaad chaen tiao qaio diaeir taal
chaear teed tar kieed oein teal siiio seao chieo
oeeao oer kir oeieeeed tial sid tar ter seay
saay oeo said tiay oair qaay chead taaio tel tin
chied oeean sey kiaed cheao dio oaio daey seaao
siil chieed chear ter chieeey cheeo seil oer qeio
kiael oan oiel say kir oeaiio oaaao cheal chin
oaen cheaad chaen qar sial chair tail oeiy chian
chead siay oaeo chiaeo tian tieel qen chiiil diaiy
taar deian sein chead oaaed tin sier chead siil
oaaaid siio char qiear tair chaao oaid teao chiid
taed oiaiiiin der sio oaiir

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey
tiiil daieo oiied oier chier kiier qaao oieo chiel
say taar oeeen teeo saean chaid qiad chaaiay tiar
kaeied qir qaaio chiiin deeo saeao say tian tiar
kiel oed tiaiy qeio keao din oir oiien cheay cheo
said tial oiiel siien kiiied oieir teal siad
chieed oiy teey chian cheeel oied qiel oan cheaay
oay kiel chaid teao cheil seiey kao cheao chiiey
seeil chiied kain der oao chieo cheey chay qial chel
qaaiy sieao oaaao oaaaiiean saad qaid tial chan
chaeay ted oer kir chao seaar chial oaeo said oieo
daio diao keaid daio oen sail oaeil oiier sein
saiao tear oaid qieo chaair teeeo oeeao chaid
qeeeey kied chaeaal kiid ked kead tied taaiy teey
oao chieo cheey oiaiy kay kaiy oiel chaed dail oain
deed qeeid qey qeir kao cheao chiiey oiel chiael
cheear oiiy oeiey tied oeean cher cheal cheiay saay
sear qeien keid diieo said teio tiy kaaan teaey
taaio oied chead kiiey teed qeiaio chear qaiay
qaiao tear saeo tiao chey qar sial sair taaar
cheeeo qail sir kieil tein kair taaan kiiy cheer
chiid chaeo taael oied qaen qiied qein ter chael
oear keal siay chaiy cheeeo cheeao oiel oier tead
siay oaeo chiaen teiao oiey siir oeil chiao seo
chieo deeead chaan saar oier chian kiid tiaeao oier
kir cheal sied qain cheeer oiiio teal char taeay
qead chaain sar cheian sein chaied oieo oaaaiiean
oied chaey seio oaein

feao dio oeean kay keeao seeiid keeo chio oain
deed qeiaio chear tiieio taair seal oiy tiiin
teed qeiaio chear qey tiieil taal oeeaey qar
saain cheeer dead siay oiid tiiar teael qaen qail
oeid cheeid chey qear qey teed qiad chead qio cheeer
ter cheey chaied cheil cheaad chey qail oiieo diaen
kay tiao qaeil sen siear kay tio saio dain oieiy
qeeel chaiid oieo chiiied oeeo sen chiieir qear
qeien qeaad oaiid oer taier keeen tiao keey
deeao deeeaaien oeeer cheaid oial seian kiel
cheeao oeian kaeiay kir cheal seial daair seio
kaen qaad kaeieo sair qar siead dael chiaen qail
oiieo diaen tair saair qaiil qaay oier chiy qeiar
kieio qair cher seay sieo saaar kay qieo cheel
oiaid chien dail sieo chiiied oeeo oain deed
qeiaio chear tiieio taair cher oao chieo cheey oaad
keel oio cheeead sain deal cheio teel dail sen
siieo oao siad chieed oiy ter said oeieel tad
taid qead qiiey qeied ken saiiy saao seied qaen
kieey cheear kaeid tey qial chair taaar deed
qeiaio chear tiieio taair oaien oaaeey chaaio oeid
chiiey qiir cheao oael siil chead qair oaen chiean
oear cheear teed qiil dao oiiied chean chiel oiao
kiil cheal chin oir sied oiier tead siaiil ked
kead tied taaiy ter oao siieo teal chad keao oied
kieen oieil tein teaeo kier oay tiaer ter chiiil
cheil saaaiiean oaid diien oaear qaeid oieo daead
oaio daey seaao siil chieed chear ter cheeao
deeeaaien oiio chial sir tiao qaeil sen sail chey
kaiiy tail oeiy oieed oein taio chad kiier kiiy
oaen sied keid daear oaiiin daar oeeen tail
oieeo sean oeiar qar cheeo sid taaey sio chain
oeao chiiad qeil chien diean cher chiiil cheil
saaaiiean oeay cheo said tial oiiey chial oaeo
siil cheieed char qead chaain oay qeaao siil chieed
chear qeir qein qio chead qeiey teed teao cheao
sail oeey oaiay qeal oiy tead seaiil tein kid
sir qiiil oeao oeeao oiiid qaeo tied kean seir
teear qieao chieeo chiaen qaad kaeieo sair qeeaeo
qiier saad qaio daio oiel chaay chieiy qieo oeeao
teial oiy qey kaio seao chied cheeay qear qel ker
taaio oaaeed kied oiiey teed qeiaio chear tiieio
taair chiiin qey kaio seao chied cheeay qaair teiey
said oeiy chaio sied keid oaaaiiean seaaan oar
sieaao oeieil oan sear tieeid oaaan cheein taied
cheil cheal saiy chied oeean seay chail sieo dair
kay tieo seaiil kier oieer oier siel chaey siaiiy
keeer cheaid oial oeier seiaed teao chieed oiy
teed teao cheao saiy oir chead seao cheel dear
oeieey taaar chaio oaid daear seain sail oieen
oaid diien chian tead cheao chaeiar kiiy cheer oieo
chaid kier oael chead tieo daael chieo oean chir
oiieo chiiieeain chian qaeid daaiy tiaead qay
kaiio seaay teaar oeir ter said oaio daal oair
siey daied cheil chaear cheeel oaair teeo seil oer
kir chiy keao chaid qiad dao chaaar cheeel seeeey
tair cheeeal oeeil chaeo qien seaad oaiid oer
kaaeen chaiy oeao sead cheier siaiy kay tair siead
dael chial sen oear chiaear teey saial kian teeeal
dail sen cher sio oaiir oeil sier teer chieiy
keeao siad oaaiy kaaeey chaar oaaio oaaaiiean
oiir died kiier keeel seeeey kiel sier qaiiy
saeo qied kean cheal saiin cheey oaiid chieed char
taeay qier qid qaid chiiad qen siir chaaan kaen
cheaad chaen tieo sir kaid oaaaiiean sar oaiy seao
oaieel qiio oer kair kay qiead diieo chey qeeiiy
saay oiir dieo chaeay tieo sir kaid oaaaiiean
sieo chieen tiey sein taied chaaio oiy taeay qaid
oeiy cheey oer keiey kaid tian taaio saaiy kiid
kieo oien oir oaaal oial cheeeaaien siey oiieo
chiiaad tear saad kaeieo sair qeeaeo qiier sear
qiey chaio oaid chan sar siiio oaaeed tein taiy
dead siay oir oeeed chier sein taied chaaio oiy
kaien siian taieo oeeey der cheeao deeeaaien sein
kaio seao chied cheeay qiaiy qear qail oaaaay kir
oeil say qir keeao chio oaeieo chey qeeiiy oeer
daieeo diaial sen chier tead siay oaiio oaio
cheeil chey qar seeiin saay siead dael saiy cheal
siil oaaaid siio chear taeay qaid oeiy cher oiaen
chied qein qaid oeiy chiao seian qier chead siil
oaaaid siio char

peir teear qieao chieeo chiaen qaad kaeieo sair
qeeaeo qiier saad qaio daio oiel chaay chieiy qieo
oeeao teial oiy qey kaio seao chied cheeay qear
qel ker taaio oaaeed kied oiiey teed qeiaio chear
tiieio taair chiiin qey kaio seao chied cheeay
qaair teiey said oeiy chaio sied keid oaaaiiean
seaaan oar sieaao oeieil oan sear tieeid oaaan
cheein taied cheil cheal saiy chied oeean seay chail
sieo dair kay tieo seaiil kier oieer oier siel
chaey siaiiy keeer cheaid oial oeier seiaed teao
chieed oiy teed teao cheao saiy oir chead seao cheel
dear oeieey taaar chaio oaid daear seain sail
oieen oaid diien chian tead cheao chaeiar kiiy cheer
oieo chaid kier oael chead tieo daael chieo oean
chir oiieo chiiieeain chian qaeid daaiy tiaead qay
kaiio seaay teaar oeir ter said oaio daal oair
siey daied cheil chaear cheeel oaair teeo seil oer
kir chiy keao chaid qiad dao chaaar cheeel seeeey
tair cheeeal oeeil chaeo qien seaad oaiid oer
kaaeen chaiy oeao sead cheier siaiy kay tair siead
dael chial sen oear chiaear teey saial kian teeeal
dail sen cher sio oaiir oeil sier teer chieiy
keeao siad oaaiy kaaeey chaar oaaio oaaaiiean
oiir died kiier keeel seeeey kiel sier qaiiy
saeo qied kean cheal saiin cheey oaiid chieed char
taeay qier qid qaid chiiad qen siir chaaan kaen
cheaad chaen tieo sir kaid oaaaiiean sar oaiy seao
oaieel qiio oer kair kay qiead diieo chey qeeiiy
saay oiir dieo chaeay tieo sir kaid oaaaiiean
sieo chieen tiey sein taied chaaio oiy taeay qaid
oeiy cheey oer keiey kaid tian taaio saaiy kiid
kieo oien oir oaaal oial cheeeaaien siey oiieo
chiiaad tear saad kaeieo sair qeeaeo qiier sear
qiey chaio oaid chan sar siiio oaaeed tein taiy
dead siay oir oeeed chier sein taied chaaio oiy
kaien siian taieo oeeey der cheeao deeeaaien sein
kaio seao chied cheeay qiaiy qear qail oaaaay kir
oeil say qir keeao chio oaeieo chey qeeiiy oeer
daieeo diaial sen chier tead siay oaiio oaio
cheeil chey qar seeiin saay siead dael saiy cheal
siil oaaaid siio chear taeay qaid oeiy cher oiaen
chied qein qaid oeiy chiao seian qier chead siil
oaaaid siio char

feao dio oeean kay qieo oaaed qied qaaaio dail
sen seir qio cheied dead seo chaain kaiiad daaen
qaain cheal chaio chear qeal oiy qey qiio sied tied
chier cheey chiial oaaid qean chaiiin cheil sier kiid
qier kiiy chied oeean sein teal siad oiian keeed
chier cheey chian oaeiad kaio daair sead chaain oiel
chieo oean chiiy oiier kiel chaed daaen kieil tein
kaaal dieo oeid chael chead kan seaaan oar sieo
oaaaal tear chiao keiy chiiin kao chieo cheey oiiio
cheel chiiieeain chaiy oaiir oeaao chieey tiiil
oaeid daaiy tiieir keay oiiid dael chail sen chaio
cheeer qaial kian qaay oeay oail saaaio dian
kaeier qaiir oiil oeaay teeo oaeied tieen taiy
daieo chaead kian keay oiiio oeaiil cheeeaaien
chiaeiy qiey chaiid diaid tein kiieo chiiieeain
siel oeao oier teal siad chieed oiy teed qeiaio
chear ter chieeid cheied seeeeo oaead kian qaid
saiar qeeel chaio cheial chin oir chied oair saial
kian keeao chio oeid deaad chey qiaiy tiao qaeiy
seial kaaaar cheay oaid saay cheeeo chaieel
saaaiiean oieil tein teal siad oiiad oiy ter oao
cheao diair kiir dier qaao oeao dad kao cheao
diaid tein kir cheal seial daair siey deed qeiaio
chear kear chiair kao cheao diaid tein kir chian
kaiiad oiaid chiiiio seied qaen kear oeein kaeid
tey tio saio oeied qaen kear cheear teaal teey
chiel chiael cheear oeiey qeio kiaey oeeer dead
sial saaain dio saio oeied qaen kear sey kaeir
teaeo kier sar seeen qein oaio seeid kael oen
oir saao oed qeil sin seiaed tiay seain saay
chiaer kao cheao diaid tein qaao oed qeiy sio sial
saaad kier oaien sier oaead keean teio kaid kaen
teeead chaao oaieel qaen ter oear oaeo teao
chaeiar kir chaaio oeid cheal chio seaiir dieo oeel
chiied kear ter sio sail chaid qiiio saio chiiied
oeeo sair qaay saay oaid dear chaaio oeid chiiey
tieeen oeir kiio siad chaeiar keao chaid qiad
deeeaaien siear qaid saiar keay oieo oead taed
oaiid say tair qeied kiy qio sail sieo oaaaiiean
seeel chiaed oiiey taaar dead cheaer tier kaead
keean qeaaey oieil tein keao oed keid oaiy oir
oeil sier kiiy oeao oiiir qeain chial sid teao
chaeael chin oaaeey chaar oeeel chael siil chead qair
saio deer tad qail oeean keay oaad kaeieo sair
qeeaeo qiier chair oied chaeaal qid qied kaid
keeay kir oiid taal cheo saaaid oien oail saeo
qaal oiey chael oear qaar oeain oeeao siad oaaiy
kaaeey sier chead siil oaaaid siio chad tair keo
siiil kiey chaio siieo kiael oan chair chieo seaal
qaaiy cheey chaio sieaad oay kir oiiy oaeiar qeiay
teeeo oial seil oer kaaiar kear chiao oeeed oiier
sar chair chaaan keil sier qiel chaey oeay deeo
sieo cher kiel oiao kiiy daied deay chiean chiiil
cheil oied qen siir cheeed chier cheey chead oead
kieo oial sen sead chaain oaen qail oeid cheein
ter seay oay kiel oiiid qaaaid siio oiy kao cheao
chiiey oaer qied keid chiiied sieao siien teed
tead kaey sar oaiy chil tey qied keid dael oiey
oiio saad qeiy cheey chead oead kieo oial siel oan
sar seeiin chaaar chaio siil oaaid qier tied oeean
chiair kao cheao diaid tein kir siiir deeo oeid
diaeir qead chaain saay chiaer kao cheao diaid tein
taaio oeid oiiey teeo saean chiaiar cheey oao sied
qieo daear seo oieo chaead kian keay cheo oiel
sied qaao oieo deeead chaao oier taier qio chaaao
tey qiiio oeaiil cheeeaaien sead chaain saay siiio
sieo ched qeo oieo chaead kian keer diiin kieed
oein qiey deaal teey oar seeel dael oiiieeain
chied oeean oeeer oaao oiain oaid diien chaiy
chaeiad tiaiy kieel kiiy oead chaain oeay dieo
oeel chiied kear keeer daieo oaiio oaein teaal
teey chaid diien chaiy oaao chaed oeiaer taaar oio
sial saaad kier cheaal teey oaair tio saio oeier
tiey oiael oan saay seeid kael seao chean oeeer
daieo daio deeeaaien cheaal teey oar oeay cheo
oeil oiiid tear seeel diieil qey kaeir teeeo
oeeaey qar chaaar daio sieaad oay kir chaiy chaeiad
qeiy sead chier seeel diieiy qel kaeir kair
kiiieo cheein kiio chead keal chin oaaed keer daaad
dear sar saaio oaaaiiean oeay diao qaeil sen
sail chey qar chail oaen saad qaaad qeeaey qial
chair qaad tair qiiio oeel dair tiael tein qeo
chaid teao chaaiay taar dio seao saay dian qeo
oieo oaaiy oaio char kaien oir oaiio saio daey
saay said chiair kao cheao chiiey oaid diien siian
kiel chaed daaen qial said qeao oiiay oied chieo
oiil chier chaaar oio sail chair qieo saaar qeaaao
dial sid teied daair oaio char qead chaain saay
chail oeiy siey daio seeid taid teao chaeael chin
saeay oied chaey saiil tieo oieo cheay siaiy tiao
qaeiy oaaan kiiy oied seial dael oier chieeid
oaaan deear kead oan sar siead chaey saay oaid
dear seao oer tied cheo cheeeaaien siey chiaid
chaaeel sair teaar deey chead oaeil oial chear
seeiiy seeel dael seio oaein teey chiiil oeao
chaed oied qiel oan oied chaey cheeo sid qieo
seaiil kaen qar sial said qiied char kiio siad
cheao oian qaay siead dael sail oiey oiio chial
sir keo oeear oaeil deeead chaao oan saay chiaey
qaad tair kaid dear saao oed keao oaieel tian
kiiy saeio sen cheed kear kieen taaao siil chear
teaal kaio chiiio dail sen chiao seian

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy chiiil cheeeo
chiiaad qen chaio sieaad daeaad sair kail chey
qeeen keay oieo oead taed oaiid say keao chaieed
cheieed chear qiir oiel chaey oiir deed teao cheao
saiy oaaal oiead oeiey ter oao chieo cheey chiir
dier tiy keeeo chaid teieo chan oaiid chieed char
ter seay siaiy tiao qaeiy chiy keao saal cheied
sean chiid tiaeao oier kaaiao keear sier oeeal
deeead chaao oer kir chaaio seo chaed oaiid say
tead siaiiiid teed tad taid qead qiiey qiaiy kay
qar saaio oaaaiiean oeay oieo oeaiid oiaiid oier
teaeo kier oeay oiead daeaad sen oiao kaid diieo
chey teeeo chain diaid tien diao qaeiy oiier qeiad
kiy tier tiy qeiad ken oeeeo chaid teien seaad
oaiid oer kaaed keer deeao deeeaaien saiao tear
seo chiael cheiy siear teeo sieo cher qeaad oaiid
oer qeio kiaey sey kaeir qaeo qead chaaio oiy ter
said saao cheer teio kaeo cheeay qaaio oaaaiiean
oiir oaiao tear chaiy oeao sied keieed chin saeo
qead chaaio oiy tier qiiir oaad kaeieo sair
qeeaeo qiier sar sial chair kiir diiar keiey kaid
tian qir tio oead keeel qaaaid siio oiy qio sead
keay oeer daied oeiy sey taaal chair tair cheeeal
oeeil daeaad chey tieed deaad sir qar chieo oaio
chaey oiir oead deeeaaien cheear teed qeiaio chear
tiieio taair oear seien keeeo oieo oiaiiiir kiao
qiad chaaio chear tier kiiy seaad oaiid oer kir
oiiy oiiy seaad oaiid oer qaeay cheeo oaaaao
chieed say kaiiin chir oiieo chiiieeain oeiey kaid
tiao tey qeiaey teiao oen oeaid daio deeeaaien
said oeiy chiao seian qier chead siaiiiid taed chiy

feao dio oeean kaer qeied qiien tieeo chieo oiiio
chead chead kiiey taio siieo qaal deeeaaien chael
oear keal siay oeaid chiey sey teed qeiaio chear
qaid oiiel char taio siieo kiael oan oeer diiin
tier kir seo oieo chaear kiiio dian kair tail
oaeil oiay seo oieo chaead kian qar cheeo sid tiay
oiiy oaear qeo oieo chaear taio sieaad oay qeain
chair seaaed daead oaaaao chiaid tein kiiy seiar
qeiaed tiay sier cheeeo oeeao chaid kear teey oao
sied qieo daear oiieo chiiieeain saeay oeaio cheao
chiiieeain oir chiey sey taaao oiil chiao tein keao
oiaid cheear saiiy chead siay siian qaaao diaeir
teey sio chead kaey siead chiiied oeen oied oieir
kir oeao oael siil chead qair cheael qaen kaien
chied oair oaeil tiiiir chiiy oeao chaieed dey tair
cheeeo oeio taio oeear qar chair oied chaeaaaad qid
qied kaid keeay keao oael siil chead qaid chey
qaay oeaio cheao chiiieeain sier chead siil oaaaid
siio char teey chiaeid kaey oaid dear sier saaao
chaeo qieo chaair teal seial daair said oeiy oeein
ked teal oiy qier kiiy chaieo daio deeeaaien sead
chaain oay qeaao siil chieed chear kiao kead taio
oeeo oiieo din oeiey qeio kiaey chaeay qio sial
saaad kier said oeiy oaeo teao cheao saiy saiir
oeaaed chiiin oaay sieo oeaiid chiy qier qiian tao
chael cheeed qaid chey teeo sid qieo seaiil tin
oiid tiaeao oier qaaaid siio chad kiier qeiay
kiid qier kiiy chao chael cheeed qair oir chaieo
daio deeeaaien oiaiy seil chieo dail oaio chiiied
oeeo chey qiaiy tiao qaeiy oiid tiaeao oier
qaaaid siio chad kiier taiy oaao oael char qio
oaead kael chad kan seeaer qeiay kiid qier kiiy
oaeiad qeiy cher cheey chaio sied qiiiy teiey oael
seao oiel chieo deeead chaao oaio oiy taed diay
siel chaey chail oaen sieo seaiil kier oiaiy oaaal
oiead oeiey qeal chin chaar oeeen qied keid oiiey
teeead seaal qiiio sid qiair cher sieao chieeo
chiaen qar saay oeaio cheao chiiieeain seil chiao
seo chaid tean oier oead taed oiaid chaain kiiy
chao chael cheeed qair sier chaieo oaiio sio siien
teey chiaeid kaey chair sied keid daead oeean qaay
sieo oeaiid chaiin tair oeaio deaao chao chaain

feao dio oeean kay qeaao cheao chaain oied oieo
oeiaer qail saeil chey tair cheao oieo dair qeeen
teao oieo dair qar seiaiy cheey sieao oieo
oaaaiiean chial oaeo siil cheieed char qein tiiiid
cheied char qead chier chial oaeo siil cheieed char
ter cheeao deeeaaien chieeid cheied seeeeo oaear
taio cheeer qaial kian kiel chaed oaiid say kir
chead chad tead saeo taed oeeao say kiiy chaieo
daio deeeaaien chaio cheeer qaial kian qar seeel
dael oiiieeain chiy qaeo taed oeeao oier qaao
oaieel qaeo qay taier qaid oeil seaan kear
chiieio taair chaeay ted oed kear ter chaieed oir
qein oaien oair kaean tieeiy cher chieeo chaal dail
sen cheey sead chaain oay qeeal oair cher cheaal
kaio oaaaiiean cheaar diaeay saio cheao oier kid
sir qar saay sieo oeaiid chiy qead chier siaiy qad
char ter chaieed oir qaay oeo said tiay chied oair
oaien oir oaaan tian qaio cheao oier kaaaad oiaid
oer kir oied cheio keid deal siiin cheer chieil
tein kir chiey sey tead oeid dead sair qear qeien
kied oieir keeel oear taeay qead dan cher chieeo
oaieed chaeaey cheeeo chaio daio cheio keead chan
oael seio chaal oiaiid oer kaead tan chead saeo
keao chier oiao kead tain oear teear kiao kaiio
qien oir oiien dail sieo dair kail chey keo oiel
