cheaao chao oied qaen kieel chaal chaear keal siay
sieo dair qiead oeiey qeaal kan sar seeen teeo
oaio deeal chey kaid dear oeiey tead siay cheal
siad dan oieo oaar qid kiel say tiy keao chaieed
dan sier til tey keo oiel cheaao chao oied qein
taeay kaeo teiay oay keaio chiiio chiiieeain oeiey
qeio kiaey sey kiio seao chiiel oiy taeay kaeo
teiay oay qeiaed tial chan oieey cheal saiin cheey
sio sail oieeo oeo sair teeo seaiil tein teeel
dad keal chin chiey sey kao cheao chiiey oaer teao
oieo daid kiy ter said oaio daal oair siey oio
sail oieeo oeo saeo qey taeay qaiao tear oir
oiel oiaey cheey cher chael oear keal siay oiiy
chair kaiil tin oead qaaail oiy qiir dead seao
cheel dear chaiy dao cheal saiio sio chear kaio char
qead deeeaaien cher chaieeo diaial sen saar oeio
kiaey cheear kiao kil tain oaid diien oir oead
taaid sin oear ter oiaeo sin oied chaey seio
oaein kear said oeel chiiied oeeo sid qier kaio
chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean qeeey daaao seeiid kao sean chiel
oiaid cheied char tiiiy oaao oiiid qeil oao seao
cheiy chieeo chaeaaay cheao oieo dair qar seeen qaay
oeaio cheao chiiieeain seil chiao oeel daeaad sir
qar oeay oeeeed daead sir tial oaeo siil cheieed
char ter sail oeid cheen seaaed daead oaaaao chiaid
tein keao oiiir teael qaen ter chiaeay chied oair
oieo daio chear taeay qey ter chead saeo taed oail
oiel chaaan cheeeo chaio cheal sein chael seio chaal
oiaiid oer keao oiiir tied cheo cheeeaaien cheear
teed teao chieed oiy qaial kian qar sieo chiaid
tiay siiir oeeeed daead sir tair oiiio diieo
oead qier tiy keil sal kiiey sar oaiy chied
chiiieeain chian tao oied qeil cheeal chey qail
saead oiaid oed tey teaay cheeo chiair tail oeiy
chaied diaiy tieel qen cher seir keein taio sieaad
oay qiel chian chao oied tiaiy kaeiad kaio daair
cher oeal sein sieao chieeo chiaen teao cheil chear
oir oiiy siiir cheo said kaeay qail saead oiaid
oer qiel oeao deeead chaao oer kaaiar kieed oaair
taaar chaeid tey qar oaiy chiy keeeo chiiaad qaen
kead oan siad cheeel chead kian qar chair chaaao
kiel oer kir sier saao chieo oaail say qaiiy chead
siay siian keid chael chial oiieo deeay qial chair
tail oeiy siey deal cheied daead sir qeeel daaao
seeiid kan siead oaiy chaar chaiy chiy qeo oieo
oaaiy seir kieo daio chead qay kir oiel oiael sin
siaiy qead kaieer chir saeio chan chaied oieo
oaaaiiean oied chaey seio oaein tieo dio saaaid
chiaiy

feao dio oeean qeeaeo qiier chio oeid deal siiio
saeo qey tier kiier qaao oieo chiel say tiey sey
taio oiil oiiey qaad kaeieo sair taao oiiel char
ter cheear kao siad chiel chiieo saeo qey kieel
siir oaao seier kir siel chaey chaio chear taied
diaiy tieel qen cher chaaao oiil chiao tein taiy
oeo chaed oiiil oiao kay teeo seaiiy qail oieen
seied kiy kaio seeid qeiey kiio sead taio char
teeao deeeaaien cheeao chear qail chey qial oaaaay
teer chay keaio oeier kair taaen qiad chaaio cheio
tiy taaio seo chiaeao say tao chied qiey cheey saar
oeio kiael chieo oiaiid oan seeel deal cheial oiy
qio sead keal chin chaar chaiy chiy qiel oeao deeead
chaao chien oaad kaeieo sair qeeaeo qiier sar cheeo
sid tiay siiir diean oear saaao chaaio sied tair
qar seeel dael oiiieeain oeid oeiey tier taiy
oeo chaed oail oiel chaaao chear tier tian tead
saail oer kir oaiio oaio dael oiiieeain cheeel
oaair qear qein taed oeeao say kir oiieo
chiiieeain oeao sied tear sail saeil chey qar
cheiao oen oeaid deeeo deeeaaien said oeiy chiao
seian qier chead siil oaaaid siio char tieo dio
saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kay qied keid daeaaaao chaiio oain
deal siad dao chaaad oaaiy kaaan qieo cheeel kaaay
dieo siiil qier taier kiio siad chaeiar keeed
oial siey siey chaien oeao sied tear seieir oeay
chiel oieo oiair cher chiiin teal chaio chaeo chiaen
teeead chaao chaid teao cheil oan saay seaao chieey
tieeen der saial kian tad kiao qeio oaein ter
sieao oaaao dead chao oier kieel qiier oaid dear
chiar qiian teal sead tiaeir qeay seier qaiil
kiiieo dear sail oiieo oiair seir kao cheao chiiey
saay cheiey said oeiy chiial oaair qeaaan oar
seaao chieaad chiiio dail sen sio seaaed chiiied
oeen chil qir qieo cheel cheeay tao cheal seeeey
teio taid qieo chaan siear teio tieeo keao oer
kir oaaeen chian kair kay qiiil chiiiiy teey cheear
qio seaaed chiiied oeen saao oael char qiey daaal
chair tieo sir kaid oaaaiiean seeel dien oar
saiil kiiieo oiel oan saay sail oiieo oiair chier
taiy cheeel oear ter chiiaan sey tiiaao siio chien
chaieo deeeo chied oeo sair kied chaey oaen tad
qail chey qar siaiy tiao qaeiy chiy kaaeen seao
chaal chil ker qaao oeao dad kao cheao diaid tein
taieo deeeo deeeaaien cher chaeay kaeo teiay oay
qeeiin said chaid teao cheil oeal chin chied oeean
siey chaio siil del tar taeay kaeo teiay chieao
oer tiy kaaeen chaieo oiied oeo sair qar chieao
oer tiy kaaeen seao oeeel kear taeay kaeo teiay
chieao oer tiy kaaeen oied sied qaio dael siair
chaeay qead chaain sar siiid oair oaen oaid diien
chiy keao oiao kiil cheal chin chiey sey teal saaio
daear oeid oeiey taeay kiio chial sir qar oiel
chaal oied tear oay qiiil sier taier qey teal
cheied dear chaier tiair taio sied keid oio siiid
daey siead diieo oaio daeaad oaio chiiied oeen
oeiey qaao oael char ter chead seaiil teio qir
qiad diaiy keiey tied oeean cher chael oear tian
chiy taieo chaead kian qeeel deal cheial oiy taied
cheier oeer diiin kiio cheao chaaeel oaio daey
saiil qaay chir oaaal cheail daid kier oeal seao
chain chear oiiio diao keao oael chad tair qar oaiy
chiy kied oieid tan oiao qiad chied chear taed
chaeay qiaiy kay qar seeel dael oiiieeain chaiy
chieel kiiy daeay qey ter seay saay cheio keid
dead sair kiiil deear qaid oeiy cher oiaeo sin
siead chiiio chiiieeain chead siay oaeo chiaeo tian
kaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean tieo sieo ched qeian qaeid daaiy
qiey chiaed daear chiar qid kiel say keao oiiir
teael qaen kaien oir chao cheal siied oen oir
oiieo chiiieeain chaiy chied seeid kan oeead kaen
kaiy chiy tao chael cheeed qaid chey qaiil keeey
qier oieel qiad cheier chiean cher oear oeeen qar
chair chieo seaal qaaiy cher oaeo teao chaeiar kir
oeeel seao chaaio char ter cheeao deeeaaien saial
kian qiaiy qead kaeo kiy tiaeiy teey chiiil daiel
oied tear chiean siir chaen chieao oer qeio keao
din chao chael cheeed qair oaaial keel dain oieil
tin chaaid tian taar oeeen kay teio kaid tail
saaiy kied chieaal kian qar cheeo sid tiay oaen
cheaar chear chieeo oaieed chaeael chin chian qaeid
daaiy qaid oeiy chieeo oed tiaiy kir oaaeen oair
qear qiey deaal teey daaar oieao diair kiir dael
cheeed teed qaar kaiiin chir saein oiiy oied seeid
kan oied chaey seio oaein ter cheey cheear kiaed
oiiey tiao qieo chan oied chaey cheeo oaaaao chieed
say kaiiin

feao dio oeean qied keid daeaaaao chaiio chey qaid
chaal oaad qaid tein qaeid cheier oeer diiin keao
daaal tiiiy oeao oeeel kear kieey oiio oaead
kian tail oeiy oeao oier keaid chaeay kaiil tin
chaier kao oaid oeiey qiear taio oiiil qien
oaaial teeeaaien siear qeial chael chieo siiil
qier keil sier taiy deael qaen tiiil oaeid daaiy
qio sead keal chin oir saeay cheeo oaaaao chieed
say taar oeeen keo oeeao tead kaen qar sieo cheel
cheeay qiiin ter sead chier oay qaad qaio daio
seiay deaal teey chaer qiiio saead deien oir
oiieo chiiieeain oiiy oaeiay kair kal tar taaal
chair keair kair kaean taied chaaio oiy ter sein
taid qiad cheieeeeo oeaao oiy tead oeel deed tear
qaial qiieo oao siad chieed oiy qein teeao
deeeaaien oaeo taio oen chiaeiy qail oaeil oiier
saay oieio qair sey tiiel dear oir oeil oied
qaen tian cheeel oaair tiean cher chaio chial oeel
daid kier oear saaao chaaio sied tair qiiil chieo
oeeao oer kir oiieo chiiieeain seio keao din
oaeiay keil sier taiy chieel kiiy chiio chial sir
keo oiieo oeeaey qeaaan cheer diiaan siey chaio
chaaal tair keair qiiir dio saio dael oaeay keair
qiiir oeeael kiiieeain oeiey tiy qaein oiiy oied
seeid kan oied chaey seio oaein ter oael seao
oiel chieo oaid sin oieil tein kiiy oaen cheiey
chaid kiiieo dear oiiy oiid kieo oieo cheal siay
saeiy chaier kaieed cheil oaen tio saio dain chial
oaio chaio seeid kiiey teey chian saeay cheeo sid
tail sieo daid tial sen

feao dio oeean qear teear qio sial saaad kied
say kieel kiiy chieer daiy dieel qiel oao cheiad
oiair chier kaeiad kaio diieiy qio sead keal chin
oeiey tiy tiel sail oeey oaial kian kail chey
qaay oaen tieo siiil qier qaiiy oiiy oeaid oial
oiel cheay cheied chael chad kier kir oiid kieo
oeiaer qeao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kay teeead chaao chaid teao cheil
chaear oaaan tiy taieo daio deeeaaien chaied cheil
oael chin oiiy saeay oied chaey chieo sid qiair
chieeen der chaieeo diaial sen chian oeid oeiey
qead chaain oiiio deer kay qail oiieo cheeay kir
oiieo cheo cheeeaaien oieed oein tier tiain qiead
diieo chey kieil tein kaeiay keiey qeio kiaey
said oaio dael sair chian seao oer tied cheo
cheeeaaien cher chieeey saay chieed deaad sir kieiy
teaar oeir tiieiy ter chaied oeiy sey tieeo oed
tiaiy qaao oaieel qaeo qay qier oaiiay oir oeeed
chier cheey chead oead kieo oial oeier siaiy kay
qar said oer qiiir oail oiieo cheeay qaao oael
char qio siier siaiy qaid oiao qiad chiel chiien
saio cheao oier kir oaeil deeeo oeiy sey tael oil
teeay oeao saal dear oeeo saio deaar oeiey qaao
oaeo qiaid oer qiiio teal chad kaen kieil tein
kaeiad kaeo chiaen qar oeeao said oaeo taio daaiy
teed qeiaio chear ter chaio chial oeel daid kier
chian sier cheeeo oeeao chaid kear ter chied keao
oael char ter sio oaiir chiy keil sal kiiey sar
seeel dael oiiieeain oair qaay cheeead chaao chaid
teao cheil chaear oir siel chieo deeead chaan chian
chead siay sier chiain qiead diieo chey tail oaio
chiiied oeen oir seo oaio oiiy oey tael oil teeay
oied chieaal kian qiaiy qeieir seial cheeiy chian
kaiio sid taael say keid diieo sen oiio saad
qeiy cheey oer qaeay oear teaal kair kail chan
chead cheel deio qaen kaien oir saeio chan chaied
oaaao oio oiiieeain chead siay oaeo chiaeo tian
kaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kied chaey sieo cheel cheeay kiel
chaed chiel chieo deeay tair cheeo oeid diaeir qeay
oay qieo sid teao cheil cheed kiy ter sieao cheel
oiiey tio oeid deal seeao oain oaer tio saio
deeay oir oead taed chiey oeeer oaao chaed oeiaer
teao chiir ked teal oiy tier kaiiay seain seeen
kay qieo cheel oiaid chien deeo saio diiaal chien
oaiil qaay chir oeao saal daaiy keeen qiaiy qad
char tiiil oeil chaal oied tear sein oaien sier
cheeeo oeeao chaid kear taaal oaio diieo sir qead
taio chial sir qeaao chieaad chiiio dail sen chiair
taed oiiil oied qaen qiear kied chael oiy tead
cheaer ter oao chieo cheey oaay oeo said tiay chied
oeean oeeer oaao chaed oeiaed tein tair taaar
deal siad deal seao oaaiy qier chian qaao chaed
oeiaed tian kir oaiio seaiid oeieiy oaieo sir
kay qaid saiad ken saeo taed oaid oied qein
taeay qaiao taair cher chieeo chieo oial seiy chian
oair qaay siad chaaio oil taeaad sair qar seaaay
oiel oiael say kir saeio chan chaied oieo
oaaaiiean oied chaey seio oaein

feao dio oeean kay qeeel daaao seeiid kao sean
dao chaaio qair tair cheeo oeid diaeir tiiin tead
cheaer kiio siad chaeiar kir oead taed chiey oeeer
oeain sear tieeiy cher chaied oaaaid siin siad
chiel chaaao chieo said tein kied oieir kir oead
taed chiey oeeer died oair oaien oair qeaaey sar
seeel chael siil chead qair siiir diean oear saaao
chaaio sied tair qead taio chial sir qeeen keo
oeeao tear ter seay oeay dieo seaiil tein kir
oaaan taiy chiid kaieel qaeo qay qeao oer tied
chiiieeain oir seo oaio oiiy cheeeo qail sir teeo
seio taeaad sir taaar daed oail oeeeo oaio chear
ter chiiin qey tael oil keao oeeey chiio cheao
chaaeel oain deaal teey oiaiy qad char ter chiiin
taio oiil oiiey taaan kir oaaeen chaiy oaao chaed
oeiaer qear qiey oio chead qeiey teaal teey oar
siad chiel chaaao chieo sair qar chio seao chaey sein
chier qiiir cheo oeeao tear ter seay chaaar oio
siieo kier teael qaen qaid oeiy seay oay tio
saio deeay saiiy chiy teal sied qaio dael oaeay
tio saio deeay oir chead seao cheil chear chaiy dao
chaaio qair qiaiy kieio qiier sar sail saeiy cheer
deaad chaen qear tieeiy cher seay oay qieo cheel
oiaid chien oaiil qaay oier saead chied oied qaaiy
kir sier siiin ter sey tael oil teeay seao oaiil
kiiey sar chaaao kiel oer teael qaen ked qaeo
tiao seian kir oiieo chiiieeain oiiy oaeiay kir
oeao oael siil chead qair oaen chair oiiio diieo
oead tiiiiy ter chied keao oael char tael oear
keal siay siiir cheo oeeao qaad qaid tein tiel
chiao sen siir chaaal oial cheeeaaien siey deaal
teey oiad diaiy keiey qaao oael char ter oiaen
chaied oaaao oaaaiiean oied chaey seio oaein

feao dio oeean kaer qail oeid oail oaio chiiey
tiiin kael cheeeo oeil oiaed chan sar sieaao
oeieiy chian chaiel oied kaaio saaio chan sar oay
qieo cheel cheeay keeeo chaid teieo chey keo oaiiad
deeead chaan chaeay ted oer kir oeao oael siil
chead qair oaen chiean oear oaeo teao chaeiar kaid
qieo seil oer keeel seao chaaio char qio oaiir chiy
keil sal kiiey chio seao chaey seio sin sear qeio
kaen qail oeid oail oaio oiiey qiead diieo chey
qar seaao seao cheil sin sein chiiad taar oear
qein taed oeeao say qaao oaeo qeeel cheal seao
oaaaid siio chin oir oeil chiiiiy keal siier seeil
chaeo teeead chaan cheey chian seao chiiad tiaiy kiel
oiael say kaiiin daar oeeen qein oaio char qead
deeeaaien cher chieeeo dear oiiy seaad oaiid oer
qaiir chaer qar seeen kay qial chel qaaiy oiio
cheao chaaeel sir qaiil qaay diar kead tied qiel
chaaao cheied chiain sar seeel dael oiiieeain chaiy
cheiey tair qar oaiy chian kiel chaed chiel chieo
cheeay keaid oeeao say keid oeiey qaid oeiy cher
chieeo chieo oial cheeeaaien chian cheeel oaair qear
teey chaed oeeao chieo oiaiid oier kir oiieo
chiiieeain oaen sieo saaar qail saeil chey qeeid
qiir chiid kieo oieo cheal siiy chiel chaed chiel
chieo cheeay tiel say tiar kead tied qiel oao
siiio saead deien chiar qaeo taaio qaeid oen sead
chier seeid qiir diar kaeil dial seao oeeel qid
tian taiy oeial qen oeal siay oeao sied tear
seaaal chey qar cheiey chead cheaer qeal oiy ter oao
chieo cheey oear tiiad qeil chieo oien oaaed keer
chieey oeal siay siian tieel qaeid oen chiiil oeo
oaio oear taied deay oaid dear oiid kieo oieo
cheal siiy diel say kiel chaed chiel chieo cheeay
tiar qeao oeeel qid tian kiid kieo oeiaer kaer
teied chael chad qay qiel chian chao oied qeil cheeal
chey qeiaed qeao cheear chiad qein kiiied oeeo oead
qied teeel oiair oiio siad chaeiad qein qead chier
cheaay cheaad chaen kied chaey oaen qeial chael chieo
siiil tad kaen qeeel chaio siad chead kan oeo oeel
daeaad sir qaiil qaay chaaan keao saal daaiy
keeen teeo saio diiaal chien oaiil qaay diar kead
tied qiel oao siiio saead deien oir oaeil daaey
sey tael oil teeay saead chied oied qaaiy keiey
qaao oaeo qiaid oer kir oeao oael siil chead qair
oaen chair oiiio diieo oead qier kaid qieo chaair
keil sier qiel chaey oaen qail oeid oail oaio
oiiey qiead oeiey qeaal keal chin saao oed qiel
chieo deeead chaao oiiey teey cheey chiael oeil oaio
siil dey qeeel chiaed oiiey qeeiiy oeer diiil
cheil saaaiiean chaaar deaal teey oiaiy teeo seil
oer tied oair oaeil kao siay oiiy oaeil diay
seao chiair tier kiied kear teal siad deal seao
daaiy taed oeeao oan cheeo oiid kaey said oed tey
qeao chain cheal siay oiiy chead siay siel chieo
deeead chaao sin saay sein sio siier seiaed qeao
cheear saeid cheier oeer cheal seao oaaaid siin
cheaad chaen qaay chieo oeel chiier tead siay oir
oaiio oaio dael oiiieeain oaen sear teey chaed
oeeao chieo oiaiid oier kir oaiiiy saiay teal
cheial oiy

feao dio oeean kied chaey oaen tieo siiil tad
kaen teied chael chad qay kir oiid kieo oeeaey
tiael tio oeiy oaeo qiieo teed kiy taio sied
keid deaad chey qied keid oio sear ter sio sieaal
chiaen taaan kir oieel kiiy oeir taid qead keal
chio oeeeo oiaid oaean cher chaio chial oeel daid
kier chian sier cheeeo oeeao chaid kear tied keao
oael char ter sey tael oil teeay oeeel siiio oaio
diieo sir qar cheeo sid tail oaio chiiied oeen
chian keaid oeeao say kir seo oaio oiiy dian oair
kaen qiad chaaio oiy ter chieeey sail saeil chey
qar cheiao oen oeaid deeeo chieer oied chaey seio
oaein tieo dio saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean qail oeid oail chey kaer qied kaid
tail seo chaaao cheied chiain sar cheaar oio chead
kael oen oiid kieo oieo cheal siay cheael qaen
kaeo qiieo teed kiy teed qeiaio chear ter sein
tead cheaer taeay ted oer kaiio seaiid daio dian
kir oeao saal daaiy keeen teeo saio diiaal chieo
oiay oieel kiiy der chaid qead keal siiio saead
deien oeao oael siil chead qair sier cheeeo oeeao
chaid kear tied keao oael char kaeo teao chaeiar
taar chiiil daid tin oaen oeo seil oiiid tear sar
seeel dael oiiieeain oiiy seaad cheo cheeeaaieo
oer tied oair oieer diar kaio dien dail oeiy
oeeer chieel kiiy daeay kiio chial sir qeeel dael
oiey cher oiio chad keal seao oaaaid siin sein cher
chaaad dear sein chier tian keid diieo chey qar
saaio oaaaiiean sail saeil chey teiao oen oeaid
daio deeeaaien said oeiy chiao seiao qein tieo
dio saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaer qeied qiien kaio cheaay sail
oaeieo sen seay oay qaiil kiiieo dear sail oiieo
din chiar qeo oieo oaaiy cher sein kao cheao chiiey
chiael qeeeer chear oeeen tiean seal oiy ter oiaiy
sier saio cheao oied tey qar oaiy chiy keao saal
cheied sean cheeeo chiiaad qaen kear chaed oail
oeeeo oaio chear ter oear oeeeo qail sid tar ter
cheaal tieen kiiy sier chiain tieo seaal qaaiy
oaien oir oaaal dial oeel daid kier chian sier
oeeel seao chaaio char tied keao oael char ter
chiiaan oeeer cheeeo chiiaar qeio kiaey seien tead
chaio chead kiiied oeeo said tein qaao cheed tiay
saiin qaiiy chaieo daio oen cheey chian saao cheed
tiay chiy kied siad cheeey seeel dien oar seaao
chieaad diaial sen chier kiiy oiel cheeao cheier
chaid cheeay kay qaid saiad ken saeo taed oaid
oier kir chead seao cheil chear oaen chaid cheeay
teeo seil oer taiy cheeeo saal oaeo kian qar
sieaao oeieiy cheey oear seien kied sied qaio
deal siiio chey qeao oaaao deaad chieen kiieo oial
seil chien oar said oiaid chiel oiaid cheied char
ter chiiaan chian oeeed daael oed qaeo taair chier
kaear keeel seeeey qar oaiy chiy kaiiay oeaid
daio deeeaaien chier kaead qir tiaed qay keeel
seeeey qar seeel dael oiiieeain oaen seeid qiir
cheaid oaiin sead chaain saay chieo saiil oiel
chiaeao oan sear keeer oeiad kiy taaal oaaaay kir
oiieo chiiieeain oaen sail saeil chey qar sial
chair qein chiiil chiel oiael say kaaeey chaar oeeel
dael oiiieeain oiiy oaiiay cheeel oaair tiean
cheey chian sir qaid chiiad qen cher chiiin tian
saiid dian tied chiiieeain oiier keeel seeeey
qaay chiael qeeeer chiio chad taio chieaal kiiey
teey chian chiy qeao oieel cheal seiain seiaed qeao
cheear oaiiin chiiy saeio cheal siay cheael qaen
kieen taaao siil chear qel ker qaeid cheil chaear
oaeo chiaen qeao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kay qied keid daeaaaao chaiin seao
chaal chiy tiiin keao daaal tiiiy oeao oeeel kear
ter cheeao deeeaaien saar dead sial oiir ked teal
oiy qaial kian qeiaey qar oied chieo oeiaed taio
chiier taiy dead siay saao oeao oier teal siil
oaaaid siio oiy tieeo chaeaaay chieen kaid
oaaaiiean oeer chiio oaear keal siier seeil chaeo
qaid oan saay chail oeiy oeao oier qeil chieo
chieio qeeao chiao sen chieeid cheied char qio oaear
ter sio seeid taal chear taied oaaaid siin said
saiar qeial cheeiy chiy keil sal kiiey oeo oeeao
tear taeay ted oer kir oeao oael siil chead qair
oiiy sier chead siaiiiid teed tad taid qead qiiey
qead taio chial sir qar oeo chieer sey tael oil
teeay chiey chieeiy chaied oaaao oaaaiiean sear
qeien kiid taed oied tao oiiir kir oaaeen chaiy
oeao chaal dad qay keiey tiy kair qaay siead cheao
chiiieeain oaiiay oir seo chaaio kiel oed qieo
dair taaar oio siieo kier kaiiad oiaid chiiiio
seier kir chao chiiad keel chien oar saay sieo seil
chaal oied tear siaiy qad char ter chiiil cheil
saaaiiean sear qein teed qiad chead qio cheeer
kiio chial sir taaar chaio chaaal tair qar sail
saeiy oaen said saal oaeo kian tiean cheey chian
chao chaair saial kian kieio qair said cheeeao oair
cher oiaeo sin siead cheao chiiieeain chead siay
oaeo chiaen qeao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean tieo seaiil teio qir qaiil qey
tiiin kaio chieaal kiao tar teal cheio teey oar
sar saiil taed deeead chaan cheey oaieo oain der
cheeao deeeaaien cheey saial kian qeiaey qear qel
ker kaaiao keear oir oeeed oiier oiir chieed oein
qaiao tear chiy qeo chieo oiil chier sar oaiy chiy
qeo oieo oaaiy oear oieo daio chead qay kiiy sir
qeeil chiied qir qeaaan deaad chaen kay kiel chaid
teao cheil seiey kao cheao chiiey sar sial chair
taaar diieio taair saaal chiier keeed oial siel
oan oeer dead oeid dear oiiy oied oieir keiey
tiy kiiy oaaeey chian qeao oed tey keeao siil
cheieed chin

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey taio
oiil daead oain daio seeid taid teao chaeael chin
chaar oeeey cheal siay saiin kiiy siel chaey siead
chael oiey cheed qiil dao oiiied cheao oen cher cheed
qiil dao oiiied cheao chiel oiao kiiy der said
oeieeeed qaad qid qied kaid keeay kir seio kieo
oeiaer qead taio chial sir qiiil chieo oeeao oer
tieey qiel chaey sein seal oiy qeir qaid oeil
chiair kiio chaio oid tio saio oeier qiiio qin
oeer diaeio chio oeiy chaied diaiy qiel chian chao
chaieel oaein kiaid dead chead kiiey tiiad taar
oiil diair tail oeiy chiieir teeo oiid kaey oay
qiiil seaao tied kean oiiied oeeo saio dael
seeao chiao sen cher chiiin qey teed teao chieed
chear kaio chieel teeal chan sead chier siaiy kied
chaey sar seeen teeo sieo cher tao oaeo taio daaiy
qieao oieo oaaaiiean saiil taed chier oeeer chiel
oien oial chair tail oeiy cheey chiaead tian oeaid
chiey siir chaaan tiy qeaiid oial chey qiead diieo
chey qiaiy kied chaey sar saay sieo seil chaal oied
tear cheaay siaiy kay qar oaen taaan tian keeed
chier sead chaain oay qaid saiad ken oied cheio
teel dio sair qar saaio oaaaiiean char tael cheeed
teed kiy tiair tael oil teeay oaaan kaen cheaad
chaen tieo sir tiean cher chiaeay oaen oieio qair
sey qieao chieeo oen oaail chier seeid qiir oier
chiy qeiar tio seao saay chaen cheaad chaen kiiil
deear tier tian keaid oeeao say tieey qiel chaey
sein sey kaio seeid kael oen sieo chiiir qeil
chieo dieo oaaed teeay keer deeao deeeaaien said
oeil chaer deeo saao chair qeiael tian kir oeaiid
dar tian oeid diieo sen chael oear tian oeao
oaieel qeo oiio cheeed kaey oiiil daid tin oair
kied chael seier teeo oead qay taar oiead daear
oaen seeaer kaaial keel dain sar seeen qein
oieed oein kaio char kaien oir oeeed chier chier
til tey qaid saal oaeo kiao kiy tead said teio
qir qar chail oaio chiiied oeen oaen oieil tein
tead siay oiiy oiid taal oieo cheal siiy oeio
taaio kiid qay tair keaio daey chail oeiy seaal
kan chiead dear oaear keil sal kiiey cheaay siad
chaaio chin siel chaey siaiy kaid oaaaiiean seeeo
tial cheay oeer cheal seao chier sein saaal chiier
tead siay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sieal chead taaao
chead kian kaer qiad cheeel siiio said tein tiel
say taar oeeen kay qiiio saeo taed cheo oeeed
oiay oeeel oear qiey dieo chaeay kaid diien siian
kiel chaed chaen qeaaan deao chiid kaey chad qail
chey qaaio oaaaiiean oay qeaaey oaid diien siian
kiel chaed chaen teeo oiid kaey saaio dael seeao
say taar oeeen qeier teeean chaier diey said oeil
oiiien chiel oiaid cheied char qead deeeaaien cher
cheeao deeeaaien sead chier oaid diien siian kiel
chaed chaen teeo oiid kaey cheio tied oeeao say
keil sier qiel chaey cheaad chaen qaair qail saead
oiaid oer kaaial keel dain siead oaiy oiel oiael
say taar oail saeiy oiier keid daaal oeeeo oied
qaen qiey chaeo taio oen chied qein teed tead kaey
sar sear kao cheao chiiey oeo oaid taiy saao cheed
tiay chead siad chaaiay keil chiiiiy keal siier
oied chaeaaaad qeil oan saay chail oeiy sey tiieil
taal oeeaey qiead oaiy chaar oeeel chael siil chead
qair oiiy cheael qaen kear cheed qiil dao oiiied
cheao oen cher oear cheed qiil dao oiiied cheao oeo
cheal cheio teey dair sead taio chial sir qar chair
chiao qaio diaeir ter chiiin tiair qeiel tied kean
chaio chad keeil chel ken saead chied oied qaaiy
keiey kid sir qar oay tieo seaal qaaiy cher oao
siieo teal chad keao oier kiiy cheael qaen tier
kaead qir qieo saeo qaal oiel oan oieio qair sey
kaeid kear tael oear keal siier oied chaeaaaad
qeil oan saay sein chiieir qeeael qiil chean chaied
oen siir chaaao qeiy oeal siay chaiy chiel oiaid
cheied char qiey cheal siay oeao chaaio qaeil sen
chaied deay sar siiid dael chail chan said oeil
oaiin kiio sead taio char kiaed cheao din chaar
oeeen tail oeiy siey daaao oiil chiao tein taiy
deal seial daair sead chaain chaaar deal cheied chao
char kear chiaid taain tead siil oaaaid siio char
ter oao chieo cheey oein oieed oein kiio saad qeiy
sey kaeir keil sier qiel chaey sein oieed oein
taied deay cheaay sieo oeeao teial chin chaar oeeey
chieen ter said sid qaid oiao qiad chaain oied
chael cheeean chaiin tead oiieo oier kaaan qiel
chaey oeay oiiio oeel daid kiey chiaid qin chiao
qaeiy siian qeiad kiy ter cheal cheiay sear tiaid
taain kaiiay oied oiil chiao tein qeain cheaar
oeal chin siel chaey oail chey kay kied oiiio
oeeaed qiel oier keiey qaao oael char qiear kay
qieo oaaed qied qaio cheao oiair oiaen oeal siay
oaen oied chaey seio oaein taaao siil chear qey
kiaed dael siair chiao seian kaio chao cheeead
oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy sey taio siieo
taal chiiieeain chiar qaaaid oiao qiad dead oiy
qeal oiy qiir oeaad oeaan chaaar chiiied cheio keid
oaid chey qeaao seeeey qar siiid oair oeiey qeio
kiaey cher sail oeid cheen chiao qaeiy siian kaeiay
kied oieir kir oeeed oial chear cheaan kiiil deear
ter chaeay kiio chiao keied char tiiio kaey chieo
seaal qaaiy oaeo teao cheao oian qar sieo cheel
chier sail oeel deal saiy oaaial keel dain sial
saaar qein oaeo qiad chier oied chien oeeil oay
taar oeial cheeiy oaear qaeid cheil oan siaiy teeo
seio taead sir tio seao saay chaen siaiy tiao
qaeiy oair qeier tieo oaio diieo chey keer chael
chead tieo daael oan sein chaio oiil chan cheeo sieo
cher taier taaad oeaiien oiio siaiiir seo oieo
oien oir oeil chaed oaid say kaio chao cheeead
oaeay

shiiad taar chail cheal siay chied oair seal oiy
teal sieaal chiaen teeead chaao chaid teao cheil
chaear siiir oiiid dael chaiy sied sen sieal chead
taid kied say kir sier saaaid siio sied qaio
dael siair cher oiaeo sin cheaad chaen qaid chiiad
qen said oeiy chiao seian taar oeeiiy sein chaio
oiil daear

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey taio
oiil chan sieo oeeao qiil chieer oaer keeo cheey
deio qiiil qeiaer qial chieen teer diao qiiiy
keeao siad daaiy qio sead keal chin chaar oiead
chiiied oeen cheer oaid saiar qar chio seao saay
deer oeeeey qieo cheel oiaid chien oiiio saead
deien oieel kiiy der chaaad dear oiir oead chaain
seier qieo cheel oiaid chien oaid oiao kiil daear
oir oaiiiy chian oiid ked kear tiaeiy ter chiael
oiiay oay teiad oiair chaeay qier kir cheal siil
chiel cheay sein oieed oaair tiean cher chaieeo
diaial sen sio seaaed chiiied oeen oied oieir
taiel oied kaaio saaio chan sar oiiieeail kied
tear sein chier til qir qiiil chiiiiy ter chael
oear keal siay oiiy oeao cheed qiil dan sier kead
taaid sair chaar dio seao saay dian tao chieeid
oaaaid siin siaiy kear ter oiio seeeeo oaiir
saeay seao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oail chey
qaiil tiao seo sio chaaao kied siad cheeel siiin
chiar qaao oiaio oiy qiir chaaan kied cheeed taaal
chieen taier taaao cheio keean keair kiel oiiid
tiaiy kir oaiio seaio chiay saaao chaen qeaao
chieey ter chiaeay saaao chaen qeaao chieey qead
chaain sar oaiy saaao chaen kied chaey oiel chaal
deeead chaao say kir seo oaio oiiy chiiio deear
tier kaear keeel seeeey qar seeel dael oiiieeain
oeiey tair tieo oaaed tial chan oear oaiid chieed
char ter oaio seeid kael oen sier siiin ter cheey
siey deaal teey oeied ken saao chied keao oed
kear kaio siad chier seeel dien deeo seil oer
qiiir deeo saean cher oaio seao oeiy saar oaao
oael char qey taio oiil daear oeeeo chieo oial
oear ter cheeao deeeaaien saar chaeiay kir chao
seaar siiid daey cheied daair cher sail oeel deal
saiy cheer oaay siiid daey chieo oaaed tial oaio
daey seaaan cheeao siad daaiy qiir cheeed oiier
oeay oaiil qaay chair qeier qeaao siil chieed chear
ter chiiin keeer cheao saal daaiy taeay kieo oial
cheeeaaien cher chaied oaaaid siin saay cheiad oiair
sey tael sead qaid oiiel sein saiao tear chil
qeiar qar chio seao saay chaen cheaad chaen kiiil
deear tier qiiir oiiil chiiiiy ter chiiil cheil
cheaar chaiy chieel kiiy der cheean chaen oiiil deear
qey tael oil teeal chan chiaeiy ter oeeer chaiio
cheel chaeay kiel chaed chiiieeain cheer deaad chaen
qar oiel chiael cheead chan saiao tear oir oaaal
oial oieer seiaid qein said saio deed tial oiid
qay kair qeier qiiil chiiiiy ter cheean chaen oiel
say qaao oaeo kied oan saay sein oaio chad taear
chaaid qiel oan oeer chaio seao oeiy chian cheeel
oaair teeo seil oer tiy keeeo chieo oial oear ter
said oeel chiiied oeen oaen oiiil deear kaio char
kaieo seiy cheal seeid keay sar seiaiy chian chied
oeean sey teal oead kiiied oeen oir oeaiio oaaao
cheal chin oaen sear tiair tead oaaed tio oaio
daey oeo oeaiil oeeed oiay oieel kiiy deey oiaen
chier kiier qaao oieo chiel say keeeo chaal oier
kieel qeio oan oeo siiin tead oeid daaal oaain
siaiy qeao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oail chey
keeao seeiid qaid sen cheed teao cheil chaid qiad
chied oiy qiir cheeeo chiiaad qaen keeer chiid kieo
oial oead taed oaid say qier chil qir qieo cheel
cheeay keeeo chiiaad qaen qeir kieo daio chead qay
kir cheal siad oiiar oiir chieed oein qier tao
cheal cheeeo chieed chear ter oiaed daear cheer oeaao
chieey ter chiiil cheil cheaar oeiey tair qeier qieo
cheel oiaid chien oaiil qaay oiiir chaer qiiio
saead deien oaiiin cheao chaaio qaeil sen cher oao
chieo cheey deeeo oein tiair taio oiil daead oain
chaio cheeeo oaeo kiao qeean seir taied cheil cheaad
chey qail oiieo oiaid oed tey keair tiar taieo
oean oiel chaey oaiil tin oiiy oaen chieo seaiil
tein taaio seo chieo oean chir saao chiiiio seaar
cheiey siir chaaeey oaen sieo cheel oiair

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oail chey
qaad qaio dael sied keid dead chey kaid diien
oaear kiel cheeao cheier oeer diiin tead said tial
oead qeaan kiao taaal tiaeir ter chaaad cheil oeay
oiir der chaied oaaaid siin seied tian kaear
qaaain oiaiy teeo seio taead sir qar chio seao
saay cheiey qaao oaeo qiaid oer keao saal cheier
oiel chaal oail oiey cher sey taio chad keeil chel
ken saeid cheier sar siead chiiied oeen oaen sieo
saaar qaid saiar qar oaiy chiy qeao oaieel tear
siaiy kied chaey sail saio cheao chaen qiiid cheao
cheeay tao cheal siil dead saaiy chieeeo chiier kir
oair qaair tair sieo oead taed chiiieeain saiiy
siiir oaad qaio dael sied keid dead chey qar oiel
chiael cheear siiir oiiio oaaeed tein keiey tair
qaay chieo seaiil kier cher seay sein chiaeid tieo
oiier cheeo oiid kaey sar cheeo oiid kaey saay
sein sal qied saaiy kaaed tial oaio dain sieo
oead taed chiiieeain oaiiin chir oiel oiaey chian
oair kay qeaaey chair chieo seaiil kier oear oao
cheao diaid tein kir oeaiio oaaao cheal chin oaen
cheaad chaen kil kead oiiey qeir qey kaeir kir
chead seao cheil chear oaen chiean oeein kaio chieaal
qair chaar oeeaer qieo dair kied chael siar chaaed
keer daio siieo kiael oan seeid qiir diel oan
chail oeiy seal oiy tael oiiien chiio oaead kian
tail oeiy seaal kan oeer oeay oay qieo cheel
oiaid chien oiiio oaaeed tein tiar qaio cheao oied
tey qar siiio oaaeed tein qiiir oaad qaio dael
sied keid dead chey qeaao chieey kieer oier oir
chiey oieed oein qey taio sied qiiil ken oiel
cheeao cheier oaed tian qaio cheao oied tey qar
oaer qeaaed daead oaaaao chiaid tein kir oiien
oaay sail oiieo oiair oaeo taio cheier cheeo saio
oaiy oiel oiael say kair qaair qiiil sier kir
oeeed chier oeeer chiid kieo oial oead taed oaid
say keiey tied oeean cher cheean chaen oear qey
kaeir kir oead qaaaiy sein oieed oein qier kiiy
chiel oan chail oeiy chiiil diaiy kair qial said
qiied char qaid oaio cheao oiair chaeaay qieo chiiir
qiel chaey oaiil tin oir chiey chian saeay seiaed
tiay chaar oiead daear oaen seial dead sen oeeer
daaio seo chiiad qen oieey oeeer chiid kieo oial
oead taed oaid seaan tieo dio saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaiil tail oeiy seal oiy kao
seial chiiio oaiid oeao deeead chaan siear teeo
sieo ched tead chaio oaaaid siin chieen kied chaey
seied qiien kiiien daar oeeen keo chead sial oeir
oaaao cheiy cheal saaio daead sid kiy teey siey
diair tead cheaed ker kaiiin oaiid dian qiiir
diaed qay keil oiiieo dan saar cheao oiiir kaiio
seaay tair oaean kaiid chieed char tied qeio qay
kir oaaan qaiid dian qiiir deao oieo dair qar
seaao cheao chaeael chin siiir oeeeed daead sir qar
saay oeaio deaao chan seir qey kaeir kiiy chaieo
deeeo deeeaaien oael seio chaal oiaiid oer kir
siiir oeeeed daead sir qial oaeo siil cheieed char
ter chiiin kieed oein keeer chied oieid qir qail
oiieo oiaid oer kir oeaiid dar qiey diiiid cheied
char qaiao tear chil qeiar qar seeel dael saiir
chier qiiir oail oiieo oiaid oer kiiy cheer chaer
kaen qeeeed daead sir teeo seaiil tein taaid
qiel oan sar cheeo sid qail oaid tin oaiiin oaiid
dian kaiio seaay tair oay teeo saaad qaad kiir
ter chaied oaaaid siio oen saiid deear tier kaead
tey qiead diieo chey qar seaaay cheael qaen tiaeiy
teey cheear tiiiid cheied char kaio chad keal chin
chaaid qiel oan sar seaao chead kian taaar deal
saaio daead sid kiy kaien siian kaaaad oiaid oer
kir oaaan qaiid dian qiiid oiiid daey sail oiieo
oiaid oed kear ter chaieeo chiiio daiy oaear qaeo
taed oair chaeay qey tiiad taar oieo oead taed
oaiir oieey cheear tiiiid cheied char taeay tier
kiid taal oien oir seio kien deaad chiier qiiir
oail oiieo oiaid oed kear tieeo oaiiy teeel
oaair kied chaey oeer diaeay saiid dian kiiy
chaieo deeeo chieed oen cheaeo kier oay tiaed qay
kir oiien oaay oeaio chiiio chiiieeain oeid oeiey
taeay qey teey oiio saad qeiy chaal oiaiir oiio
saad qeiy chied kiier kiel cheeao cheied chad qay
kiier kaaaad oiaid oer kaaal oean chir oiel chied
keier seaaan deaad chaen tair oaean kiio cheeed
teed qaar kaiio seaal teear qaid chaien oar chieo
saeil chan chier kaear keaid oeeao say kiier qier
oeaid oaiio sio siien kaiid chieed char tieeen der
cheean chiiy chaieo deeeo chieer sead kaen taaen
qeeeed daead sir qeiaey keer oaaal chead kan
seier teeean daeay kieo oial oieer sar cheian
sein oieed oein tieo oial seil chieo oien oir
saein oaear kaeo oeo saeiy oeiey tead siay oaio
chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oail chey
kaid deaar oaead keean qeiair kied chael oaiin
qeiair keer oaid oaio daal oair oaien siian
kaaer taaar chaio oaid daear oir seo oaio oiiy
oaar deeel oaair kear teey chiael chied chin oir
cheal siad oiin cheey chaied cheil cheal saiy oair
taaar daaao seeiid kian qar chiao qain dian chair
qaay sein oiaiy seain cheeo oaeier kir oaaan
qaiid dian keiey kair tail oaen seiaid kiy ter
chaaad cheil oeal chin oaid tian qier seaad oaiid
oer kir sier siiin ter sail oeel deal saiy oaen
oieil tein qeain sar seeen teaad chaen kaen
qeiair qear teear taied cheil chaear oir saeid
cheier oaen qeaao chieey qead chaain chaaen qied
keid oaiy oir seil chieo dio oead kieo oiiil
chiien saaao chaen tair sail oeel chao chad qay taar
oein chiiaay chiiil oaeid daaiy qiir chaaeen oair
kaid diien chiy kaiio oeel deaad sair qar seeiin
cheaad chaen kaen keeao siad daaiy tiaear ter
oiaen saar chiel oiiieo oiel oan cheaan kaen tieo
sid kiy qiir chiid kieo oien oiier qeao oed kear
qaial kian keeao saiil tieo oaid say tiar keil
chiiiiy tead oeel deed tial sen cheear tiaid kaen
qeiair qeao oeo oiiied seiey

peaal kian tail oeiy seal oiy qaid oeiy chiaid
ker keao chiiad kaeil dail sen siir chaaan qeao
oaiil kiiey seio chaaad cheear saead chied oied
qaaiy teal siad oiin saiao taair sey kaeir kir
oeeed oiied oen cheaeo kier oay qeeal oair oaeio
taieo chian oaaal oial oieer oear ter cher oiaeo
sin cheaan qeao oeo oiiied seiey

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oail chey
qeier keo oaaeed kier seir taied chieed oiy qail
said teio tey keer diiin tiair tiael chied chieo
chied oial oeier saiil qaay chir chead seao cheil
chear oiiy oeaid oied kear kaio chad tair teao
cheil saaaiiean sar siiid dael chaiy siiir oiead
dael siair chaio cheeer qaial kian keer dead oeel
deed qiiil chiieo sen chian chao chaair chaio cheeer
kiio cheao deay oied chiao saio deer keay chied
chiao seaiid deeeo oiey sio sail chair teio taid
qien oir chead sial saaaio dar kiio sead taio char
kiaed cheao oiair cher seay siaiy teao cheil cheaar
oaear tao chiiaad tear seeay daaan kir oaeo siiid
daair chaeay ted oed tey

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy tiair
kieo oeeao oao char taeal teed qaar taar oeeen
qeier teao chaaio chio sir qiail qaad kiir qead
dan chiaid taain kied seeid kaey oaiy chian keao
oiiir teeeo oial seil oer qeao oiaey oiao taaal
tiaeir ter sio seaaed chiiied oeen saao oaeo
qiaid oer tiy qeiar qar seeel chael siil chead
qair cheael qaen ter chiael oiial oen oir seo oaio
oiiy deey chier tian keiel kiid teer qieaao deeeo
oiey sieal chead qaiy oeiey tied chen oir saeid
dear sar cheiao oen seo chaieed oaaaid siio oiy
qaid oeiy chiao seiao qein

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey
keeen qaad keel deaar chiar kiid tiaeao oier taar
oeeen kiiieed deeeaao cthaieeay dio saio oeier
kiid tiaeao oier kaeil deeeo oeiy oeeed chain
dead cheaer ter chiaeal chan oear chiael cheieeeed
tear qaiao taair

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy qey tead
saio oin oaer qaad qaio diieiy qiir chaaan kiid
kieo oeeaey qar chieed dieen sein tieeo oaiiy kir
cheal siad oiin seio kaen qear keeer chaiio oeid
oiiey qiad oeil chaain kir chiey oaien oaear taao
oieo oaiir oieer diar kaiio seaiiy qaad tair
tiao keel deaar oaaiao keear chiy tiel say kir
sier chead siil oaaaid siio char qeay oeay oeeaeo
qiier oeo oeaiil oieer sar seeel dael saiir saar
oaiid dian tied oeean sein tead saio deaar

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy tiair
qaid oieeo oeo sid qaad keel deaar chaar oeeen
qaid chiiad qen chaio sial chao saaaiiean chaaar
oail saaad teeead chaan chieeen der oao oaid dear
siiir chied cheaao sid qier keiey tiy kir oaiiiy
saar chaeiay kiiy cheer daaar deal seeid taair
oiio chad kiao taio chaeo chiaen tieed dieen chieeo
oaiiy kaiiiy oao sieaad chiiied oeeo chey qeiaey
qial chey kiir chaeo taaay qiieo chaed cheeay kaaiar
qiir dead chad tieo daaey saar oier oaead keean
teio keid diieo sair teaar oeay seier qaad keel
deaar chiar kaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean qial said tin sieo dair qeier
keaio chiiio cheo cheeeaaien chiiil oaeid oaaiy qeao
oeeey tiey oiaed daear oaear qeao chaal deear ter
oaeo qiieo teal chin oiiy chaieo deeeo deeeaaien
seir qein kaiio qay kir chiy keil sal kiiey saad
qaid tiay oeiey tead siay oeao oael siil chead
qair oiiy oir oaeiey oieio qair sey kaeir kir
oaiiad oeeel dael saiir cheey oaio chaaay kieer
oier oir oeiey qeio kiaey chaied oeil sin oied
chaey chaaen qiiio oaio dael sier kaio chao cheeead
oaeay

qiier oal tian qeiar qar chio seao saay chaid tian
qaiiy saao chaed oier taar oiead oaiy oead kieo
oial oeied seao cheiy oaio siil del tar tieo dio
saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean qial said tin sieo dair kail chey
qieo oeaiid chil tien dieo siiil tar qiir oeio
taaio kiel oan seaaed daead sir keo chiaid tein
kir oaeil daaey sein tead cheaer ter chiaeay oair
kieio qiier sar chiao keeao qail sen oaiin ter
cheed tead kaey sar chio seao saay chiiy oaeied
kaen oieio qiier oal tiaer tiiil daiel oied tear
sar seaaed diiil cheil cheaar siiir dieo siiil
qier kaio chao cheeead oaeay

peaal kian tail oeiy seal oiy qio sial saaad
kied chien dieo siiil qier taar oeeen teied oaid
said teeay qeaao siil chaaio chear teaal tey taaar
chieed oiin saar dao chael cheeed qair chaaid tian
keaid chiel chaal dail oaen cheiey siir chaaan qeao
oaiil kiiey sar saad qaid tiay oieel kiy teey
chier kaead qir qiiil sier qaao oaeo kier cheey
chian chao chael cheeed qair chaaid qen cheeao
deeeaaien chead oaaed tin chaaid tian teal siad
oiin chier kaead keean qaad keid daid keo saaar
qaid saiar qeiaiy chian cheaeo keear sier saaaio
dar ter chieeid chaaal oial oieer chaaar chaio chaaal
tair qar chio seao saay chair qaay cheaay cheeo oeid
dair keeao cheeead oead keeil chey kiel chieo oiair
cher sio sail sieo chiiied oeen cheer dair sieo
oaaeed kaen tiao qaio diaeid qey keeer daieo
deeeo chieed oiy kear sio sial saaad kier oeeer
chead taed cheeay qier chao seied qeil chaid kiy
qiey daio sieaad oay qier oeao chiiaad tian kir
sier chao seied qeil chaid kaiin

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oail chey
qeied qean dao chiaead kaein qaid oeiy oiaed
oiiey qar oied chaey oeo oaaeed kied say kaaed
teeay tieo siiil qied qein qiir chaaan kiid
tiaeao oier qiiir oeial cheeiy oied oieir kaeiey
oieio qiier oay qeeeed dear sier siiin ter oear
chiao oeeed oiier

pio cheaal oiy qaid oeiy sieao oaaao deaad chey
tieo siiil tad kaen keer chaeo qiieo teal chin
oeil chiiad qaeao chieo oiay siiio teal chad kaen
qar seial cheeiy siiir oaid saiar qeiail sin
oieio qiier oay qeeal oair oear chaio oiil oiiey
taaan kir oeao oael siil chead qair cheael qaen
qiir cheaid chiel chaay oeier qieo cheel oiaid chien
cheo oaaed qaiid say

shael chead kan chaio chear qeal oiy qio cheaal oiy
qaid oeiy chaid teao daaiy kaio chaaal ked qein
qiir oeio taaio qeil oan seaaed daead oaaaao
chiaid tein kir oaeil daaey sein tead cheaer ter
chiaeay oair kieio qiier seier qeeeed dear sier
oaead keean teeean der oear chiao oeeed oiier
seeel chael siil chead qair chaar chiiil daid kier
chaio oiil daear oeiey tead siay chaar oaad qaio
daio oiel chaay diao keeao qaad tieen keao chaieed
del qaiir chead oead kieo oial oeier sial said
qiied siied char teeao oaaan deey chiiaay cheey
oaeo qiil chear taid qeied tian kaiiin chaio chao
cheeead oaeay

shael chead kan chaio chear qeal oiy taio sied qeaio
seao cheiy sio cheaal oiy qaid oeiy sio sial saaad
kied say qeao oeeel qid tian taar oeeen qaad
kaeieo sair qar chaaao kiel oer kaeiad kaio daair
chiael oiiay oiir dead cheaer tiaeay cheer chieio
qiier oay qeeal oair oear oeeen qar chair seio
chaaad cheear oeao oael siil chead qair oaen oeer
oaaal chead kan sein chaio oiil daear

feao dio oeean qial said tin sieo dair kail chey
qail oiieo dial oiid kieo dio oaiio sieo dair
kaer qeeid kiiey kaio chaaal ked qein qiir chaaan
tead sial oeir diiad qen oaio oaiin qiiid dael
chaiy siiir deaad cheen oiir oiied qein teed qiad
chead kaey seeen kiiieed deeeaao cthaieeay dio
oaaed kil tiiiid chiir teed qeiaio chear qail oeid
cheen chaaar der chiaeay cheer dair saair tieo chieen
kieel kiiy oaiao taair chiair kiio chaio oid qeeil
chaeo qien oaeil daael sin said oiao kaid chaey
oaen qeaao chieel kaen qar siiid dael chail sen
sein ter chiaeay oair kieio qiier seier qeeal
oair cher chiiin keeer oaeid daaiy qeir teear
teaal keel oaiy oiier keaid deeead oeaio chan
chead cheaer tiiey cheeer oaeid daaiy ter sein
tiael chieeo oen oair kaen qaad kaeieo sair
qeeaeo qiier chieaad daey saay oieio qiier oay
tiaer ter chieeey seiaey kear keeer deal cheao
cheier seeen qeier tio saio oeier kaiiay oeiey
tair qar sieaad cher kaen cheeo saio oaiy saiiy
oiel chiiad qiel oan sein sier qeao chieo dial sen
said oeiy chiaeiy ter oeal chaio oaaaid siin oied
chien cheead teear qiir oeo oaio oiiy diair kiio
chaio oid qeeil chaeo qien chead siil oaaaid siio
char taeay tier kir chao oeeeo kil qin seaaey
siaiy kear ter chaieed oir qein oaieo sir teeo
saio oaiy oaiiiy cheey saial kian qeiaey teaay
cheio taid qien saao oaeo kier cheey chian oiiy
oaiiay cheeel oaair qeeel chaey oeer diiaay cheey
chiao seian taaid qaio diaial sen cher chaid kiiiey
oaen oear kear chiaid taain qaao chiao saaiy qaeo
taed oeeao oier kir chiel sin saiil kiiieo cheier
saay sein sio cheied dead sen siied taear oir
oaaeen siiir dieo chieen keid oeiel kaen qiaiy
kaid diien oaio chao cheeead oaeay

shiaeal sen chaeaay kiiy oeaid oial oiel cheay
chiean cher chead saead oiil oeil oan saay oieio
qiier oay tiaer ter chiiin teey oaio chaio oid kiy
kaio char qead chieer saay chiaer kaiid chaiin oaiio
chair qiiid dael chail sen chian oead tiair ter
seay seier tieo chied cheo oieel qeio oaein kaier
cheil sier

shiaeay oeieeeer taaar daaao sieaal saiir oaien
oaiiad deeen oiian kied chiao teeean oar oaiy
oaear tao chiiaad tear cheaar oeir

shiiin teey chead oead taed oaiir cher seal oen
oaiio seaay qeier keo oaiiad oaid siin sar seier
keo oeeao tead kian taaar daieeo chiiio dail sen
sieal chead tiieo oiieo din oir seaan qaiiy saao
seied qaen keal siay chieel qen oeal seao dead
seio chaeaaay sar seeel chael siil chead qair cheer
oeeay oaay cheiad oiair cher sein qio seaaed
chiiied oeen oeiey kid sir qiiio oaaeed tein tian
kied sied qaio diieiy ter cheey chael siieo teal
chad kaen qaaio cheen saay oiid dieo chaen qeaao
chieey ter cheey siio sied keid oin chial oaeo siil
cheieed char teey chian chaieo deeeo chieer oeead tin
oeil chiiiiy teey siieo chaed cheeay kaiiin daar
oieaao deeeo oiel oan chieed deed qail oiel chaain
kir chian kiid kieo oeeaey kied chaey seaao seao
oiial oiy ter chiiaan cheey chier taiy chaiio oeel
deaad sair qial oaaaay qiel chaey oaiil tin chair
qied teeeal say taar oeeiin saay oeeo oead taed
chen oeid oeiey taeay qead chaain oeer oaaal chead
qair oaear kaeil oaid sair oaeo chiaeo tian

shiiin qiey chiaid oeil oer tiar qiian kied seeid
kaey sar chieo saeiy cheer oeeid qiir chead kiael
chieo oiay oaaan kaear tao chieeid oaaaid siin sar
seier keo oeeao tead kian teiad oiair cher chaieeo
chiiio dail sen sein qio seaaed chiiied oeen chaiel
oied kaaio saaio daear oaeil daaey cheey oiaed
daear oied oieir kir chiey cheey chaid qeier tiean
oear chaio oiil oiiey qieo seil chaal oied tear
seeen tieen qar siaiiiio dieen siey oaiao taair
sey kaeir qeo oaio oiiy diair tieeiy chier kaear
keeel seeeey qar sieaad cher kiiy oeid chaey chaaen
qiiio oaio dael sier kiiy oaen chiao keear chair
qaiil tied qein tiiin teey oaieed dear sar
seeiio oen chair taaar oied kead qaaaiy sial chair
qein oaio chad taear chaaid keier oeer diaeay chied
oeean cheey oaieed dear oied chien chiiio deer
qeeiio oen chied oeean chiair taid qiad deay oied
chael cheeean chaio chieaal qaid sir keair qiel
chiael cheiy chiiaan chaeay qead chaain chair chaaen
teeo chil keier cheaad cheen cheeo oiid kaey cheaad
cheen chail oeiy chian saeay oied chieo oaiil tieo
oieo cheay sial chair qaay chiao qiiiy qeeel chiaed
oiiey qeeiiy oeer daieeo chiiio dail sen cheey
said oeiy chieeo chieo daael say tied oeean chiair
tieeo chaal oaiid oied qaen kaiid dan oeal siay
saiir chaer qieaao deeeo deay saay oiel chaed daan
oied oieir kir seo chiael oiial oen cheaeo keear
sier oeao saal daaiy keeen qieo seil chaal oied
tead chin oead tiair qiir deeel oied qen chian
oeao saal cheier saay seeiin sear tiair tiiel
dear oir oieel qead tain saay oied chiao saio
deer qiiid dael chail sen cheey oear oeein tiiiid
chier sial oaaaay kaen chiao qiiiy keeao siad
daaiy tiiaay siir chaaan kaear qeao chiiad qeil
sin chio saio oeier kiid tiaeao oier teal siad
oiin chead cheaer kear chaio oiil daead sir qaiil
qaay chaiio oeieeel sin oieio qiier seier keo
oeeao tead kian qar seier tieo sieo ched tey
qeiaiy oear sey kaeir teaeo keear oaaan kead
tiaid qin seaaey sar saay siaiy qad char qiir
deeel oied tear sieo cheel oiair

shiiin tiair taio oiil oiiey taaan kir chian tao
oaiid chieen oaer taaen qaid siio seeid kaey
cheaad cheen chail oaen cheiey said oaio oeaal kian
qeaaan oeeen teied oaid said teeay teiey said
oaio dael oil teeay oir oaeil daaey sein tead
cheaer tiiey dier keiey tiy keaid chiel chaay deiey
said oeel chiiied oeeo sir keer diiin kaio chieel
teeay chair keay chiel oieo din oir oead tiaid
tian oaiiay chaar chiiil daid tin oiiy chao oiaid
chied chin oir chiey chian cheael qaen kaien sier
oeao saal cheied siien taio oeail daid kier sail
oead kien oiaiy kaid diien chead siil oaaaid siio
char ter chaio oiil daear oaaal dial oeel daid
kied oan

feeer oaeo keeel kaeiar tiar qiian kied seeid
kaey chaaar daaad cheil oeal chin oir oeaid deeead
chaan saar chied oieir kaaan kaear kead tiaid tien
oaiil qaay chir seaiieo oeao oiaiiir seaan kaaaad
oiaid oer kir chiey cheey oaien chaar oiead oail
oeey diair taio oiil oair oaeiao sin

feeer oeao sead oen seir keein tead oaaed tiay
siiir dieed dieen chieeo oaiiy keeed oial siel
sin sar oail chan chaid qeier tiean oear chaio oiil
daead oeean qieo chaiel oied tear chair oiiio
diieo oead tiiiiy ter oear chieeid cheied seeeeo
oaead kian qial oaaaay qiel chaey oaiil tin chair
qied teeeal say taar oieaao oeieiy oeeer oeao
sead oen said oeiy chaaao cheied chiiied oeeo seao
say kaaeeo chan chier kaear kaiio oeel deaad sair
qar seeel dael saiir chaeay qier qiiir cheo oeel
deal sail chey

shiiin tiair kiaed chao saail sin siiio saead
deien chiar qiian tail chier oeair keil chaer oein
oiio sead keal seio oan sar siead chiiied oeeo
chan chead cheaer tiiin qein qio chieed deaan chiel
sin cheiey said oaeo sial saaad kier chael oear
teey chiiil cheaid deeead chaao oan seaaan cheer
diiin tiair taid qiad chied oeeo chel ken saeid
cheier sar seial cheeil chan chead cheaer kiao
tieaaio sieo cheao daio cheieeer oaiin tiaeay
oeiey tair qaay sieo sid taael say qaeid cheier
chio seao saay died oair chaeay qier keaid deeead
chaao sin oieiy qar sieo chaiel oied tear sied
kaear qier seio kieo oeiaed kay qeeen qeier
qaiil tieo oiair cher chiiaao sin siaiy kaid diien
oir chiey oiaed oiiey teaar oeir qaid oeil say
taier keal seial chiiin

shiiin tiair tael oil teeal chey keeo oaeo oeo
sair qar chaaan keiey tiy qier oaead qir qaiil
qaay cheeed chier sead chaain chaaar deaal tel qiair
siear kaid diien oiiy oeeeo chieo oial oear ter
cheeao dan oeeer oaeid daaiy taeay tier kaeiay
qaao oaeo kier sier qiiir chiel oieo oiair chael
oear qaar oeio keeey keao oiiir teael qaen qiieo
chaed cheeay tao chied qiey siir chaaao qeil sin
chieo sir kaid deaar oir oaiiiy chian oied seeid
kan oeead kaen qeeen qeier qied kaear tao chieeid
oaaaid siin oaen qieaao deeeo oiel oan said
saiar qar cheio taid teeead chaan sio sail oeead
chaan sein kiaed oiiey teaar chear chaio oead qaid
oain chaeo teao chaeiar kaaiy died oair chaeay qey
ter chiiin tiair taio oiil oiiey taaan qeo oaio
oiiy daio oiil oair oeao sieal chead qair oeiey
tiy kir chead sial saaaid siio char qeay seier keo
oeel deal sail chieen qiiir deian oied chaey chaaen
teiad cheeay

shiiin teey oaeir qaeid cheier sar seeiio oen oair
taaar deaal teey oar seaaay oaen seiaey kear
keeer deal cheao cheier saay sein oaied chier cheiey
oiio chad qiid daaiy qiied keay seeen qaay chieo
sid qier qaio dael saiir cher chiiaao sin oaid
diien chiy taid qied teeeay chieeey oeeo oaaed tin
chaaid tian taar oaad qaio daio oiel chaay oein
chiaeiy tael oiiien cheal siay chieel qen cheaal
tieen kaear qaeid cheier oied chaey seied qiien
taaal oaio diieo sir qeier teao cheeel siiio said
tein kir chao said cheeel oiil chaeier keaid deeead
chaan chead cheaer taeay tier kir oaaan kaear keeeo
chiao seo oeen oaer qeied qiien taaao cheied chiain
sar oeo chaaid tear sein chier kaead qir qiiil
chiiiiy tiiey dian oeiey tair qar oail chan chaid
qeier tiean oear chaio oiil daead oeean qieo seil
chaal oied tear oeer daied deal siiy chaear tieel
qaeid cheier

sheey cheaal keel oail said tein kiiy oeaiio oaaao
cheal chin seio kiaey cheear kiao kil tain sar
cheiao oen oeeeo chaal oier keeeo oiaid oaean chieo
dio saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean tail oeiy oaeil kan chiao keeao
qaad tieen kiel chiair kieer oeio taed oair chaaao
cheied chiain oear tiiaay oeeer cheaid deeead chaan
said oeiy chead cheaed qeaan qail oeel chiiied oeeo
chey qeeen qeier qaiil qaay diar qiian keeeo
oiaid oaean chiao oeeed oiier sar oeo seaad daio
deeeaaien seir keein tead oaaed tiay oiiy seo
oaio oiiy daeay ted oer qeo oaio oiiy deey chaaao
cheied chiain siaiy qead kaen qar seaaay oaeiay
keiey kaid tian qier chao chieeid oaaaid siin sar
oail chan oaien sier oeao oael siil chead qair
chaar oiead oaiy oaear keeeo chaal oied qeao chiao
oieer

feao dio oeean qial said tin sieo dair kail chey
kay keeo saio deaar chiar kaeil deeeo dan siir
oeio taaio kiel oan seier qail saeo taal chaiio
teeay qeial chiiio chen oir cheaeo kier said cheeao
cheil oael chin chian kaiio seaay tiaeo teeead chaan
chier kaid qeier tieed deaad sir qeaaay oair
teiey oiio chad tiaeiy qio cheio keid dead chan
chieeo oaiil qein kear chiaid taain qaeo taed
oeeao oier kaaiy chair kaid diien oaear taieo
deeeo chieer chio seao saal chier chieo oaaed kaeay
qeial cheeiy oair qeaao chieel kaen qeeay oiaiy
kay kied chaal deeead chaao oer kir oaear keil sal
kiiey oeo chieer chiaeal chan chier qiiid oean chaiio
oeel deaad sair kaiy oair qiiio oeieeey cheey oer
kaear taao oieo oaiir chiaeid tieo oiier seaaan
oeeen qieo oead taed oaiir oaeo qiil chieer sar
seier qeeaeo qiier cheaar oeir qeay seaaan cheo
oeaiil oieer chair oied chael cheeead chaao oer kir
oaaal oial oieer chaaar daeay qier qier seio taal
oaiir oiiied oeeo sid teear tael seio oao seao
oaaaid siin oaen qeaao chieey qiir chaaeey oaen
sieo cheel oiair

shael chead kan chaio oir kail chey qeial deaao chan
chier kiier kaaiao keear chaar diao keeao tial sen
chiael chieen said oiao kaid chael chin chian kaiio
seaal tian keeed oial siel oan seeaer qaiiy chied
oeean sey kiio chaio oid qeeil chaeo qien oaaan
kaear teeeaad cfhaaaiio cphieaaiy cfheo chiiad taid
qeeeed deer qaad kaeieo sair qeiaiy siey oaiao
tear oir oaaal oial oieer sar chaaar diieio taair
sier tian kaeil oaiin chieo dio saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy teal
oear teaal tiy taar oeeen qail chair qar chieed
dieen chieeo oaiiy qier oaead keean qieao seeid
kan sieo oeel oin siiid dael chail chan chieeo
oaiiy kiiy cheer deiey said oeiy oaiid cheear
oaaiar ter said oaio chaio chad kan chio seao saay
deer dieo sir kaid deaad chan cher chieeey said
chiiad qen siied qein tiiin tiair taid qeied qean
chaiio oeel deaad sair qar oay qeaao chieey qail
oeid deaad sair qeial cheeil chan chieeo oaiiy
kaaer keer chieed oiin oeal siay sieo dair teiey
chaeal tiieo oaeo chin cheaeo kier seiail say keil
sier qiel chaey oiir oier tieel qen chaid kiiiel
chey keer oail oaeil oiier oiir deey saar daieo
oaiio sin chial said qeao oaaao oeiey qeay seier
qeeaeo qiier sial said qiied char teeao oaaan
cheal siay chieel qen oeeer chaaiy cheiey tair kied
chaey oaen teeo seil chaid qaail say kiel sier
kaaan kaear kieel kiiy oeir kiiid qeil oiiieo
chieed oiy kear oaieel qaen qeal oen siel oeao
deeead chaan cheey oiaen chaied oaaao deaad chan
oieen teey chiiaay chiair teed qiil chieer oaer
qail chair teaay seier keo oeel deal sail chey

shael chead kan chaio chear qeal oiy qey teal chead
qair chaar diao keeao qail sen oaiid chieed char
ter cheed tead kaey siaiiiio dieeo oan saay oieio
qiier oay tiaer qeay seier qaaid kaeay tio saio
deeay chiar qeaao tey kiiio deer qiead chiiio
oiaiiiio dieeo chien oail saead oiaid oer kir
seial qaen tiaid qin cheaay saiil kiiieo cheier
seeaer qaiiy oiiy seiar qeeen qeier qied kaead
tan sio chaaao qeio tian oaiiay oir chaaio seo
chaed oaiid say tead siil oaaaid siio char qeay
saair qail saead oiaid oer qaio oio siil diair
tail oeiy seaal kan seeiin seier qail oiieo
oiair sead chaain oaen qieo sid kaeay tio saio
deeay seil chiao seo chiael cheead chan oear oeein
tieeiy cher sail oead kien chied chaal deeead chaao
oer keiey tied oeean sein kaeid tey qeeay chaid
dear oaaed keer chieel oiao kead tain cheeo seio
qaid sen oeal siay cheer oieo saaar kaid diien
chiy qeiar qeeiio oiey chael oear keal siay sieo
dair qeeeo qaad qir qiead oaiy chaar chaiy chiy
kiied tiaiy qeo oieo oaaiy oieey cheear kiio chiao
keied char tiiaay cheey oaieo sir tiael tein qaiiy
saiil saeo qied kean chiieir teaad cheen sein
chiaeid qied keao oan cheeo seio qaid sen sead
chaain cheaad cheen cheeo seio taead oead tiiaay
cheey oiio chiao keied char kaieed dear chair cheao
cheieey teeean der oiio saad qeiy chaio chial oeel
daid kier chieeo oaiiy qaiiy chiy qaaaid siio sied
qaio diieo chey qar siiid oair oair qeier qiad
chaaio oiy tieeey saay oieil tein kiel oiael say
kir saein oaen siaiy tiao qaeiy saiin tied oeean
sein kao cheieo chan

feao dio oeean qial said teio qen oeao oier tiel
say kiid kieo cheo chaid kied say taar oeial cheeiy
chaao oieo oaiir chaio cheeer tead cheaer tiiey
chaiin taeay tied qein tiiil daiel oied tear sein
cher seal oen oaeiad qeiy cher cheal cheiay saay
sear tiaid taain keid diieo chey

shael chead kan chaio chear qeal oiy qey kieo deeead
chaao oer kiid taal oaiir siir oeio taaio qeil
oan oay kiiieed deeeaao cthaieeay dio saio oeier
kiid tiaeao oier kaiiiy oeeer chaeiay teal siad
chieer chaaar dead cheaer tiaeal chan saiao taair
cher chieeey chieo oaaed tial chan chiaeiy tael seeid
kiain chiao keel deaar oaiio said tein kaiio
seaal tian tead siay oaead keean qeio oaein
taied oaaaid siin oiir dead cheaer kieey sey kaio
chaio chaaaao oieel kay kaid diien oaear kaiio
seaay qiead chiiied oeeo oieel qeao cheier sial
oaaaay qiel chaey oear taid kiiiel chey kaiil tin
saiiy siiir oaad kaeieo sair qeeaeo qiier oeeao
siad daaiy tiiaay siir chaaiy oiiir diao keel
deaar oeiey kaid tian tiel sin chiean oear said
oeel chiiied oeeo sir qaay sein sio sieaal oen
oaiiin dao seaar oied chaey saad qaio deaad chey
taaar chaeo qiil chieer seaaay saeay cheio taid
qien oaeiay taier tael oear qeaal kan oeer chao
chieo cheey daaar chaeo qiil chieer siaiy kay qaair
keo oaaeed teio tey teaay cheeo chiaid tein kaaiar
teey chian saiir chaed tian qiel oeao deeead chaan
siied keal sean chaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seal oiy tiair
qieal chead qaid sair qeiaeo chao oier taar oeeen
qeaaed daead oaaaao chiaid tein taiel oied tead
oiy taaad cheil oeal chin siiir oeaao chieey kao
chieo cheey oaay chieo sid qier keeeo chaal oaair
tiao qaeiy siian taar oiead chiiied oeeo chan saar
chied oieir kir cheeeaad cfhaaaiio cphieaaiy cfheo oieo
chaear teed qeiaio chear taeay qead chieer oaiy
oaear kaaiao keear oir seo oaio oiiy cheeer daiel
oied tead chear taeay qey ter chieeey seiaey qial
oaaaay kaen chiao qiiiy keeao siad daaiy tiiaay
siir chead taaid sin oear ter chiiey oeir kaieel
qaen qiear qeaaey seiein ter chieeey siaiy qead
kaen qeiaey keer oaaal chead qair oeao saal cheier
sead teear teed teao dao chaaar diey cheal chaio
chaeo chiaen qar oail chan chiaeid kaey oeer diiaay
chian oeao saal cheier

shael chead kan chaio chear qeal oiy qey tiael chieen
saad keel deaar chaar diao keeao qail sen sey
kaio seeid kael oen seo chiiad tel qeeeed deer
qeial chiiio chen chied oeean chiair tiao seian
kiier kied cheio tied diay oir seaad oeaan oaen
qeaao chieey tieeey sear kiaiy saao oed kaeiay
kiiy sir kieil tein taaio oeil oied qen chieeen
diiin qio sieaal siiir chiiied oeeo cheey oeeaeo
qiier chaaar dieeey sial oaeied tey qial chair
qaad tair qeio oaein qeaal kian keer chao chieo
cheey chaen qeial chiiio chen oeiey tiy kiiy oiier
kaaiao keear saeay oied chieo deeo chiaid tein
kaiiin chir seaiieo oaieeao chaio oieo cheao
oiaiiir oaaaad oiaid oer tead seio chaeaaay oieil
tein kir seo oaio oiiy oaar cheiey tair qieaad
ched kieer oieio qiier oay tiaer taaao sied qaeao
say kiel chaal oiaid oer qeo oaio oiiy daeay qier
keaid chiel chaay dail oaen cheiey siir oeo oaio
oiiy dieeiy chaeay qier

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oail chey kay
teiad oial seio qin saad keel deaar chiar qaao
oiaio oiy qiir oeio taaio kiel oan oeo oeil
oiiieo dan seir kaio seao chied cheeay keair tiar
keaid oial oieed oiy qiear kaer qeied tar tieeo
oieo oeieil oan seiail sin oieio qiier oay qeeal
oair cheey chian saeid cheil oaen keead tail sen
chaied deal siiy chaaiar kiaiy oaaial keel dain
oeer deaal tieen qaead chied oied qaaiy kair
qeied tar taaal oaaaay kiiy cheer cheo oeil oiiid
tear oeead kaen qar said chiiad qen sail oeid
cheen oaen qied kaear kir oied oieir kir seo oaio
oiiy died qein taeal qey qiir deeel oied tear
cheiad oial saair qeeael qiil chean seal oen seain
chair sieo cheel oiaid chaain kaaal cheail daid kier
cheey chian seio keao din chead seao cheel oeied
qaen tieeen oio oaiir oaeil oaid oan seier qieo
cheel oiair sail oead kieo oiay oeiey tied oeean
oear oaeo teao chaeiar kir sier oeao sieal chead
qair chaar chiiil daid qiair chian oeao saal cheier
seao oeo oiiied seiey

feao daaen qial said tin sieo dair kay qeaao
siaiiiid teal seeao chiao seian taar chail sen
said oeil say qeo chaed oiiil chiien chiar qeo oieo
oaaiy chiair taio sied keid deaad sid kiy tael
oiy qeio kaen tiaed qay kaaed keer deey chian
chaao saiad tar tier qiian keao chaid qiad chieed
chad qay taaio saaiy kiid taed oien oir oaio
daead seaad chen oiier tead sial oieeo seaao oiy
tieeen oieao oaaao daear oeil seaan kiao qiieo
kael chio oiiey cheeo oeid diaeir teear qio oied
chael saiio chieeo say kaaiar qio seeiid kiao kiy
taeay kiio saad qeiy oaeil kan char tiaid ker
qeao sied keier oeaio cheao oiaid oer kaaiar qein
teear kaeir taar dail oeiy saar chead qaaaiy
siead oaiy chaar oail saeil chan chier kaead tey
qiad chaaio oiy taeay kaeo teiay seeen qeier
qeial deal siiil chaal chiy ter oiaed oair chair
taaar oail saio daear oir oied oieir qaao chaed
oeiaer qiaiy kied chaey sar oaiy chiy taaio oaaaad
oail oiey siear qeier kied chiao chil qeil oier
keil saaain oiel chaey oaiil teio qen said oeiy
chiiaan cheey chiael cheeel siil dey teaay oay qaid
oieo oail oiey cheal cheao cheil oaen kaid diien
oiiy chaaio oaaaad oail oiey seal oiel chaay chail
chey tair oaiil tein qeao oiaio siien keeen tair
oied chaal deeead chaao oer kir chao oeeel teay
chair sieo cheel cheeay keao oael siil chead qair
oeiey qeio kiaey seay saay seial deal siiil chaal
chiy ter oeeer oaao chaed oeiaer qiead chiiio chen
oir seo oaio oiiy oaar chair kaeo tian kied oieir
qeo chaed oiiil chiieo chey qial chair tail oeiy
sier kead qaaail oiy qeaal kan seeen qeier
qeeeed daead sir qar sail oaeo chio oaeil oiier
cheaar chear oeal sein chaio sied keid deaad chey
tio oeid daaal chey qail oeid cheein ter chaaad
dear oaeo tian kied oieir keid chael siien taeal
tair kaid diien chaar oiead diieo sen oer kir
cheeel oied qeo oiio seio oan chair saad qaid tein
kir chiar qeaad cheo cheeeaaieo oer tiey chiair
kiaed daey chio saio deeay oir chiey chiair kiaed
cheao chier cheal saaio daear saao oael char qein
kao siad cheeel siiio chey qaay sein oiio saad
qeiy chiair taio oiil oiiey tieo sir tiean cheal
cheao chieer seeel dien cheair qaeo qied kean
chiaeay sieo cheao oiaiiir oaaaad oiaid oer teaeo
kier oay tiaer ter oael seio oao seial daair
sein kao siad cheeel seean oieer oier saao cheed
tiay chian qaeid cheier sar siiid oair oair qear
tiair taed oeeao say keil sier qir qeeaer kaaial
keel dain siead daear chaar dio seao saay chair
tieo sir kaid deaar oir chiey chiair tiiiid chead
kier kair kaen qieo oead taed oaiid say qeo chaed
oail oeeeo oaio oiy kaeo qiieo kaey saair qeaaao
cheeay kaaan kaear tao oaeo taio daaiy kiaed
daear oir chiey chiair tiiiid chead kier keiey
keeeo chaal oier kiiy seo chaieey sead chaain sar
oied chaey seeeed oay kiid taal oieo cheay seeen
tieaaio seaiien oiao qiieo kao saail oer qaao
chaed oeiaer qar chio seao saay oiiir oear qeio
kaen tio oeid daaal oaaio oiy qaid oeiy sein
tiiiid oid tead qey qeay seao cheied cheio keead
chey taaar oio chiao qeaio chear qio oaead kael
sied tey qeao cheied cheied daair chaio seeiid qein
tiiey deey chiiiid oid tear qaaal chiier tead siay
chiey chieo oiaid chael siar oaao seied qaen kiao
qiad chied char tier tian qeo chaed oiiil chiien
oiiy siiir deeo oeid diaeid kiao kay tiao qieo
chan saair kiel oeao deeead chaao say keaiio oaaao
cheay seeid qiir diy qaeo taed oair oieey cheear
tead cheaer kao siad cheeel seean chiiad taar dair
oaen keo said tial oiiel oiy teal saaio daear
siel chieo deeead chaao sin oiiil deear qaid oeiy
chael oear keal siay oaen oaid diien chian qeo
oiain oaiil tin oaaal chaeay qial chair kiir cheal
sein chiael chiel chaaal oeel oal tan cher cheear
kiio siad chaeiar kaaal oeio oaein taied diaiy
taar oaiil tieo oaid say keeed oiay oiiy oaid
tiao qir kaid diien saiir dair siiio saeo qaaaio
chaaiay qiiir deeo oeid diaeir kieed oaiin

shael chead kan chaio chear taied diaiy kaear kead
kieo oaail oer taar chail sen chiair kao cheao
chiiey oaer qaad qaid tein kir chiar qaaaio dar
taeay qead chaain oail sen chiair taid teao chiiel
char teear tied qeio qay qeao oaieel qaen tieeen
der chaaao seeiid kian qiaiy kay tieed oin chail
oeiy seaal kan oaiy chied oair chiair kaeo qeio
oan oaer taaen tio saio deeay oeiey tiy kaaan
qiel chiael oaaao ched kiieo deeen oaao oiiid
qiiay seied qean cheao chaieed dey taaar oied
keieed chad qay kead kieo oiair cher oeeer oiel
chaid taio cheier oeaio chiiio oaiid sin oaiy seao
chiiad tiaiy kaead qir keo oiel sier qaeo qied
qaaaid siin cheao dao chaal chaear kieey oeal siay
oaear keao saal dear chiao kaeo chin chiel saiy
saao cheed tiay chian kead kieo oaail oer kaaal
oiiio oaaeed tin oeid daaal oeeeo oier kaaiy
oaao cheed tiay oiiy seio tiao sen chaaao siil
chear keeer oeaan tead siay oeieeeer qeier qail
saeil oeel chiain cheian siaiiiir tier tian kiid
taal oiaiid oan

peir teear tiiil oaaao deaad saeo keao chier oeao
dio oeean qial said tin sieo dair kail chey keo
oeil oiiid tead sean chaaal deeeo dao oiao taio
dear oeer chaeo qiieo teal chin chiy teeeaad
cfhaaaiio cphieaaiy cfheo chiiad tel qeeeed deer taaao
kiel oer kaeil deeeo oeiy oeeer chied saaio chiiio
dian kir oaeiel oen cheaeo keear sier oaaal deeeo
chieed cheio qieo oair oaaan kaear kaiio oeel
deaad said tein tiar kaead keean teied oaid said
teeay qar saiil qeaao qin saay chaaar oio sial
saaad kier siied keal chin sier chian qiiio teal
chad kaen qeeen kiiieed deeeaao cthaieeay dio oaaed
kil tiiiid chiir kaio chieel teeay oaaiy chaen
siaiy kay qar oaid dear oeao saal dear sieo
chaiel oied tear seaaay oaen oiid dieo chaen qial
oaaaay teer dail sieo chiiied oeen chieel qaen
qiied qein taieed chiid qied teeean oiaiy qad chio
sir qar cheian said chiiad qeo chien chied chaey seio
oaein tieo dio saaaid chiaiy

shael chead kan chaio chear qeal oiy taid qiad cheied
seao cheiy chiiil oaaao deaad saeo keao chier siir
oeio taaio kiel oan seier kiiieed deeeaao
cthaieeay dio oaaed kil tiiiid chiir keeeo qail sir
qeiail sin oieio qair oear cheear tiiil oaaao
deaad saeo kiiio chier chiiil daiel oied tear
oieil tein kir cheeeo oeeao chaid taio dair tio
seao saay cheiey tair qial oaaaay teer diao qiiiy
keeao siad daaiy teaao cheay oeer diiil cheil
cheaar cheer chaid diien oaear kaiio oeel deaad
said tein kir oaiiiy chead oaaed tin chaaid tian
kir saeio chan oieen tiiad taar oeao oeo oiiied
seiey

feao dio oeean qeeen qeier tieo chaid taio chaain
saay char tead oaid tail sair qeeel dael sead
kaen qeeen kaeo tian tiy qiiin kieer chaeiey sear
kear saiao tear saiir oaad qaio daio oiey sey
taio oiil daead oain diieio taair seir ter chaaad
dear oied chaey chaaen qiiio oaio dael sier kir
oaiiiy saad chaio oein tier tead oiiil daal
oeiair qar seier teao cheeel siiio sair qiead
chiiied oeen oied oieir kir oaiio oeil oiiieo
oeil sin sear tiair kaieel qaen tieeo oaiiy
kaaaar oear taeay qier tiy kiel oieo chier oiaed
daear oiid taal chiel chaey siead oeiey kieer chiid
kieo oieo cheal siiy diy keao saal oen sio siil
chiel cheal oiy qeir ter chiiil oeil chaal oied tear
sar chair oiiio diieo oead tiiiiy

feao dio oeean qeeen taaao kiel oer kir saao
chieo oaail say kir oeil oied qaen qaar chaiio
seaay qial oaaaal tan oer keil chiaead qay kiiy
cheer chiid deed tial sair keead kaen qiail tiao
qaeiy oaaan teer oar siead chiiied oeeo chan chaio
cheeer tead cheaer ter sey taio oiil oair oeao
oael siil chead qair oeiey kair qar sieaao deeeo
oiey oieed oein qey qio siieo kier kaael deead
tien cheo seio keao dio sair qeeay daaar cheeeo
qail sir tair oaean teed qeio sio char taeay qead
chieer seeen qiaiy tiao qaeiy siiin ter cheed tead

oiaiy oaio chao cheeead oaeay

feao dio oeean tail oeiy oaeil kan chiao keeao
qaad tey tiaeo taio chaain oaeo tian teal siieo
taaal oaain sear tael oear teey oaeo taaay qiieo
chead cheeeay siir chaaal oiil dan saad chaio diial
oeio qin chieed deaad sir qiaiy kay qar chio seil
oeao oier kair teaay oay tio saio deeay oiiy
oaaiy oiel chiael oaaan chaeo teial chin seio kiaey
sey kaeir kir oaaal oial cheeeaaien oeal sein
oaiid chieed char taeaaaad qir tiean cher chaieed
oir qear kear seien qeiad kiy kaiid chieed char
kear saad chaio cheal seao cheieed oiy qaid oeiy
oeein kao sied qieo oiiey qaad keid daid qeeaey

tieen kiier kied oieir taaio seio keao cheeay

feao dio oeean qeeen tieen qar seiaiy saar dair

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oail chey kay
qieo cheel oiair oaiin teaal teey daaar cheal siay
chair taaen keeo saaad qiiio oaeil deeeo oeo oieo
oien cheael qaen tiiil chaeo chiaen qaad qaio daio
oiey seir teed qeiaio chear ter seir keeen keer
oail oeid deaar chian kiid tiaeao oier kied oieir

feao dio oeean kay keeo oeid oaiy chiar qaio cheao

shiiad taar chaiy chaaio oaaeed kier oiiied oeeo

shiiad taar chaiy chiy taiel oied teay oaer teeead

feao dio oeean kaiil tein qiel chaey oail chey kay
keo oiel sied teeeo oiay oair kal tar kiaid oeil
oer tao chiiaad tear oeer diiin qaid oeil oiiien
oeo oieo chaead kian taaan kir oaear qeo oieo
chaead kian tieed deaad sir qar siiid dael chaiy
siiir cheo oiel sal teeeo oiay oir oaead qir keeo

chiiin chiiieen cthir oeil say kaen chiao qiiiy keeao
siad daaiy tieeen oiir cheeed oiier chaaar deaal

feao dio oeean kay tial oaeo siil cheieed seeeen

feao dio oeean qeeen qial oaaed teien siiio teal
char ter sio oaid chael char keeer chaiio seaay qar
seeen keeao said oied qaaiy kiid tiaeao oier

oeeo sir keer cheal siay oiiy oaaal dio chair

qay keiel qeio kiaey sail oeid oaaaiiean oaen

feao dio oeean qeeen kay qeeel daaao siil chaaiay
qeo chaed oail oeeel siiio chey tair seeaeo qiier
seiaiy chaeay qaid oeiy oaieeao chaio cheieeer sio
oaiir oir cheeeo oeeao chaid qeeeey qeiaiy cheey
saial kian tair cheao cheieey tio cheeel dio sair
qiiid oair siiir oeeaeo qiier chair chaaen tio
oeid deal sail oeeeo say kair kay qial oaeied

cheied chiain oeer oaar cheao oiiir kaiiin daaio
oeid oiiey keer chiaeo sin cheaan kear taio oead
qaid chey kiel chaal deeead chaao say kir saein
oeiey tied oeean chead oead qead cheier oaiil tail
oeiy chaaad cheiy oiaed oiiey taaar oieao diaid

feao dio oeean qeeen qeeaeo qiier sar cheao oieo
dair qeiaed qeao cheear oir chaao oiao qiad chiel
cheal siiin siiir deeo saio oaiy saao oaeo qiaid
oer tiy qeiar qar chio seao saay deeeo oeeao chaid
qeeeey qiaiy qear ter oao chieo oeaiiy oair qeier
qiead diieo oead qier kir oeeed chier chier kaear

qaid chiao chao oier qaiir oaay chieed oio
oiiieeaio oear kaeo taio oen chier tiao qein

oeid dead sen chaeay kaeo teiay seaao seao cheel

shiair taio oiil daead oain oio cheaal oiy kieo
deeead saaaid oeao oeaaer daaao chieaal keao dean
chiel oeao oeial chao sied qien deed qiil cheieed
chean chaio chan cheio tied dean chair teao
cheieeeeiel chaal dean chiid taal oiaiid cheaao chao
chaeaal qain dieo din saeo taed dio sain dieo
dieaaio oiel oed keieel qean oaio daeaaaaeal
siil oaio deer qiiio oeid daid tio oieeo oeo
oeieel qean chiel chaed chiel chieo dean chiel oaiil
kain dier qaao oieo oiiir deed qiil cheieed siar
chaio chan oeo oeel deal char tieo oeeao chaal
oiaiid chien deao oaaaid chieo oeeeed deer tial
seaal qiil chiir keao dio sain chiio chin saio
chiiied seao chaaaad oaar tao chaal oail sir qiieo
tied kaio chan chieo sid tial oieen seao oen oead
oao sean deal cheao chaeiy oaiil taer cheal oaar
qeio kieo oeeeed daid qeeid taain kiel chaed chiey
oaio siad oaaair chieo dieaaio oiel oed keieel
qean chaio chiiied seao chaaaad oaar qeain chieo sid
tial oain daaao siad chead kao chaio oeo oer kaio
chan chio oaeieo oeean qear kiiied seieil chien
oaad kaid qiil oeo cheaao chao chaeaal kain dier
kiid taed oeeil deer qail oaaeed qiil oid qaiin
chieo din oaio chaieeao cheaao teeear oiel oaiil
kain dier kiel oieo dio said qaaio deer qaad
keel daeaad saio deer qeao oen chieo dieaaio
oeieeeeiel chaal oiad cheieeeeiel saay oaaaid chaal
oaar kiid kied qaear oaiil keao oiil dan oeal
saiio cheal siil oaaid teao oeo chaer oeao oen
chiiaal oien dail oieeo sail siiil teao oaaaid
oeao oiar cheaid dial oaio chan siiio sieo ched
qien dier kiid taed oeeil deer keo sieo chiiied
siil oen oiaid oeil chieo dio saaad teeen cheeeo
oieo deeead sieo cheeel qen oiaid oeil chieo dio
oieen seao oen chieo dieaaio saaaid chaed deeead
saaaid oeao oiar oiel chieo dial oaio chan siiio
sieo ched qien chieo daiy saao oaeo teao chiir kaio
siad chead kieo oaar qaaaid oaiil qeo chied teey
deeead oeel deer keo oiel oieo oeeeed deean oaeo
teeal seaiid dean chiid taed chiil daeaad oeir
dieo din saao oiao tio oead kieo oaar qeil

seaal qean chiel chiiaad keel ched kaen sail sieo
deal sail sid qiil chiir tieo din seao chaed
deeear oeaid dial sieo chao saiin sio cheaal cheao
chiid qal qeo oien oiad chiel oao siaiil kien oeao
oeo oiiied seiey

deeal chey qar oaiy seiar teeo oaio deeal chey
kear keein keal chaio oaaaid siio saiio oiiey
taed oied qeo sey kiao qiad chier sar seeen teeo
oaio deeal chey qar seeen taaao kiel oed qein
qiieo oao siad chieed oiy kiio chiao qeo oer tiy
qeiar qar oaiy oiiy sier oeao saal daaiy tiiil
oeil chaal oied tear sar siiio oaaeed ken oair
taaar chiio seao chiiel oiy qail oeid oaaaiiean
siaiy tiao qaeiy chair qar cheiad chier chieo dio
saaaid chiaiy

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy seir kiio cheieo
chey teiad oial saaid qeao cheiy chiael chiel chaaal
oeel oay tiel say taar oiead chiiio chiey oeal
chaio chear kieo oaar qid kiel say kaaal chaeal
tian tead sial saaain dieo seaiil kier chier
qiian keeeo chaal dad teed qaar keil chiiiiy qaar
deeeo oaad kiid qay kaaal dail oeaiid deeead
chaan siied qein qiir cheeed oaid sal tan oieey
sey tiael chiel chaaal oeel oay keiey qeio kiaey
chiiin kiio cheien oir seo oaio oiiy cheeer cheao
chaid teao oiel oan cheaar oeir ter siey dieeo
chaal oail oiel say kir oeaid deeead chaan cheey
oiaed cheal chin oied oieir kir oeao oael siil
chead qair cheael qaen ter oao chieo cheey oaay sear
keeer oeao oaieel qaeo qaid sir qar siiid dael
chail sen chian oeiey qeio kiaey chaeay tier kir
chao cheal chaio oaaaid siin sein oear oaio seao
chied cheeay teiad dail sen chiiad taar oeeel dael
sead taio chin oaen chaaar oiieo oao seeid kan
siiid daeaaaaeal siil oieo diiiid chear

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy oeeer oaao oiain
seial dead sen oiaed oiiey keer oeay sear teear
kiio cheao deal oiy qey tiael chied cheiad deeay
qeeen qial said qaid oed kiieo deeen oaao oiain
saay seaaan oar chio seao saay oiiir oieo oead
kieo din oir oaiio oeel deal sail chey teaar oeir
qaid oeiy chaied oaaaid siio sein cheey oiaed chan
said saiar qar seial cheeiy chiy kieel qiad cheier
siaiy teeo seio kao char ter chiiil oeil chaal oied
tear sein saial kian qar oaiy oaen oied oieo
oaar kair kaen qiiio oeel ched kiid teer teaay
oay qeial dead seo oer qaeid oaail qein ter
chiiaan cheey chier kaear keeel seeeey qar chad kael
chead qin siaiy tiao qaeiy oir oaaan tiy tao cheal
chaio deeeaaien cher sail oeid oaaaiiean oear qio
sail sieo oaaaiiean sar oeo oiel oeao deeead
chaan chier qier oeao chaieed dieen qeao chieo oaid
oiiey kiaed chan oeer oaaal chead taid tin chiy
keao saal cheied char tiael chied chio sir teiad
cheeay

feao dio oeean kaiiy tail oeiy cheed qeiaio chear
qaid oeel daio char qeal oiy qiir chaaan taaio
oied chead kiiey teed qeiaio chear ter sail oeid
oaaaiiean oaen qear qeien kiid taed oied tao
oiiir kir chiey sey keal chaio oaaaid siio sean
chiid tiaeao oier kaaiao keear saiir chaer qar
seaaao oaid sin siail tiao qaeiy cheaeo kier oay
qeeal oair chier tiy keeel seeeey qar seial chiiio
dial chan sey tael sead qaid oiiey saiil taed
oiair chaeay kiio chiao keied char tieo oean chiiy
oaen chad kael seiair qar oay qial chel qaaiy chaio
oiil oiiey taaan kir oeil chiiiiy ker keil chiiad
qen siir oeo oaio oiiy dian chied chiiieeain oiier
keeel seeeey kaid diien chiy tao oeeiy chaier qead
chaain oay qeeal oair cher chaaad cheear oiiy seaad
oaiid oer kiiy oaen oiiil daid tail sen chaio
sied keid oaid oiiey sar sied teeeay chian chair
teio taid teeead chaao cheeao oiel oan cheaay sail
chied oaiil tier kir
